end

-- PROMPT_HACKER v3.2: единый журнал ошибок
local LogService = game:GetService("LogService")
local function PH_reportError(where, err)
    warn(string.format("[Prompt_Hacker] %s\n%s\n%s", tostring(where), tostring(err), debug.traceback("", 2)))
end
local function PH_safeCall(where, callback, ...)
    local args = table.pack(...)
    local ok, result = xpcall(function() return callback(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)) end, debug.traceback)
    if not ok then PH_reportError(where, result); return false, result end
    return true, result
end
local phLogConnection
pcall(function()
    phLogConnection = LogService.MessageOut:Connect(function(message, messageType)
        if messageType == Enum.MessageType.MessageError then
            warn("[Prompt_Hacker runtime] " .. tostring(message))
        end
    end)
end)

Instance.new("UICorner", MainFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

-- Плавное появление существующего окна (размеры/цвета/расположение сохранены)
do
    local finalSize, finalPosition = MainFrame.Size, MainFrame.Position
    local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    scale.Scale = 0.94
    scale.Parent = MainFrame
    MainFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(finalSize.X.Scale, finalSize.X.Offset * 0.94,
        finalSize.Y.Scale, finalSize.Y.Offset * 0.94)
    MainFrame.Position = finalPosition + UDim2.fromOffset(0, 10)
    TweenService:Create(MainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = finalSize, Position = finalPosition, BackgroundTransparency = 0
    }):Play()
    TweenService:Create(scale, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Scale = 1}):Play()
end

local CloseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
CloseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
CloseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -31, 0, 4.5)
CloseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
CloseBtn.Text = "❌"
CloseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
CloseBtn.TextSize = 12
CloseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
CloseBtn.Parent = MainFrame
Instance.new("UICorner", CloseBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    _G.ScriptDestroyed = true
if phLocalDummy then pcall(function() phLocalDummy:Destroy() end) phLocalDummy = nil end
    if _G.PromptBuilderMusic then pcall(function() _G.PromptBuilderMusic:Destroy() end) _G.PromptBuilderMusic = nil end
    pcall(function()
        local radio = game:GetService("SoundService"):FindFirstChild("PromptPersonalRadio")
        if radio then radio:Destroy() end
    end)
    _G.ScriptPaused = false
    flying = false
    invisActive = false
    godmodeEnabled = false
    noclip = false
    zonesEnabled = false

    pcall(function()
        local char = Player.Character
        if char then
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hrp then
                hrp.Anchored = false
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                for _, obj in pairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if obj:IsA("BodyVelocity") or obj:IsA("BodyGyro") or obj:IsA("BodyPosition") then
                        obj:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                if godGroundCube then
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(godGroundCube.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                elseif ghostSimulationCube then
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(ghostSimulationCube.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                end
            end
            if hum then
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                hum.WalkSpeed = 16
                hum.JumpPower = 50
                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if animator then
                    for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                        track.Speed = 1
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        if markerA then markerA:Destroy() end
        if markerB then markerB:Destroy() end
        if zoneBoxVisual then zoneBoxVisual:Destroy() end
        if godAirPlatform then godAirPlatform:Destroy() end
        if godGroundCube then godGroundCube:Destroy() end
        if godVisualClone then godVisualClone:Destroy() end
        if ghostSimulationCube then ghostSimulationCube:Destroy() end
        if ghostVisualClone then ghostVisualClone:Destroy() end
        
        if _G.SavedZoneBoxes then
            for _, boxData in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes) do
                if boxData.Visual then boxData.Visual:Destroy() end
                if boxData.MarkerA then boxData.MarkerA:Destroy() end
                if boxData.MarkerB then boxData.MarkerB:Destroy() end
            end
            _G.SavedZoneBoxes = {}
        end
        
        if savedPoints then
            for _, pt in pairs(savedPoints) do 
                if pt.Visual then pt.Visual:Destroy() end 
            end
        end
    end)

    if ScreenGui then
        ScreenGui:Destroy()
    end
    -- Разрешаем повторный запуск только после завершения полного закрытия.
    PH_EXEC_ENV[PH_EXEC_KEY] = nil
end)

local TabScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
TabScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10)
TabScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
TabScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1

local TabListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
TabListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
TabListLayout.Parent = TabScroll

local pages = {}
local tabButtons = {}

local function createTab(name)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
    btn.Text = name
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.TextSize = 13
    btn.Parent = TabScroll
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

    local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    page.BorderSizePixel = 0
    page.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    page.Visible = false
    page.Parent = ContentArea

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    layout.Parent = page

    layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 15)
    end)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for _, p in pairs(pages) do p.Visible = false end
        for _, b in pairs(tabButtons) do
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
            b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
        end
        page.Visible = true
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 40)
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
    end)

    table.insert(pages, page)
    table.insert(tabButtons, btn)
    return page
end

local function createToggle(page, text, callback)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 35)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.Parent = page
    
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -65, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = text
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
    lbl.TextSize = 14
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.Parent = frame
    
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 55, 0, 22)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -55, 0.5, -11)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
    btn.Text = "ВЫКЛ"
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.TextSize = 11
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    
    local state = false
    local enabled = true

    local function renderState()
        btn.Text = state and "ВКЛ" or "ВЫКЛ"
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
        btn.TextTransparency = enabled and 0 or 0.45
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = enabled and 0 or 0.45
    end

    local function applyState(value, runCallback)
        state = value and true or false
        renderState()
        if runCallback and callback then
            local ok, err = pcall(callback, state)
            if not ok then warn("[Prompt_Hacker toggle] " .. tostring(text) .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
        end
    end

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if _G.ScriptPaused or not enabled then return end
        applyState(not state, true)
    end)

    -- программная смена состояния (Set без callback; Apply с callback).
    local api = {
        Set = function(value) applyState(value, false) end,
        Get = function() return state end,
        Apply = function(value)
            if not enabled and value then return false end
            applyState(value, true)
            return true
        end,
        Toggle = function()
            if _G.ScriptPaused or not enabled then return false end
            applyState(not state, true)
            return true
        end,
        SetEnabled = function(value)
            enabled = value and true or false
            btn.Active = enabled
            btn.AutoButtonColor = enabled
            renderState()
        end,
        Button = btn,
        Label = text,
    }
    _G.PH_Toggles = _G.PH_Toggles or {}
    _G.PH_ToggleOrder = _G.PH_ToggleOrder or {}
    if not _G.PH_Toggles[text] then
        table.insert(_G.PH_ToggleOrder, text)
    end
    _G.PH_Toggles[text] = api
    return api
end

local function createInput(page, text, placeholder, callback)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 35)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.Parent = page
    
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = text
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
    lbl.TextSize = 14
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
    lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    lbl.Parent = frame
    
    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 24)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 5, 0.5, -12)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    box.PlaceholderText = placeholder
    box.Text = ""
    box.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    box.TextSize = 13
    box.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
    box.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function() callback(box.Text) end)
end

local function createButton(page, text, callback)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    btn.Text = text
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.TextSize = 13
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.Parent = page
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if not _G.ScriptDestroyed then callback() end end)
end

-- Локальный тренировочный манекен: виден только этому клиенту и не наносит серверный урон.
local phLocalDummy = nil
createButton(mainPage, "🤖 Создать тренировочный манекен (локально)", function()
    if phLocalDummy and phLocalDummy.Parent then
        phLocalDummy:Destroy()
        phLocalDummy = nil
    end
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local oldArchivable = char.Archivable
    char.Archivable = true
    local ok, clone = pcall(function() return char:Clone() end)
    char.Archivable = oldArchivable
    if not ok or not clone then return end
    clone.Name = "PH_LocalTrainingDummy"
    for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("Tool") then
            d:Destroy()
        elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
            -- Манекен должен оставаться физически подвижным; HRP нельзя закреплять.
            d.Anchored = false
            d.CanCollide = false
            d.Massless = true
        end
    end
    local hum = clone:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        hum.Sit = false
        hum.AutoRotate = true
        hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.Viewer
        hum.HealthDisplayType = Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.DisplayWhenDamaged
        pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
    end
    clone.Parent = workspace
    local myRoot = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if myRoot then clone:PivotTo(myRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -7)) end
    phLocalDummy = clone
end)
createButton(mainPage, "🗑 Удалить тренировочный манекен", function()
    if phLocalDummy then pcall(function() phLocalDummy:Destroy() end) phLocalDummy = nil end
end)


-- Примечание для loadstring: серверный TrainingNPC требует серверного Script
-- и не может быть создан/управляться одним клиентским загрузчиком.
-- Остальные клиентские функции и исходный интерфейс сохранены.

local function createParagraph(page, titleText, contentText)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 55)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
    frame.Parent = page
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    
    local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
    tl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 20)
    tl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 2)
    tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tl.Text = titleText
    tl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    tl.TextSize = 13
    tl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    tl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    
    local cl = Instance.new("TextLabel", frame)
    cl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -22)
    cl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 22)
    cl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    cl.Text = contentText
    cl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    cl.TextSize = 11
    cl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
    cl.TextWrapped = true
    cl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    cl.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    return frame, cl
end

local function findTargetPlayer(str)
    if str == "" then return nil end
    for _, p in pairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= Player and (p.Name:lower():sub(1, #str) == str:lower() or p.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #str) == str:lower()) then return p end
    end
    return nil
end

local function updateMovement()
    if _G.ScriptPaused or _G.ScriptDestroyed or invisActive then return end
    local char = Player.Character or Player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue; hum.JumpPower = jumpPowerValue; hum.UseJumpPower = true end
end
Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(1) if not _G.ScriptDestroyed then updateMovement() end end)

-- АВТОНОМНЫЙ ЦИКЛ АНТИ-СТАНА (АБСОЛЮТНО СТАБИЛЕН)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if antiStunEnabled and Player.Character and not _G.ScriptDestroyed and not _G.ScriptPaused then
        local hum = Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end
            if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
            if hum.WalkSpeed == 0 then hum.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue end
        end
    end
end)
-- УСИЛЕННЫЙ КОНТРОЛЬ СКОРОСТИ И ПРЫЖКА (В КОНЕЦ ЧАСТИ 4)
local function updateMovement()
    local char = Player.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        hum.UseJumpPower = true
        hum.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue
        hum.JumpPower = jumpPowerValue
    end
end

-- Постоянное удержание заданной скорости и прыжка, чтобы игра не сбрасывала их обратно на 16/50
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if _G.ScriptDestroyed or _G.ScriptPaused then return end
    local char = Player.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        if walkSpeedValue ~= 16 and hum.WalkSpeed ~= walkSpeedValue then
            hum.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue
        end
        if jumpPowerValue ~= 50 then
            hum.UseJumpPower = true
            if hum.JumpPower ~= jumpPowerValue then
                hum.JumpPower = jumpPowerValue
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- ----- КОНЕЦ ЧАСТИ 4 -----
-- =========================================================
-- =========================================================
-- ----- НАЧАЛО ЧАСТИ 5.1 -----
-- =========================================================

local randomGreetings = {
    "Версия 2.7. Усиленный Год Мод без случайных спусков, 3D-радар и глаз скрытности готовы!",
    "На связи! Все системы v2.7 в норме, удачной катки и аккуратнее с админами.",
    "Prompt_Hacker v2.7 готов к работе. Помни: главное — не палиться на ровном месте!",
    "Системы активны! Если античит начнет чудить — жми паузу в шапке меню.",
    "Загружено без ошибок. Выбирай нужную вкладку и погнали тестить!"
}
createParagraph(mainPage, "🤖 Твой ИИ-напарник Prompt_Hacker", randomGreetings[math.random(1, #randomGreetings)])

createInput(mainPage, "Скорость бега (Дефолт: 16)", "Число...", function(Text) 
    local num = tonumber(Text)
    if num then walkSpeedValue = num; updateMovement() end 
end)

createInput(mainPage, "Высота прыжка (Дефолт: 50)", "Число...", function(Text) 
    local num = tonumber(Text)
    if num then jumpPowerValue = num; updateMovement() end 
end)

createToggle(mainPage, "⚡ Активировать Анти-Стан (Anti-Stun)", function(Value)
    antiStunEnabled = Value
    if not antiStunEnabled and Player.Character then
        local hum = Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = walkSpeedValue end
    end
end)

-- =========================================================
-- 🧍 ANTI-RAGDOLL + ЛОКАЛЬНЫЕ ВИЗУАЛЬНЫЕ ПАКИ
-- Важно: это клиентская best-effort защита. Игровой сервер может
-- принудительно включать ragdoll/разрушать суставы, а эффекты Motor6D.Transform
-- видны только на этом клиенте и не реплицируются остальным игрокам.
-- =========================================================
do
    local antiRagdollEnabled = false
    local antiRagdollConnection = nil
    local antiRagdollCharacterConnection = nil
    local savedStateFlags = {}
    local glitchEnabled = false
    local glitchConnection = nil
    local glitchPack = "Глич"
    local glitchMotors = {}
    local random = Random.new()

    local blockedStates = {
        Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll,
        Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown,
    }

    local function applyAntiRagdoll(character)
        local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        for _, state in ipairs(blockedStates) do
            if savedStateFlags[state] == nil then
                local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return hum:GetStateEnabled(state) end)
                if ok then savedStateFlags[state] = enabled end
            end
            pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(state, false) end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            if hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = false end
            if hum.Health > 0 and (hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown) then
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function restoreAntiRagdoll(character)
        local hum = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            for state, wasEnabled in pairs(savedStateFlags) do
                pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(state, wasEnabled) end)
            end
            pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = false end)
        end
        savedStateFlags = {}
    end

    local function setAntiRagdoll(value)
        antiRagdollEnabled = value
        if antiRagdollConnection then antiRagdollConnection:Disconnect(); antiRagdollConnection = nil end
        if antiRagdollCharacterConnection then antiRagdollCharacterConnection:Disconnect(); antiRagdollCharacterConnection = nil end
        if antiRagdollEnabled then
            applyAntiRagdoll(Player.Character)
            antiRagdollConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if _G.ScriptDestroyed or not antiRagdollEnabled then return end
                local char = Player.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    applyAntiRagdoll(char)
                end
            end)
            antiRagdollCharacterConnection = Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                task.wait(0.25)
                if antiRagdollEnabled then applyAntiRagdoll(char) end
            end)
        else
            restoreAntiRagdoll(Player.Character)
        end
    end

    local function clearGlitchTransforms()
        for motor, _ in pairs(glitchMotors) do
            if motor and motor.Parent then
                pcall(function() motor.Transform = CFrame.identity end)
            end
        end
        glitchMotors = {}
    end

    local function setGlitch(value)
        glitchEnabled = value
        if glitchConnection then glitchConnection:Disconnect(); glitchConnection = nil end
        if not glitchEnabled then clearGlitchTransforms(); return end

        glitchConnection = RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
            if _G.ScriptDestroyed or not glitchEnabled then return end
            local char = Player.Character
            if not char then return end
            local motors = {}
            for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and obj.Part0 and obj.Part1 then
                    table.insert(motors, obj)
                end
            end
            if #motors == 0 then return end

            -- Маленькие рывки суставов: не двигают корневую часть и не меняют физику.
            local count = (glitchPack == "Сильный глич") and 5 or (glitchPack == "Робот") and 3 or 2
            for i = 1, count do
                local motor = motors[random:NextInteger(1, #motors)]
                local amount = glitchPack == "Сильный глич" and 0.18 or 0.08
                local rx = random:NextNumber(-amount, amount)
                local ry = random:NextNumber(-amount, amount)
                local rz = random:NextNumber(-amount, amount)
                local pos = glitchPack == "Робот" and 0.025 or 0.012
                motor.Transform = CFrame.new(random:NextNumber(-pos, pos), random:NextNumber(-pos, pos), 0) * CFrame.Angles(rx, ry, rz)
                glitchMotors[motor] = true
            end
        end)
    end

    createParagraph(ragdollPage, "🛡️ Защита персонажа", "Анти-рагдолл пытается удержать Humanoid в обычном состоянии. Совместимость зависит от конкретной игры и её серверной системы.")
    createToggle(ragdollPage, "🛡️ Анти-рагдолл (best effort)", function(value)
        setAntiRagdoll(value)
    end)
    createParagraph(ragdollPage, "🎭 Визуальные анимационные паки", "Паки меняют только локальную позу суставов. Другие игроки их не увидят без серверной поддержки игры.")
    createButton(ragdollPage, "Выбрать пак: Глич", function()
        glitchPack = "Глич"
        warn("[Prompt_Hacker] Выбран визуальный пак: Глич")
    end)
    createButton(ragdollPage, "Выбрать пак: Сильный глич", function()
        glitchPack = "Сильный глич"
        warn("[Prompt_Hacker] Выбран визуальный пак: Сильный глич")
    end)
    createButton(ragdollPage, "Выбрать пак: Робот", function()
        glitchPack = "Робот"
        warn("[Prompt_Hacker] Выбран визуальный пак: Робот")
    end)
    createToggle(ragdollPage, "⚡ Включить выбранный визуальный пак", function(value)
        setGlitch(value)
    end)

    Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        table.clear(savedStateFlags)
        table.clear(glitchMotors)
        task.wait(0.35)
        if antiRagdollEnabled then applyAntiRagdoll(Player.Character) end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do task.wait(0.25) end
        setAntiRagdoll(false)
        setGlitch(false)
    end)
end


local function getCustomMoveDirection(hum)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local moveDir = hum and hum.MoveDirection or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    if moveDir.Magnitude == 0 and (flying or godmodeEnabled or invisActive) then
        local dir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then dir = dir + camera.CFrame.LookVector end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then dir = dir - camera.CFrame.LookVector end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then dir = dir - camera.CFrame.RightVector end
        if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then dir = dir + camera.CFrame.RightVector end
        if dir.Magnitude > 0 then return Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z).Unit end
    end
    return moveDir
end

local phantomModeType = "none"
local phantomRunning = false
local phantomMiniBtn = nil
local phantomSafeOffset = Vector3.new(3500, 1500, 3500)
local isCastingSkill = false
local castReturnTimer = 0

local function destroyPhantomObjects()
    if godAirPlatform then pcall(function() godAirPlatform:Destroy() end) godAirPlatform = nil end
    if ghostSimulationCube then pcall(function() ghostSimulationCube:Destroy() end) ghostSimulationCube = nil end
    if ghostVisualClone then pcall(function() ghostVisualClone:Destroy() end) ghostVisualClone = nil end
    if godGroundCube then pcall(function() godGroundCube:Destroy() end) godGroundCube = nil end
    if godVisualClone then pcall(function() godVisualClone:Destroy() end) godVisualClone = nil end
    isCastingSkill = false
    castReturnTimer = 0
end

local function triggerQuantumSkillCast(holdDuration)
    if not phantomRunning or not ghostSimulationCube then return end
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end

    castReturnTimer = tick() + (holdDuration or 0.25)
    if isCastingSkill then return end
    isCastingSkill = true

    task.spawn(function()
        while phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube and hrp and tick() < castReturnTimer and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            hrp.CFrame = ghostSimulationCube.CFrame
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        if phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube and hrp then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(ghostSimulationCube.Position + phantomSafeOffset + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
            hrp.Anchored = true
        end
        isCastingSkill = false
    end)
end

local function stopPhantomSystem(keepMiniBtn)
    phantomRunning = false
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if hrp then
        hrp.Anchored = false
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        if ghostSimulationCube then
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(ghostSimulationCube.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
        end
    end
    destroyPhantomObjects()
    if hum then
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
    end
    if not keepMiniBtn and phantomMiniBtn then
        pcall(function() phantomMiniBtn:Destroy() end)
        phantomMiniBtn = nil
    end
end
local function startPhantomSystem(mode)
    stopPhantomSystem(true)
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum then return end

    phantomModeType = mode
    phantomRunning = true
    local startCFrame = hrp.CFrame
    local isInvis = (mode == "invis")

    godAirPlatform = Instance.new("Part")
    godAirPlatform.Name = "Phantom_AirPlatform"
    godAirPlatform.Size = Vector3.new(30, 2, 30)
    godAirPlatform.Position = startCFrame.Position + phantomSafeOffset
    godAirPlatform.Anchored = true
    godAirPlatform.Transparency = 1
    godAirPlatform.CanCollide = true
    godAirPlatform.Parent = workspace

    ghostSimulationCube = Instance.new("Part")
    ghostSimulationCube.Name = "Phantom_SimCube"
    ghostSimulationCube.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 2)
    ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = startCFrame
    ghostSimulationCube.Anchored = true
    ghostSimulationCube.Transparency = 1
    ghostSimulationCube.CanCollide = false
    ghostSimulationCube.Parent = workspace
    godGroundCube = ghostSimulationCube

    char.Archivable = true
    ghostVisualClone = char:Clone()
    ghostVisualClone.Name = "Phantom_Visual"
    ghostVisualClone.Parent = workspace
    godVisualClone = ghostVisualClone

    local cloneRoot = ghostVisualClone:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if cloneRoot then
        ghostVisualClone.PrimaryPart = cloneRoot
    end

    local origTransparency = {}
    for _, origPart in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if origPart:IsA("BasePart") then
            origTransparency[origPart.Name] = origPart.Transparency
        end
    end

    for _, p in pairs(ghostVisualClone:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") then
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.Anchored = true
            local wasHidden = (origTransparency[p.Name] and origTransparency[p.Name] >= 0.9)
            if p.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or p.Name:lower():find("hitbox") or p.Name:lower():find("root") or wasHidden then
                p.Transparency = 1
            else
                p.Transparency = isInvis and 0.65 or 0
            end
        elseif p:IsA("Decal") or p:IsA("Texture") then
            p.Transparency = isInvis and 0.5 or 0
        elseif p:IsA("Script") or p:IsA("LocalScript") then
            p:Destroy()
        end
    end
    local cloneHum = ghostVisualClone:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if cloneHum then cloneHum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None end

    if cloneRoot then
        ghostVisualClone:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(startCFrame)
    end

    local lowestY = startCFrame.Position.Y - 3
    for _, part in pairs(ghostVisualClone:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            local partBottom = part.Position.Y - (part.Size.Y * 0.5)
            if partBottom < lowestY then
                lowestY = partBottom
            end
        end
    end
    local hipOffset = math.max(startCFrame.Position.Y - lowestY, 2.5) + 0.05

    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(startCFrame.Position + phantomSafeOffset + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
    hrp.Anchored = true

    local camTarget = ghostVisualClone:FindFirstChild("Head") or cloneRoot or ghostSimulationCube
    workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = camTarget

    local conns = {}
    local function hookTool(tool)
        if tool:IsA("Tool") then
            table.insert(conns, tool.Activated:Connect(function()
                if phantomRunning then triggerQuantumSkillCast(0.28) end
            end))
        end
    end
    for _, t in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do hookTool(t) end
    table.insert(conns, char.ChildAdded:Connect(hookTool))

    table.insert(conns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not phantomRunning or not ghostSimulationCube then return end
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            local n = obj.Name:lower()
            local isMyAttack = obj:IsDescendantOf(char)
                or ((n:find("hitbox") or n:find("slash") or n:find("punch") or n:find("dmg") or n:find("damage"))
                    and (obj.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 25)
            if isMyAttack then
                triggerQuantumSkillCast(0.28)
            end
        end
    end))

    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    table.insert(conns, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
        if not phantomRunning or gp then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.One or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Two or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Three or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Four then
            triggerQuantumSkillCast(0.28)
        end
    end))

    task.spawn(function()
        local lastTick = tick()
        local verticalVel = 0

        while phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube and char and hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local dt = tick() - lastTick
            lastTick = tick()

            if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                if not spectatingPlayer and not freecamEnabled and camTarget and camTarget.Parent then
                    workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = camTarget
                end

                local moveDir = getCustomMoveDirection(hum)
                local currentPos = ghostSimulationCube.Position

                if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                    local flatDir = Vector3.new(moveDir.X, 0, moveDir.Z).Unit
                    local step = flatDir * (walkSpeedValue * dt)
                    local ray = Ray.new(currentPos, flatDir * (step.Magnitude + 2))
                    local hitPart = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray, {char, ghostSimulationCube, ghostVisualClone, godAirPlatform})
                    if not hitPart or not hitPart.CanCollide or noclip then
                        currentPos = currentPos + step
                    end
                end

                if hum.Jump and verticalVel == 0 then
                    verticalVel = jumpPowerValue
                end

                local downRay = Ray.new(currentPos + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), Vector3.new(0, -(hipOffset + 3.0), 0))
                local floorPart, floorPos = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(downRay, {char, ghostSimulationCube, ghostVisualClone, godAirPlatform})

                if floorPart and floorPart.CanCollide and verticalVel <= 0 and not noclip then
                    currentPos = Vector3.new(currentPos.X, floorPos.Y + hipOffset, currentPos.Z)
                    verticalVel = 0
                else
                    verticalVel = verticalVel - (196.2 * dt)
                    currentPos = currentPos + Vector3.new(0, verticalVel * dt, 0)
                    if currentPos.Y < -300 then
                        currentPos = Vector3.new(currentPos.X, 50, currentPos.Z)
                        verticalVel = 0
                    end
                end

                local lookVec = moveDir.Magnitude > 0 and Vector3.new(moveDir.X, 0, moveDir.Z) or ghostSimulationCube.CFrame.LookVector
                if lookVec.Magnitude > 0 then
                    ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentPos, currentPos + lookVec)
                else
                    ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentPos)
                end

                if ghostVisualClone and ghostVisualClone.PrimaryPart then
                    ghostVisualClone:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(ghostSimulationCube.CFrame)
                end

                if not isCastingSkill and godAirPlatform then
                    godAirPlatform.Position = currentPos + phantomSafeOffset
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentPos + phantomSafeOffset + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
                end
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end

        for _, c in pairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        if phantomRunning then stopPhantomSystem(false) end
    end)
end

local function createOrUpdatePhantomMiniBtn(mode)
    if phantomMiniBtn then pcall(function() phantomMiniBtn:Destroy() end) phantomMiniBtn = nil end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "PhantomMiniToggle"
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 34)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -65, 0.13, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 50)
    btn.Text = (mode == "invis") and "👻 Инвиз: ВКЛ" or "🛡 Бог: ВКЛ"
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.TextSize = 13
    btn.Active = true
    btn.Draggable = true
    btn.ZIndex = 60
    btn.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)

    phantomMiniBtn = btn

    local lastTap = 0
    local function toggleMini()
        if tick() - lastTap < 0.25 then return end
        lastTap = tick()
        if phantomRunning then
            stopPhantomSystem(true)
            btn.Text = (mode == "invis") and "👻 Инвиз: ВЫКЛ" or "🛡 Бог: ВЫКЛ"
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
        else
            startPhantomSystem(mode)
            btn.Text = (mode == "invis") and "👻 Инвиз: ВКЛ" or "🛡 Бог: ВКЛ"
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 50)
        end
    end

    btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(toggleMini)
    btn.TouchTap:Connect(toggleMini)
end

Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
    stopPhantomSystem(false)
    godmodeEnabled = false
    invisActive = false
end)

local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local godToggleObj = nil
local invisToggleObj = nil

godToggleObj = createToggle(mainPage, "🛡 Режим Бога (Анти-урон + Уклонение)", function(Value)
    godmodeEnabled = Value
    if godmodeEnabled then
        if invisActive then
            invisActive = false
            if invisToggleObj and invisToggleObj.Set then invisToggleObj.Set(false) end
        end
        startPhantomSystem("god")
        createOrUpdatePhantomMiniBtn("god")
    else
        if not invisActive then
            stopPhantomSystem(false)
        end
    end
end)

invisToggleObj = createToggle(mainPage, "👻 Невидимость (Квантовый инвиз)", function(Value)
    invisActive = Value
    if invisActive then
        if godmodeEnabled then
            godmodeEnabled = false
            if godToggleObj and godToggleObj.Set then godToggleObj.Set(false) end
        end
        startPhantomSystem("invis")
        createOrUpdatePhantomMiniBtn("invis")
    else
        if not godmodeEnabled then
            stopPhantomSystem(false)
        end
    end
end)

-- после смерти/респавна возвращаем тумблеры в положение "ВЫКЛ", чтобы они не врали

    if godToggleObj and godToggleObj.Set then godToggleObj.Set(false) end
    if invisToggleObj and invisToggleObj.Set then invisToggleObj.Set(false) end
end)

clickTeleportEnabled = false

    clickTeleportEnabled = false
    if _G.PH_ClickTpBtn then pcall(function() _G.PH_ClickTpBtn:Destroy() end) _G.PH_ClickTpBtn = nil end
    if not Value then return end

    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")

    b.Parent = ScreenGui

        clickTeleportEnabled = not clickTeleportEnabled
        b.Text = clickTeleportEnabled and "TP: ON" or "TP: OFF"

UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed or not clickTeleportEnabled or _G.ScriptPaused or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local unitRay = cam:ViewportPointToRay(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
        local ignore = {Player.Character}
        if ghostVisualClone then table.insert(ignore, ghostVisualClone) end
        if ghostSimulationCube then table.insert(ignore, ghostSimulationCube) end
        rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = ignore
        rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude

        local result = workspace:Raycast(unitRay.Origin, unitRay.Direction * 1000, rayParams)
        if result then
            local targetPos = result.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
            if phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube then
                ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                if ghostVisualClone and ghostVisualClone.PrimaryPart then
                    ghostVisualClone:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(ghostSimulationCube.CFrame)
                end
            elseif Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                Player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
            end
        end
    end
end)

createToggle(mainPage, "🧱 Noclip (Прохождение сквозь стены)", function(Value)
    noclip = Value
    if not noclip and Player.Character then
        for _, part in pairs(Player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                part.CanCollide = true
            end
        end
    end
end)

RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
    if noclip and not _G.ScriptPaused and not _G.ScriptDestroyed and Player.Character then
        for _, part in pairs(Player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end
end)

createToggle(mainPage, "🦘 Бесконечный прыжок в воздухе", function(Value)
    infiniteJumpEnabled = Value
end)

UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if infiniteJumpEnabled and not _G.ScriptPaused and not _G.ScriptDestroyed then
        if phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube then
            ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = ghostSimulationCube.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)
        elseif Player.Character then
            local hum = Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
        end
    end
end)

-- АНТИ-ОТБРОС БУНКЕР (КОРОБКА-ТОРМОЗ ПРИ ПОЛУЧЕНИИ УРОНА)
local brakeBoxEnabled = false
local brakeBoxFolder = nil

local function destroyBrakeBox()
    if brakeBoxFolder then
        pcall(function() brakeBoxFolder:Destroy() end)
        brakeBoxFolder = nil
    end
end

createToggle(mainPage, "📦 Бункер-тормоз при ударе (Анти-отброс)", function(Value)
    brakeBoxEnabled = Value
    destroyBrakeBox()
    if not brakeBoxEnabled then return end

    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "Prompt_BrakeBunker"
    folder.Parent = workspace
    brakeBoxFolder = folder

    local walls = {}
    local wallOffsets = {
        {name = "Front", pos = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, -5.5), size = Vector3.new(12, 11, 1.5)},
        {name = "Back",  pos = Vector3.new(0, 1.5,  5.5), size = Vector3.new(12, 11, 1.5)},
        {name = "Left",  pos = Vector3.new(-5.5, 1.5, 0), size = Vector3.new(1.5, 11, 12)},
        {name = "Right", pos = Vector3.new( 5.5, 1.5, 0), size = Vector3.new(1.5, 11, 12)},
        {name = "Floor", pos = Vector3.new(0, -4.3,   0), size = Vector3.new(12, 1.5, 12)}
    }

    for _, cfg in ipairs(wallOffsets) do
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Name = "Bunker_" .. cfg.name
        p.Size = cfg.size
        p.Anchored = true
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.Transparency = 1
        p.Parent = folder

        local sel = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
        sel.Name = "Edge"
        sel.Adornee = p
        sel.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 210, 255)
        sel.SurfaceTransparency = 1
        sel.LineThickness = 0.04
        sel.Parent = p

        table.insert(walls, {part = p, offset = cfg.pos, edge = sel})
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local lastHealth = nil
        local lockedUntil = 0
        local lockedCenter = nil

        while brakeBoxEnabled and folder and folder.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local char = Player.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                if lastHealth and hum.Health < lastHealth - 0.5 then
                    lockedUntil = tick() + 1.0
                    lockedCenter = hrp.Position
                end
                lastHealth = hum.Health

                local isLocked = (tick() < lockedUntil)
                local centerPos = isLocked and lockedCenter or hrp.Position

                for _, item in ipairs(walls) do
                    item.part.Position = centerPos + item.offset
                    item.part.CanCollide = isLocked
                    item.edge.Color3 = isLocked and Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 45) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 210, 255)
                    item.edge.LineThickness = isLocked and 0.08 or 0.04
                end
            else
                lastHealth = nil
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end

        destroyBrakeBox()
    end)
end)
-- ЛИЧНОЕ РАДИО / СВОЯ МУЗЫКА В ИГРЕ (🎧)
local customRadioSound = nil
local radioSoundId = "1848354536"
local radioVolume = 1.5

createInput(mainPage, "🎧 ID трека для Радио (Цифры из Roblox)", "Вставь ID звука...", function(Text)
    local cleanId = Text:gsub("%D", "")
    if cleanId ~= "" then
        radioSoundId = cleanId
        if customRadioSound then
            customRadioSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. radioSoundId
            customRadioSound:Play()
        end
    end
end)

createInput(mainPage, "🔊 Громкость Радио (от 0.1 до 5)", "Число (дефолт 1.5)...", function(Text)
    local vol = tonumber(Text)
    if vol and vol >= 0 and vol <= 10 then
        radioVolume = vol
        if customRadioSound then customRadioSound.Volume = radioVolume end
    end
end)

createToggle(mainPage, "📻 Включить личное Радио", function(Value)
    if customRadioSound then
        pcall(function() customRadioSound:Stop(); customRadioSound:Destroy() end)
        customRadioSound = nil
    end

    if Value then
        local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
        snd.Name = "PromptPersonalRadio"
        snd.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. radioSoundId
        snd.Volume = radioVolume
        snd.Looped = true
        snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
        snd:Play()
        customRadioSound = snd
    end
end)

-- ТУМБЛЕР: ВЫКЛЮЧИТЬ ВСЕ ЗВУКИ КРОМЕ ЛИЧНОГО РАДИО (🔇)
do
    local muteAll = false
    local originalVolumes = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local muteConns = {}

    local function isMyRadio(snd)
        return snd.Name == "PromptPersonalRadio" or snd == customRadioSound
    end

    local function muteSound(obj)
        if not obj:IsA("Sound") or isMyRadio(obj) then return end
        if originalVolumes[obj] == nil then originalVolumes[obj] = obj.Volume end
        pcall(function() obj.Volume = 0 end)
        -- если игра сама поднимет громкость, сразу возвращаем в 0
        table.insert(muteConns, obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Volume"):Connect(function()
            if muteAll and obj.Volume ~= 0 and not isMyRadio(obj) then
                pcall(function() obj.Volume = 0 end)
            end
        end))
    end

    createToggle(mainPage, "🔇 Выключить все звуки (кроме радио)", function(Value)
        muteAll = Value
        for _, c in ipairs(muteConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        muteConns = {}

        if muteAll then
            for _, d in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do muteSound(d) end
            table.insert(muteConns, game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if muteAll then muteSound(d) end
            end))
        else
            for snd, vol in pairs(originalVolumes) do
                if snd.Parent then pcall(function() snd.Volume = vol end) end
            end
            originalVolumes = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        end
    end)
end

-- ПРОСТОЕ ОКОШКО КОМПАСА И КООРДИНАТ (🧭 XYZ + КУДА СМОТРЮ)
local compassEnabled = false
local compassBox = nil

createToggle(mainPage, "🧭 Окошко координат (XYZ + Направление)", function(Value)
    compassEnabled = Value
    if compassBox then
        pcall(function() compassBox:Destroy() end)
        compassBox = nil
    end

    if not compassEnabled then return end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = "PromptSimpleCompass"
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 210, 0, 50)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -105, 0.02, 0)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 25, 30)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    box.Active = true
    box.Draggable = true
    box.ZIndex = 70
    box.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", box)
    stroke.Thickness = 1.8
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 255)

    local dirLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", box)
    dirLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22)
    dirLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 3)
    dirLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    dirLabel.Text = "🧭 Смотрю: Север"
    dirLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 70)
    dirLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    dirLabel.TextSize = 14
    dirLabel.ZIndex = 71

    local xyzLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", box)
    xyzLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22)
    xyzLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 25)
    xyzLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    xyzLabel.Text = "X: 0   Y: 0   Z: 0"
    xyzLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 240, 255)
    xyzLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    xyzLabel.TextSize = 13
    xyzLabel.ZIndex = 71

    compassBox = box

    local dirNames = {
        "Север", "Северо-Восток", "Восток", "Юго-Восток",
        "Юг", "Юго-Запад", "Запад", "Северо-Запад"
    }

    task.spawn(function()
        while compassEnabled and box and box.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local char = Player.Character
            local myRoot = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera

            if myRoot and cam then
                local pos = myRoot.Position
                local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
                local deg = (math.deg(math.atan2(look.X, -look.Z)) + 360) % 360
                local idx = (math.floor((deg + 22.5) / 45) % 8) + 1

                dirLabel.Text = "🧭 Смотрю: " .. dirNames[idx]
                xyzLabel.Text = string.format("X: %d   Y: %d   Z: %d", math.floor(pos.X), math.floor(pos.Y), math.floor(pos.Z))
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end

        if box and box.Parent then box:Destroy() end
    end)
end)

-- СЕКУНДОМЕР / ТАЙМЕР НА ЭКРАНЕ (⏱ СТАРТ / ПАУЗА / СБРОС)
local stopwatchToggleEnabled = false
local stopwatchBox = nil

createToggle(mainPage, "⏱ Секундомер на экране (Старт / Сброс)", function(Value)
    stopwatchToggleEnabled = Value
    if stopwatchBox then
        pcall(function() stopwatchBox:Destroy() end)
        stopwatchBox = nil
    end

    if not stopwatchToggleEnabled then return end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = "PromptStopwatchBox"
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 175, 0, 68)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(0.04, 0, 0.22, 0)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 25, 32)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    box.Active = true
    box.Draggable = true
    box.ZIndex = 70
    box.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", box)
    stroke.Thickness = 1.8
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60)

    local timeLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", box)
    timeLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 28)
    timeLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 4)
    timeLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    timeLbl.Text = "⏱ 00:00.0"
    timeLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    timeLbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    timeLbl.TextSize = 18
    timeLbl.ZIndex = 71

    local startBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", box)
    startBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.46, 0, 0, 26)
    startBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.03, 0, 0, 36)
    startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 175, 65)
    startBtn.Text = "▶ Старт"
    startBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    startBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    startBtn.TextSize = 13
    startBtn.ZIndex = 71
    Instance.new("UICorner", startBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

    local resetBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", box)
    resetBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.46, 0, 0, 26)
    resetBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.51, 0, 0, 36)
    resetBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(195, 55, 55)
    resetBtn.Text = "🔄 Сброс"
    resetBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    resetBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    resetBtn.TextSize = 13
    resetBtn.ZIndex = 71
    Instance.new("UICorner", resetBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

    stopwatchBox = box

    local isRunning = false
    local elapsed = 0
    local lastTick = tick()

    startBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        isRunning = not isRunning
        if isRunning then
            lastTick = tick()
            startBtn.Text = "⏸ Пауза"
            startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 165, 35)
        else
            startBtn.Text = "▶ Старт"
            startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 175, 65)
        end
    end)

    resetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        isRunning = false
        elapsed = 0
        timeLbl.Text = "⏱ 00:00.0"
        startBtn.Text = "▶ Старт"
        startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 175, 65)
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while stopwatchToggleEnabled and box and box.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if isRunning then
                local now = tick()
                elapsed = elapsed + (now - lastTick)
                lastTick = now

                local mins = math.floor(elapsed / 60)
                local secs = elapsed % 60
                timeLbl.Text = string.format("⏱ %02d:%04.1f", mins, secs)
            else
                lastTick = tick()
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end

        if box and box.Parent then box:Destroy() end
    end)
end)

-- =========================================================
-- ----- КОНЕЦ ЧАСТИ 5.2 -----

-- ----- НАЧАЛО ЧАСТИ 6.1 -----

local keysPressed = {w = false, a = false, s = false, d = false}
local flyVelocity = nil
local flyGyro = nil
local flyActiveState = false
local flyHeartbeat = nil
local flyJumpConnection = nil
local flyAscendUntil = 0

local function destroyAllWasdFrames()
    pcall(function()
        for _, v in pairs(game:GetService("CoreGui"):GetDescendants()) do
            if v.Name == "WASD_FlyFrame" then v:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        if Player and Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
            for _, v in pairs(Player.PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do
                if v.Name == "WASD_FlyFrame" then v:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end)
    if ScreenGui then
        for _, v in pairs(ScreenGui:GetChildren()) do
            if v.Name == "WASD_FlyFrame" then v:Destroy() end
        end
    end
end

local function createFlyButton(parent, text, pos, size, key)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = size
    btn.Position = pos
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
    btn.Text = text
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.TextSize = 14
    btn.Active = true
    btn.Parent = parent
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    
    btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
        keysPressed[key] = true
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 40)
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
    end)
    btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
        keysPressed[key] = false
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end)
    btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        keysPressed[key] = false
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end)
end

local function stopFlyPhysics()
    flyActiveState = false
    flyAscendUntil = 0
    keysPressed = {w = false, a = false, s = false, d = false, down = false}

    if flyHeartbeat then pcall(function() flyHeartbeat:Disconnect() end) flyHeartbeat = nil end
    if flyJumpConnection then pcall(function() flyJumpConnection:Disconnect() end) flyJumpConnection = nil end
    if flyVelocity then pcall(function() flyVelocity:Destroy() end) flyVelocity = nil end
    if flyGyro then pcall(function() flyGyro:Destroy() end) flyGyro = nil end

    local char = Player.Character
    if char then
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hrp then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            for _, obj in pairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                if obj.Name == "PH_RealityFlyVelocity" or obj.Name == "PH_RealityFlyGyro" then
                    pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
        if hum then
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum.AutoRotate = true
            hum.Sit = false
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
        end
    end
end

local function startFlyPhysics()
    stopFlyPhysics()

    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

    flyActiveState = true

    -- Та же физическая схема, что в Reality Modifier.
    flyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
    flyVelocity.Name = "PH_RealityFlyVelocity"
    flyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e6, 1e6, 1e6)
    flyVelocity.P = 18000
    flyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    flyVelocity.Parent = hrp

    flyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
    flyGyro.Name = "PH_RealityFlyGyro"
    flyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e6, 1e6, 1e6)
    flyGyro.P = 12000
    flyGyro.D = 500
    flyGyro.CFrame = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame or hrp.CFrame
    flyGyro.Parent = hrp

    hum.PlatformStand = true
    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)

    flyHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not flyActiveState or not flying or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
        if _G.ScriptPaused then
            if flyVelocity then flyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero end
            return
        end

        local currentChar = Player.Character
        if currentChar ~= char or not hrp.Parent or not hum.Parent or hum.Health <= 0 then
            stopFlyPhysics()
            return
        end

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera or not flyVelocity or not flyGyro then return end

        if noclip then
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = false end
            end
        end

        -- Основное направление берётся из стандартного управления Roblox,
        -- как в Reality Modifier (клавиатура и мобильный джойстик).
        local move = hum.MoveDirection
        if move.Magnitude <= 0.05 then
            -- Резерв для встроенных экранных WASD-кнопок этого скрипта.
            local fallback = Vector3.zero
            local look3 = camera.CFrame.LookVector
            local right3 = camera.CFrame.RightVector
            local look = Vector3.new(look3.X, 0, look3.Z)
            local right = Vector3.new(right3.X, 0, right3.Z)
            if keysPressed.w and look.Magnitude > 0.001 then fallback = fallback + look.Unit end
            if keysPressed.s and look.Magnitude > 0.001 then fallback = fallback - look.Unit end
            if keysPressed.a and right.Magnitude > 0.001 then fallback = fallback - right.Unit end
            if keysPressed.d and right.Magnitude > 0.001 then fallback = fallback + right.Unit end
            if fallback.Magnitude > 0.05 then move = fallback.Unit end
        end

        local horizontal = Vector3.zero
        if move.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            horizontal = move.Unit * flySpeedValue
        end

        local vertical = 0
        if keysPressed.down or PH_UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then
            vertical = -42
        elseif PH_UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) or tick() < flyAscendUntil then
            vertical = 42
        end

        flyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(horizontal.X, vertical, horizontal.Z)
        flyGyro.CFrame = camera.CFrame
    end)

    -- Прыжок / стандартная кнопка Jump на телефоне даёт короткий импульс вверх.
    flyJumpConnection = PH_UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
        if flyActiveState then flyAscendUntil = tick() + 0.28 end
    end)
end

-- СВОБОДНАЯ КАМЕРА (FREECAM С ПОВОРОТОМ ПАЛЬЦЕМ / МЫШЬЮ И WASD)
createToggle(visualsPage, "🎥 Свободная камера (Freecam)", function(Value)
    freecamEnabled = Value
    if freecamEnabled and flying then
        flying = false
        stopFlyPhysics()
    end
    destroyAllWasdFrames()

    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if freecamEnabled then
        if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

        local wasdFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        wasdFrame.Name = "WASD_FlyFrame"
        wasdFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 156, 0, 124)
        wasdFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
        wasdFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.04, 0, 0.92, 0)
        wasdFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        wasdFrame.ZIndex = 50
        wasdFrame.Parent = ScreenGui

        createFlyButton(wasdFrame, "W", UDim2.new(0.5, -19, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 34), "w")
        createFlyButton(wasdFrame, "A", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 40), UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 34), "a")
        createFlyButton(wasdFrame, "S", UDim2.new(0.5, -19, 0, 40), UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 34), "s")
        createFlyButton(wasdFrame, "D", UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 40), UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 34), "d")

        local camSpeedBox = Instance.new("TextBox", wasdFrame)
        camSpeedBox.Name = "FreecamSpeedInput"
        camSpeedBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 28)
        camSpeedBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 82)
        camSpeedBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
        camSpeedBox.Text = "Скорость: " .. flySpeedValue
        camSpeedBox.PlaceholderText = "Число..."
        camSpeedBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 220, 255)
        camSpeedBox.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        camSpeedBox.TextSize = 13
        camSpeedBox.ClearTextOnFocus = true
        camSpeedBox.ZIndex = 55
        Instance.new("UICorner", camSpeedBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

        camSpeedBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local num = tonumber(camSpeedBox.Text)
            if num and num > 0 then flySpeedValue = num end
            camSpeedBox.Text = "Скорость: " .. flySpeedValue
        end)

        local wasAnchored = hrp and hrp.Anchored or false
        if hrp and not phantomRunning then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            hrp.Anchored = true
        end

        local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
        local rx, ry, _ = cam.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
        local pitch = rx
        local yaw = ry
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable

        local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local lookConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input, gp)
            if not freecamEnabled or gp then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                if input.Position.X < 200 and input.Position.Y > (cam.ViewportSize.Y - 200) then return end
                yaw = yaw - (input.Delta.X * 0.0045)
                pitch = math.clamp(pitch - (input.Delta.Y * 0.0045), -1.45, 1.45)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            local lastT = tick()
            while freecamEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
                local dt = tick() - lastT
                lastT = tick()

                local rot = CFrame.fromEulerAnglesYXZ(pitch, yaw, 0)
                if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                    local moveVec = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    if keysPressed.w or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then moveVec = moveVec + rot.LookVector end
                    if keysPressed.s or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then moveVec = moveVec - rot.LookVector end
                    if keysPressed.a or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then moveVec = moveVec - rot.RightVector end
                    if keysPressed.d or UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then moveVec = moveVec + rot.RightVector end

                    if moveVec.Magnitude > 0 then
                        camPos = camPos + moveVec.Unit * (flySpeedValue * dt)
                    end
                end
                cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(camPos) * rot
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end

            if lookConn then lookConn:Disconnect() end
            keysPressed = {w = false, a = false, s = false, d = false}
            destroyAllWasdFrames()
            cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            if hrp and hrp.Parent and not phantomRunning and not airFreezeEnabled then
                hrp.Anchored = wasAnchored
            end
            if phantomRunning and ghostVisualClone then
                cam.CameraSubject = ghostVisualClone:FindFirstChild("Head") or ghostSimulationCube
            elseif hum and hum.Parent then
                cam.CameraSubject = hum
            end
        end)
    else
        keysPressed = {w = false, a = false, s = false, d = false}
        destroyAllWasdFrames()
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        if hrp and hrp.Parent and not phantomRunning and not airFreezeEnabled then
            hrp.Anchored = false
        end
        if phantomRunning and ghostVisualClone then
            cam.CameraSubject = ghostVisualClone:FindFirstChild("Head") or ghostSimulationCube
        elseif hum and hum.Parent then
            cam.CameraSubject = hum
        end
    end
end)

local builderModeEnabled = false

local floorCount = 0
