createToggle(movePage, "🏗️ Режим Стройки (Builder)", function(Value)
    builderModeEnabled = Value

    -- 🎵 МУЗЫКА РЕЖИМА СТРОЙКИ: впиши ID аудио ИЛИ прямую ссылку на .mp3 (если обе строки пустые - музыки нет)
    local BUILDER_MUSIC_ID = ""      -- например "rbxassetid://123456789" или просто 123456789
    local BUILDER_MUSIC_URL = ""     -- прямая ссылка на .mp3 (нужны writefile и getcustomasset в экзекьюторе)
    local BUILDER_MUSIC_VOLUME = 0.5

    local function stopBuilderMusic()
        if _G.PromptBuilderMusic then
            pcall(function() _G.PromptBuilderMusic:Destroy() end)
            _G.PromptBuilderMusic = nil
        end
    end

    local function startBuilderMusic()
        stopBuilderMusic()
        local id = tostring(BUILDER_MUSIC_ID)
        if id == "" and BUILDER_MUSIC_URL ~= "" then
            pcall(function()
                writefile("PromptBuilderMusic.mp3", game:HttpGet(BUILDER_MUSIC_URL))
                id = getcustomasset("PromptBuilderMusic.mp3")
            end)
        end
        if id == "" then return end
        if tonumber(id) then id = "rbxassetid://" .. id end
        local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
        snd.Name = "PromptBuilderMusic"
        snd.SoundId = id
        snd.Looped = true
        snd.Volume = BUILDER_MUSIC_VOLUME
        snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
        snd:Play()
        _G.PromptBuilderMusic = snd
    end
    
    if builderModeEnabled then
        startBuilderMusic()
        if not topNotif then
            topNotif = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            topNotif.Size = UDim2.new(0, 250, 0, 40)
            topNotif.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -125, 0.05, 0)
            topNotif.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 30, 35)
            topNotif.Text = "🏗️ РЕЖИМ СТРОЙКИ 👷"
            topNotif.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
            topNotif.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            topNotif.TextSize = 16
            topNotif.Parent = ScreenGui
            topNotif.ZIndex = 80
            Instance.new("UICorner", topNotif).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        end
        topNotif.Visible = true
        
        if not builderGui then
            builderGui = Instance.new("Frame")
            builderGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 300) -- Компактная панель
            builderGui.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -150)
            builderGui.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
            builderGui.Parent = ScreenGui
            builderGui.ZIndex = 75
            builderGui.ClipsDescendants = true
            Instance.new("UICorner", builderGui).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
            
            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", builderGui)
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
            stroke.Thickness = 2
            
            local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", builderGui)
            title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 24)
            title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 0)
            title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            title.Text = "🛠️ Инструменты"
            title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            title.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            title.TextSize = 13
            title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            title.ZIndex = 76
            
            -- Тело панели (можно свернуть кнопкой справа сверху)
            local body = Instance.new("Frame", builderGui)
            body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -24)
            body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 24)
            body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            body.ZIndex = 76
            
            local collapseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", builderGui)
            collapseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 18)
            collapseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 3)
            collapseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
            collapseBtn.Text = "▲"
            collapseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            collapseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            collapseBtn.TextSize = 11
            collapseBtn.ZIndex = 77
            Instance.new("UICorner", collapseBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
            collapseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local open = not body.Visible
                body.Visible = open
                builderGui.Size = open and UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 300) or UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 24)
                collapseBtn.Text = open and "▲" or "▼"
            end)
            
            local function mkBtn(x, y, w, h, text, color, textSize)
                local b = Instance.new("TextButton", body)
                b.Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h)
                b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, y)
                b.BackgroundColor3 = color
                b.Text = text
                b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                b.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                b.TextSize = textSize or 12
                b.ZIndex = 76
                Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
                return b
            end
            
            local sizeBox = Instance.new("TextBox", body)
            sizeBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 138, 0, 24)
            sizeBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 2)
            sizeBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
            sizeBox.Text = "Размер: " .. blockSizeValue
            sizeBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            sizeBox.TextSize = 13
            sizeBox.ZIndex = 76
            Instance.new("UICorner", sizeBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
            
            sizeBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
                local num = tonumber(sizeBox.Text:match("%d+"))
                if num then blockSizeValue = num end
                sizeBox.Text = "Размер: " .. blockSizeValue
            end)
            
            -- Выбор типа
            local wallBtn = mkBtn(6, 30, 67, 24, "Стенка", Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 13)
            local floorBtn = mkBtn(77, 30, 67, 24, "Пол", Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50), 13)

            -- КНОПКИ ВРАЩЕНИЯ (3 ОСИ): красные = наклон (X), зелёные = поворот (Y), синие = крен (Z)
            local pitchU = mkBtn(6, 58, 44, 22, "↑ X", Color3.fromRGB(150, 50, 50))
            local yawL = mkBtn(53, 58, 44, 22, "↺ Y", Color3.fromRGB(50, 150, 50))
            local rollL = mkBtn(100, 58, 44, 22, "↶ Z", Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 150))
            local pitchD = mkBtn(6, 82, 44, 22, "↓ X", Color3.fromRGB(150, 50, 50))
            local yawR = mkBtn(53, 82, 44, 22, "↻ Y", Color3.fromRGB(50, 150, 50))
            local rollR = mkBtn(100, 82, 44, 22, "↷ Z", Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 150))
            pitchU.Visible = false
            pitchD.Visible = false
            yawL.Visible = false
            yawR.Visible = false
            rollL.Visible = false
            rollR.Visible = false

            -- Кнопки подтверждения и отмены
            local confirmBtn = mkBtn(6, 108, 67, 24, "✅", Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 50), 16)
            confirmBtn.Visible = false
            local cancelBtn = mkBtn(77, 108, 67, 24, "❌", Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50), 16)
            cancelBtn.Visible = false

            local deleteBtn = mkBtn(6, 136, 138, 22, "🗑️ Удалить выбранное", Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50), 11)
            deleteBtn.Visible = false

            local listLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", body)
            listLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 138, 0, 16)
            listLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 162)
            listLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            listLabel.Text = "📦 Поставленные блоки:"
            listLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
            listLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            listLabel.TextSize = 11
            listLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            listLabel.ZIndex = 76

            -- Список со скроллом (высота растёт сама, листается пальцем)
            local listScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", body)
            listScroll.Size = UDim2.new(0, 138, 0, 94)
            listScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 178)
            listScroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
            listScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
            listScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
            listScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
            listScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
            listScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            listScroll.ZIndex = 76
            Instance.new("UICorner", listScroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
            
            local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", listScroll)
            listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
            listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

            -- МАГНИТ: прилипание призрака к уже поставленным блокам (чтобы коробка была без швов)
            local snapDistance = 2
            local function halfExtent(cf, size, dir)
                return math.abs(cf.RightVector:Dot(dir)) * size.X / 2
                    + math.abs(cf.UpVector:Dot(dir)) * size.Y / 2
                    + math.abs(cf.LookVector:Dot(dir)) * size.Z / 2
            end

            local function snapToBlocks(pos, rot, size)
                local nearList = {}
                for _, data in ipairs(placedBlocksList) do
                    local part = data.Part
                    if part and part.Parent then
                        local dist = (part.Position - pos).Magnitude
                        if dist < (part.Size.Magnitude + size.Magnitude) / 2 + snapDistance * 2 then
                            table.insert(nearList, {part = part, dist = dist})
                        end
                    end
                end
                table.sort(nearList, function(a, b) return a.dist < b.dist end)

                for k = 1, math.min(#nearList, 4) do
                    local part = nearList[k].part
                    local cfB = part.CFrame
                    local axes = {cfB.RightVector, cfB.UpVector, cfB.LookVector}
                    local sB = {part.Size.X / 2, part.Size.Y / 2, part.Size.Z / 2}
                    local d = cfB:PointToObjectSpace(pos)
                    local dv = {d.X, d.Y, d.Z}
                    local hP = {}
                    local isNear = true
                    local contact, worstGap = 1, -math.huge
                    for i = 1, 3 do
                        hP[i] = halfExtent(rot, size, axes[i])
                        local gap = math.abs(dv[i]) - (sB[i] + hP[i])
                        if gap > snapDistance then isNear = false end
                        if gap > worstGap then worstGap = gap; contact = i end
                    end

                    if isNear then
                        for i = 1, 3 do
                            local cands
                            if i == contact then
                                -- ось касания: вплотную к грани соседнего блока
                                cands = {sB[i] + hP[i], -(sB[i] + hP[i])}
                            else
                                -- остальные оси: выровнять края/центр с соседним блоком
                                local a, b = sB[i] + hP[i], sB[i] - hP[i]
                                cands = {0, a, -a, b, -b}
                            end
                            local bestV, bestD = dv[i], snapDistance
                            for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
                                local dd = math.abs(dv[i] - c)
                                if dd < bestD then bestD = dd; bestV = c end
                            end
                            dv[i] = bestV
                        end
                        pos = cfB:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(dv[1], dv[2], dv[3]))
                    end
                end
                return pos
            end

            -- Функция обновления позиции с учетом 3D-углов (+ магнит)
            local function updatePreviewPos(targetPos)
                if currentPreviewPart then
                    local rot = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rotX), math.rad(rotY), math.rad(rotZ))
                    targetPos = snapToBlocks(targetPos, rot, currentPreviewPart.Size)
                    currentPreviewPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos) * rot
                end
            end

            local function refreshList()
                for _, child in ipairs(listScroll:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("TextButton") then child:Destroy() end
                end
                
                for i, data in ipairs(placedBlocksList) do
                    local itemBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", listScroll)
                    itemBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -5, 0, 22)
                    itemBtn.LayoutOrder = i
                    itemBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
                    itemBtn.Text = data.Name
                    itemBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    itemBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                    itemBtn.TextSize = 11
                    itemBtn.ZIndex = 77
                    Instance.new("UICorner", itemBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
                    
                    itemBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        if currentlySelectedBlock and currentlySelectedBlock.Highlight then
                            currentlySelectedBlock.Highlight:Destroy()
                        end
                        currentlySelectedBlock = data
                        
                        local hl = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
                        hl.Name = "Highlight"
                        hl.Adornee = data.Part
                        hl.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 50)
                        hl.LineThickness = 0.1
                        hl.Parent = data.Part
                        data.Highlight = hl
                        
                        deleteBtn.Visible = true
                    end)
                end
            end

            deleteBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if currentlySelectedBlock then
                    currentlySelectedBlock.Part:Destroy()
                    for i, data in ipairs(placedBlocksList) do
                        if data == currentlySelectedBlock then
                            table.remove(placedBlocksList, i)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    currentlySelectedBlock = nil
                    deleteBtn.Visible = false
                    refreshList()
                end
            end)

            local function resetSelection()
                selectedType = nil
                wallBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                floorBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                pitchU.Visible = false
                pitchD.Visible = false
                yawL.Visible = false
                yawR.Visible = false
                rollL.Visible = false
                rollR.Visible = false
                confirmBtn.Visible = false
                cancelBtn.Visible = false
                rotX, rotY, rotZ = 0, 0, 0
                if currentPreviewPart then
                    currentPreviewPart:Destroy()
                    currentPreviewPart = nil
                end
            end

            local function selectType(t)
                selectedType = t
                wallBtn.BackgroundColor3 = (t == "wall") and Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                floorBtn.BackgroundColor3 = (t == "floor") and Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
                pitchU.Visible = true
                pitchD.Visible = true
                yawL.Visible = true
                yawR.Visible = true
                rollL.Visible = true
                rollR.Visible = true
                confirmBtn.Visible = true
                cancelBtn.Visible = true
                
                if currentPreviewPart then currentPreviewPart:Destroy() end
                
                local p = Instance.new("Part")
                p.Anchored = true
                p.CanCollide = false
                p.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
                p.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40)
                p.Transparency = 0.35
                
                if selectedType == "wall" then
                    p.Size = Vector3.new(blockSizeValue, blockSizeValue * 2, 1)
                else
                    p.Size = Vector3.new(blockSizeValue, 1, blockSizeValue)
                end
                
                local char = Player.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    p.Position = hrp.Position + hrp.CFrame.LookVector * 10
                end
                
                p.Parent = workspace
                currentPreviewPart = p
                rotX, rotY, rotZ = 0, 0, 0
            end
            
            wallBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectType("wall") end)
            floorBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectType("floor") end)
            
            -- Обработчики 3D-вращения
            pitchU.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() rotX = rotX + 15; updatePreviewPos(currentPreviewPart.Position) end)
            pitchD.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() rotX = rotX - 15; updatePreviewPos(currentPreviewPart.Position) end)
            yawL.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() rotY = rotY + 15; updatePreviewPos(currentPreviewPart.Position) end)
            yawR.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() rotY = rotY - 15; updatePreviewPos(currentPreviewPart.Position) end)
            rollL.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() rotZ = rotZ + 15; updatePreviewPos(currentPreviewPart.Position) end)
            rollR.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() rotZ = rotZ - 15; updatePreviewPos(currentPreviewPart.Position) end)

            confirmBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if not currentPreviewPart then return end
                
                local placedBlock = Instance.new("Part")
                placedBlock.Anchored = true
                placedBlock.CanCollide = true
                placedBlock.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                placedBlock.Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
                placedBlock.Size = currentPreviewPart.Size
                placedBlock.CFrame = currentPreviewPart.CFrame
                placedBlock.Parent = workspace
                
                local bName = ""
                if selectedType == "wall" then
                    wallCount = wallCount + 1
                    bName = "Стена " .. wallCount
                else
                    floorCount = floorCount + 1
                    bName = "Пол " .. floorCount
                end

                table.insert(placedBlocksList, {Part = placedBlock, Name = bName})
                refreshList()
                resetSelection()
            end)
            
            cancelBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() resetSelection() end)

            -- Логика перетаскивания пальцем
            local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
            local lastMousePos = nil
            
            UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                if gameProcessed or not builderModeEnabled or not selectedType then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    dragActive = true
                    lastMousePos = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
                end
            end)

            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                if not dragActive or not builderModeEnabled or not currentPreviewPart then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    local unitRay = cam:ViewportPointToRay(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
                    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
                    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
                    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {currentPreviewPart, Player.Character, builderGui}
                    
                    local result = workspace:Raycast(unitRay.Origin, unitRay.Direction * 1000, rayParams)
                    if result then
                        -- Ставим призрака вплотную к поверхности, на которую указал палец
                        local rot = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(rotX), math.rad(rotY), math.rad(rotZ))
                        local n = result.Normal
                        local targetPos = result.Position + n * halfExtent(rot, currentPreviewPart.Size, n)
                        updatePreviewPos(targetPos)
                    end
                end
            end)

            UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
                if not dragActive or not builderModeEnabled then return end
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    dragActive = false
                end
            end)
        end
        builderGui.Visible = true
    else
        stopBuilderMusic()
        if builderGui then builderGui.Visible = false end
        if topNotif then topNotif.Visible = false end
        if currentPreviewPart then currentPreviewPart:Destroy() currentPreviewPart = nil end
    end
end)

-- 3. ПЛАТФОРМА ПОД НОГАМИ (ПОСТОЯННАЯ ХОДЬБА ПО ВОЗДУХУ + РАЗОВОЕ СОЗДАНИЕ)
local underFeetWalkEnabled = false
local underFeetPlatform = nil
local staticFeetPlatforms = {}

createToggle(movePage, "☁️ Ходьба по воздуху (Платформа под ногами)", function(Value)
    underFeetWalkEnabled = Value
    if underFeetPlatform then
        pcall(function() underFeetPlatform:Destroy() end)
        underFeetPlatform = nil
    end

    if underFeetWalkEnabled then
        underFeetPlatform = Instance.new("Part")
        underFeetPlatform.Name = "Prompt_AirWalkPlatform"
        underFeetPlatform.Size = Vector3.new(24, 1.5, 24)
        underFeetPlatform.Anchored = true
        underFeetPlatform.CanCollide = true
        underFeetPlatform.Transparency = 1
        underFeetPlatform.Parent = workspace

        local edge = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
        edge.Adornee = underFeetPlatform
        edge.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 200, 255)
        edge.SurfaceTransparency = 1
        edge.LineThickness = 0.06
        edge.Parent = underFeetPlatform

        task.spawn(function()
            while underFeetWalkEnabled and underFeetPlatform and underFeetPlatform.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
                if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                    local char = Player.Character
                    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local currentRoot = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or hrp

                    if currentRoot then
                        local footOffset = 3.75
                        if hum and hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
                            footOffset = hum.HipHeight + (currentRoot.Size.Y * 0.5) + 0.75
                        end
                        underFeetPlatform.Position = Vector3.new(
                            currentRoot.Position.X,
                            currentRoot.Position.Y - footOffset,
                            currentRoot.Position.Z
                        )
                    end
                end
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end

            if underFeetPlatform then
                pcall(function() underFeetPlatform:Destroy() end)
                underFeetPlatform = nil
            end
        end)
    end
end)

createButton(movePage, "🧱 Поставить платформу под ногами сейчас", function()
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local currentRoot = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or hrp
    if not currentRoot then return end

    local footOffset = 3.75
    if hum and hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
        footOffset = hum.HipHeight + (currentRoot.Size.Y * 0.5) + 0.75
    end

    local plat = Instance.new("Part")
    plat.Name = "Prompt_StaticFootPlatform"
    plat.Size = Vector3.new(30, 1.5, 30)
    plat.Position = Vector3.new(currentRoot.Position.X, currentRoot.Position.Y - footOffset, currentRoot.Position.Z)
    plat.Anchored = true
    plat.CanCollide = true
    plat.Transparency = 1
    plat.Parent = workspace

    local edge = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
    edge.Adornee = plat
    edge.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 60)
    edge.SurfaceTransparency = 1
    edge.LineThickness = 0.06
    edge.Parent = plat

    table.insert(staticFeetPlatforms, plat)
end)

createButton(movePage, "🧹 Удалить все поставленные платформы", function()
    for _, p in ipairs(staticFeetPlatforms) do
        if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p:Destroy() end) end
    end
    staticFeetPlatforms = {}
end)

-- 4. ВЕЧНЫЙ ДЕНЬ И УДАЛЕНИЕ ТУМАНА
createToggle(visualsPage, "☀️ Включить Вечный День (Full Bright)", function(Value)
    fullBrightEnabled = Value
    if fullBrightEnabled then
        task.spawn(function()
            while fullBrightEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
                Lighting.Brightness = 2
                Lighting.ClockTime = 14
                Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                task.wait(0.3)
            end
        end)
    else
        Lighting.Brightness = defaultBrightness
        Lighting.ClockTime = defaultClockTime
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = defaultGlobalShadows
        Lighting.Ambient = defaultAmbient
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = defaultOutdoorAmbient
    end
end)

createToggle(visualsPage, "🌫️ Полностью удалить туман (Remove Fog)", function(Value)
    removeFogEnabled = Value
    if removeFogEnabled then
        task.spawn(function()
            while removeFogEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
                Lighting.FogStart = 9e9
                Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
                for _, v in pairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do if v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("Sky") then v:Destroy() end end
                task.wait(0.3)
            end
        end)
    else
        Lighting.FogStart = defaultFogStart
        Lighting.FogEnd = defaultFogEnd
        if defaultAtmosphere and not Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Atmosphere") then defaultAtmosphere:Clone().Parent = Lighting end
        if defaultSky and not Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("Sky") then defaultSky:Clone().Parent = Lighting end
    end
end)

end
-- =========================================================
-- ----- КОНЕЦ ЧАСТИ 6.2 -----
-- =========================================================
-- =========================================================
-- ----- НАЧАЛО ЧАСТИ 7.1 -----
-- =========================================================
do

local targetPlayerInput = ""
local followBehindEnabled = false
local trollToggleApi = nil
local trollEnabled = false
local trollActive = false
local trollWasDiving = false
local trollGui = nil
local trollButton = nil

local function findExactOrPrefixPlayer(query)
    if not query or query == "" then return nil end
    local q = query:lower():gsub("^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
    if q == "" then return nil end

    -- Сначала ищем точное совпадение по нику или DisplayName
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player then
            if plr.Name:lower() == q or plr.DisplayName:lower() == q then
                return plr
            end
        end
    end

    -- Затем ищем по началу ника или DisplayName
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player then
            if plr.Name:lower():sub(1, #q) == q or plr.DisplayName:lower():sub(1, #q) == q then
                return plr
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local _, targetStatusLabel = createParagraph(playersPage, "🎯 Выбранная цель", "Никто не выбран (введите ник ниже)")

local selectedTargetPlayer = nil
local function setSelectedTarget(found)
    selectedTargetPlayer = found
    targetPlayerInput = found and found.Name or ""
    targetPlayerName = targetPlayerInput
    _G.PH_SelectedTargetPlayer = found
    if found then
        targetStatusLabel.Text = "Цель выбрана: " .. found.DisplayName .. " (@" .. found.Name .. ")"
    else
        targetStatusLabel.Text = "Никто не выбран"
    end
    if trollToggleApi then
        trollToggleApi.SetEnabled(found ~= nil)
    end
end

local function getSelectedTarget()
    if selectedTargetPlayer and selectedTargetPlayer.Parent then
        return selectedTargetPlayer
    end
    local found = findExactOrPrefixPlayer(targetPlayerInput ~= "" and targetPlayerInput or targetPlayerName)
    if found then
        setSelectedTarget(found)
    end
    return found
end

createInput(playersPage, "Ник игрока (первые буквы или полный)", "Введите ник...", function(Text)
    targetPlayerInput = Text
    local found = findExactOrPrefixPlayer(targetPlayerInput)
    setSelectedTarget(found)
    if not found and Text ~= "" then
        targetStatusLabel.Text = "Игрок '" .. Text .. "' не найден"
    end
end)

createButton(playersPage, "🌀 Телепортироваться к игроку", function()
    local target = getSelectedTarget()
    if target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        local targetHrp = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        local destCFrame = targetHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)

        if phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube then
            ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = destCFrame
        else
            local char = Player.Character
            if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = destCFrame
            end
        end
    end
end)

createToggle(playersPage, "👁 Наблюдать за игроком (Spectate)", function(Value)
    if Value then
        local target = getSelectedTarget()
        if target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            spectatingPlayer = target
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = target.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end
    else
        spectatingPlayer = nil
        if phantomRunning and ghostVisualClone then
            local camTarget = ghostVisualClone:FindFirstChild("Head") or ghostVisualClone.PrimaryPart or ghostSimulationCube
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = camTarget
        elseif Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        end
    end
end)

createToggle(playersPage, "🧲 Быть сзади выбранного игрока", function(Value)
    followBehindEnabled = Value
    if followBehindEnabled then
        local lockedTarget = getSelectedTarget()
        if not lockedTarget then
            followBehindEnabled = false
            return
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while followBehindEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
                if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                    -- Если в поле ввели новый ник, обновляем цель; иначе держимся строго за выбранным игроком
                    local currentQueryTarget = getSelectedTarget()
                    if currentQueryTarget then
                        lockedTarget = currentQueryTarget
                    end

                    if not lockedTarget or not lockedTarget.Parent then
                        followBehindEnabled = false
                        break
                    end

                    local tChar = lockedTarget.Character
                    local tHrp = tChar and tChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if tHrp then
                        local behindCFrame = tHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3.2)
                        if phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube then
                            ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = behindCFrame
                        else
                            local myChar = Player.Character
                            local myHrp = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if myHrp then
                                myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                myHrp.CFrame = behindCFrame
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end
        end)
    end
end)

-- Выбор цели нажатием по персонажу, пока включён тумблер.
local playerSelectToggleApi = nil
local playerSelectArmed = false
local trollSelectConnection = nil
local trollTouchConnection = nil

local function selectPlayerFromPart(targetPart)
    if not targetPart then return false end
    local ancestor = targetPart
    while ancestor and ancestor ~= workspace do
        if ancestor:IsA("Model") then
            local candidate = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(ancestor)
            if candidate and candidate ~= Player then
                setSelectedTarget(candidate)
                return true
            end
        end
        ancestor = ancestor.Parent
    end
    return false
end

local function raycastPlayerAtScreenPoint(point, useScreenPoint)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or not point then return nil end
    local ray
    if useScreenPoint then
        ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(point.X, point.Y)
    else
        ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(point.X, point.Y)
    end
    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {Player.Character}
    local hit = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 1000, params)
    return hit and hit.Instance or nil
end

local function finishPlayerSelection(targetPart)
    if not playerSelectArmed or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
    if selectPlayerFromPart(targetPart) then
        playerSelectArmed = false
        if playerSelectToggleApi then playerSelectToggleApi.Set(false) end
    end
end

playerSelectToggleApi = createToggle(playersPage, "🎯 Выбрать игрока нажатием", function(value)
    playerSelectArmed = value and true or false
end)

trollSelectConnection = PH_UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if not playerSelectArmed or gameProcessed or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        finishPlayerSelection(Mouse and Mouse.Target)
    end
end)

trollTouchConnection = PH_UserInputService.TouchTap:Connect(function(touchPositions, gameProcessed)
    if not playerSelectArmed or gameProcessed or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
    local point = touchPositions and touchPositions[1]
    if point then
        finishPlayerSelection(raycastPlayerAtScreenPoint(point, true))
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do task.wait(0.25) end
    playerSelectArmed = false
    if trollSelectConnection then trollSelectConnection:Disconnect() end
    if trollTouchConnection then trollTouchConnection:Disconnect() end
end)

local function setTrollButtonState(active)
    trollActive = active and true or false
    _G.PH_TrollActive = trollActive
    if trollButton then
        trollButton.Text = trollActive and "TROLL: ON" or "TROLL: OFF"
        trollButton.BackgroundColor3 = trollActive and Color3.fromRGB(50, 175, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(190, 55, 45)
    end
end

local function stopTroll()
    setTrollButtonState(false)
    if trollWasDiving then
        trollWasDiving = false
        if not _G.ScriptDestroyed and _G.PH_SetDiveActive then
            pcall(_G.PH_SetDiveActive, true)
        end
    end
end

local function startTroll()
    local target = getSelectedTarget()
    if not target or not target.Parent then
        warn("[Prompt_Hacker] Сначала выбери игрока.")
        setTrollButtonState(false)
        return
    end

    if followBehindEnabled then
        local followApi = _G.PH_Toggles and _G.PH_Toggles["🧲 Быть сзади выбранного игрока"]
        if followApi then followApi.Apply(false) end
    end

    trollWasDiving = (_G.PH_IsDiving and _G.PH_IsDiving()) == true
    if trollWasDiving and _G.PH_SetDiveActive then
        pcall(_G.PH_SetDiveActive, false)
    end

    setTrollButtonState(true)
    task.spawn(function()
        while trollActive and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                local currentTarget = selectedTargetPlayer or target
                if not currentTarget or not currentTarget.Parent then
                    stopTroll()
                    break
                end
                local targetCharacter = currentTarget.Character
                local targetRoot = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local myCharacter = Player.Character
                local myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if targetRoot and myRoot then
                    local offset = 3.2 + math.sin(os.clock() * 20) * 1.6
                    local destination = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, offset)
                    myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    myRoot.CFrame = destination
                end
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
end

trollToggleApi = createToggle(playersPage, "😈 Троллинг (кнопка на экране)", function(value)
    trollEnabled = value
    if trollEnabled then
        if not selectedTargetPlayer then
            trollEnabled = false
            if trollToggleApi then trollToggleApi.Set(false) end
            return
        end
        if not trollGui then
            trollGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            trollGui.Name = "PromptTrollControl"
            trollGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            local parent = nil
            pcall(function() parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
            local parentOk = false
            if parent then parentOk = pcall(function() trollGui.Parent = parent end) end
            if not parentOk then trollGui.Parent = Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
            trollButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
            trollButton.Name = "TrollToggle"
            trollButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 118, 0, 42)
            trollButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -140, 0.48, 0)
            trollButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 55, 45)
            trollButton.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            trollButton.Text = "TROLL: OFF"
            trollButton.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            trollButton.TextSize = 14
            trollButton.Active = true
            trollButton.Draggable = true
            trollButton.Parent = trollGui
            Instance.new("UICorner", trollButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            trollButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if trollActive then stopTroll() else startTroll() end
            end)
        end
    else
        stopTroll()
        if trollGui then pcall(function() trollGui:Destroy() end) end
        trollGui, trollButton = nil, nil
    end
end)
trollToggleApi.SetEnabled(false)

task.spawn(function()
    while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do task.wait(0.25) end
    stopTroll()
    if trollGui then pcall(function() trollGui:Destroy() end) end
    if trollSelectConnection then trollSelectConnection:Disconnect() end
    if trollTouchConnection then trollTouchConnection:Disconnect() end
end)

end

local function clearAllEsp()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local char = plr.Character
        if char then
            pcall(function()
                local hl = char:FindFirstChild("PromptPlayerHighlight")
                if hl then hl:Destroy() end
                for _, worldObject in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                    if worldObject.Name == "PromptPlayerHighlight"
                        and worldObject:IsA("Highlight") and worldObject.Adornee == char then
                        worldObject:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                for _, descendant in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant.Name == "PromptPlayerDotESP" then
                        descendant:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end

    task.spawn(function()
        while espEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local myChar = Player.Character

                or (myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))

            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= Player then
                    -- Ошибка одного нестандартного персонажа не должна останавливать весь ESP.
                    pcall(function()

                        if not char or not char.Parent then return end
                        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")

                        if not hl then
                            hl = Instance.new("Highlight")

                            hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                            hl.FillTransparency = 0.72

                            hl.OutlineTransparency = 0.15
                            hl.Adornee = char
                            hl.Parent = workspace
                        end

                        local bbg = anchor:FindFirstChild("PromptPlayerDotESP")

                            bbg = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

                            bbg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 46)
                            bbg.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.3, 0)
                            bbg.AlwaysOnTop = true
                            bbg.MaxDistance = 100000
                            bbg.Parent = anchor

                            local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                            nameLbl.Name = "NameText"
                            nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
                            nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                            nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                            nameLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.15
                            nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                            nameLbl.TextSize = 13
                            nameLbl.Parent = bbg

                            local dotLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                            dotLbl.Name = "DotText"
                            dotLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
                            dotLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16)
                            dotLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                            dotLbl.Text = "•"
                            dotLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                            dotLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                            dotLbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                            dotLbl.TextSize = 26
                            dotLbl.Parent = bbg
                        end

                        local dist = (myRoot and hrp)
                            and math.floor((myRoot.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                        local nameLbl = bbg:FindFirstChild("NameText")
                        if nameLbl then
                            nameLbl.Text = string.format("%s [%d HP] (%dм)",
                                plr.DisplayName, math.floor(hum.Health), dist)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
        clearAllEsp()
    end)
end)
