-- вот тут находится конец 1 блока (части 7.2)
-- Анти-хитбокс: рабочий переключатель расположен в блоке визуальных функций ниже.

-- Выбирает ближайшего живого игрока по дистанции в мире.
-- Ограничения по расстоянию и нахождению на экране нет.
local function findNearestEnemyByDistance()
    local myCharacter = Player.Character
    local myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return nil end

    local bestPlayer, bestDistance = nil, math.huge
    for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if candidate ~= Player then
            local character = candidate.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and root then
                local distance = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if distance < bestDistance then
                    bestDistance = distance
                    bestPlayer = candidate
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return bestPlayer
end

-- =========================================================
-- ----- НАЧАЛО АИМБОТА (CamLock) И ШИФТЛОКА -----
-- Шифтлок взят из версии 2.0.9 (с исправлениями), CamLock — из 3.5 (с исправлениями)
-- =========================================================

local camLockMiniBtn = nil
local camLockTarget = nil
local camLockGen = 0
local camLockMarker = nil

-- Сглаживание: 0 = жёсткий захват (точно в цель); 1..60 = плавно, но камера отстаёт от движущейся цели
local CAMLOCK_SMOOTH = 0

local function getAimPart(character)
    return character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

-- Возвращает (годится ли цель, часть для наведения)
local function isValidAimTarget(plr)
    if not plr or plr == Player then return false, nil end
    local ch = plr.Character
    if not ch or not ch.Parent then return false, nil end
    local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local part = getAimPart(ch)
    if hum and hum.Health > 0 and part then
        return true, part
    end
    return false, nil
end

-- Кнопка срабатывает только на тап/клик, а не когда её перетаскивают
local function bindTapButton(btn, callback)
    local pressPos = nil
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            pressPos = btn.AbsolutePosition
        end
    end)
    btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            if pressPos then
                local moved = (btn.AbsolutePosition - pressPos).Magnitude
                pressPos = nil
                if moved < 8 then callback() end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Ближайший живой игрок по дистанции в мире (от моего персонажа / фантома)
local function camLockPickNearest(cam)
    local myChar = Player.Character
    local myRoot = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or (myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
    local basePos = myRoot and myRoot.Position or cam.CFrame.Position

    local bestPlr, bestPart, bestDist = nil, nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local ok, part = isValidAimTarget(plr)
        if ok then
            local root = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or part
            local d = (root.Position - basePos).Magnitude
            if d < bestDist then
                bestPlr, bestPart, bestDist = plr, part, d
            end
        end
    end
    return bestPlr, bestPart, bestDist
end

createToggle(mainPage, "🎯 Мини-кнопка Auto-Aim камеры (CamLock)", function(Value)
    camLockGen = camLockGen + 1
    local myGen = camLockGen

    camLockMiniEnabled = Value
    camLockActive = false
    camLockTarget = nil
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("PromptCamLockStep") end)

    if camLockMiniBtn then
        pcall(function() camLockMiniBtn:Destroy() end)
        camLockMiniBtn = nil
    end
    if camLockMarker then
        pcall(function() camLockMarker:Destroy() end)
        camLockMarker = nil
    end
    if not camLockMiniEnabled then return end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "CamLockMiniBtn"
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 52)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.84, 0, 0.38, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 55, 45)
    btn.Text = "🎯"
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.TextSize = 24
    btn.Active = true
    btn.Draggable = true
    btn.ZIndex = 90
    btn.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    camLockMiniBtn = btn

    local currentTargetPlr = nil
    local currentTargetPart = nil

    local function setLooks(locked)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = locked and Color3.fromRGB(45, 175, 55) or Color3.fromRGB(210, 55, 45)
        btn.Text = locked and "🔒" or "🎯"
    end

    local function clearMarker()
        if camLockMarker then
            pcall(function() camLockMarker:Destroy() end)
            camLockMarker = nil
        end
    end

    -- Метка над целью: имя и дистанция, чтобы было видно, на кого именно наведён аим
    local lastMarkerTick = 0
    local function updateMarker(plr, part)
        if not camLockMarker or not camLockMarker.Parent then
            local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bb.Name = "PromptCamLockMarker"
            bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
            bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 26)
            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Name = "L"
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 60)
            lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            lbl.TextSize = 16
            lbl.Parent = bb
            bb.Parent = ScreenGui
            camLockMarker = bb
        end
        if camLockMarker.Adornee ~= part then camLockMarker.Adornee = part end
        if tick() - lastMarkerTick > 0.15 then
            lastMarkerTick = tick()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local lbl = camLockMarker:FindFirstChild("L")
            if lbl and cam then
                lbl.Text = "🎯 " .. plr.Name .. " [" .. tostring(math.floor((part.Position - cam.CFrame.Position).Magnitude)) .. " st]"
            end
        end
    end

    local function stopCamLock()
        clearMarker()
        camLockActive = false
        camLockTarget = nil
        currentTargetPlr = nil
        currentTargetPart = nil
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("PromptCamLockStep") end)
        if btn and btn.Parent then setLooks(false) end
    end

    local function startCamLock()
        camLockActive = true
        setLooks(true)

        RunService:BindToRenderStep("PromptCamLockStep", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function(dt)
            if _G.ScriptDestroyed or not camLockMiniEnabled or not btn or not btn.Parent then
                pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("PromptCamLockStep") end)
                return
            end
            if not camLockActive or _G.ScriptPaused then return end

            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then return end
            -- фрикам / наблюдение за другим игроком — камеру не трогаем
            if cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable or spectatingPlayer then return end

            -- каждый кадр берём САМОГО БЛИЖАЙШЕГО живого игрока;
            -- если цель умерла/вышла или рядом оказался другой, ближе — сразу переключаемся
            local nearPlr, nearPart = camLockPickNearest(cam)
            currentTargetPlr, currentTargetPart = nearPlr, nearPart

            if currentTargetPlr and currentTargetPart and currentTargetPart.Parent then
                camLockTarget = currentTargetPlr
                updateMarker(currentTargetPlr, currentTargetPart)

                local aimPos = currentTargetPart.Position
                local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
                local focusPos = cam.Focus.Position          -- точка, вокруг которой вращается камера (голова персонажа)
                local zoom = (camPos - focusPos).Magnitude    -- текущий отъезд камеры
                local toTarget = aimPos - focusPos

                local targetCF = nil
                if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.5 then
                    if zoom > 1 then
                        -- третье лицо: камера встаёт ПОЗАДИ персонажа на линии «персонаж -> цель»,
                        -- поэтому персонаж остаётся у центра экрана и не уезжает к краю
                        targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(focusPos - toTarget.Unit * zoom, aimPos)
                    elseif (aimPos - camPos).Magnitude > 0.5 then
                        -- от первого лица просто поворачиваем камеру
                        targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(camPos, aimPos)
                    end
                end

                if targetCF then
                    if CAMLOCK_SMOOTH > 0 then
                        local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-CAMLOCK_SMOOTH * (dt or 1 / 60))
                        cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(targetCF, alpha)
                    else
                        cam.CFrame = targetCF
                    end
                end
            else
                camLockTarget = nil
                clearMarker()
            end
        end)
    end

    local lastTap = 0
    bindTapButton(btn, function()
        if tick() - lastTap < 0.3 then return end
        lastTap = tick()
        if camLockActive then stopCamLock() else startCamLock() end
    end)

    task.spawn(function()
        while myGen == camLockGen and camLockMiniEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        if myGen == camLockGen then stopCamLock() end
    end)
end)

-- вот тут находится конец 2 блока (части 7.2)
local shiftLockBtn = nil
local crosshairFrame = nil
local shiftLockActive = false
local shiftLockGen = 0

-- Состояние хуков живёт в _G, чтобы при повторном запуске скрипта хуки не ставились второй раз
local SL = _G.PH_ShiftLockState
if not SL then
    SL = { hooked = false }
    _G.PH_ShiftLockState = SL
end
SL.isOn = function()
    return shiftLockActive and not _G.ScriptPaused and not _G.ScriptDestroyed
end
SL.getIgnore = function()
    local ignore = {}
    if Player.Character then table.insert(ignore, Player.Character) end
    if ghostVisualClone then table.insert(ignore, ghostVisualClone) end
    if ghostSimulationCube then table.insert(ignore, ghostSimulationCube) end
    return ignore
end

-- Пока шифтлок включён, "мышь" для игры = центр экрана (выстрелы/скиллы летят в прицел)
local function installShiftLockHooks()
    if SL.hooked then return end
    if not (hookmetamethod and checkcaller and getnamecallmethod) then
        warn("[Шифтлок] Экзекутор не поддерживает hookmetamethod: выстрелы в центр недоступны")
        return
    end

    local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local mouse = Player:GetMouse()
    local wrap = newcclosure or function(f) return f end

    local function centerPoint()
        local vs = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
        return Vector2.new(vs.X * 0.5, vs.Y * 0.5)
    end

    local function centerRay()
        local c = centerPoint()
        return workspace.CurrentCamera:ViewportPointToRay(c.X, c.Y)
    end

    local function centerRaycast()
        local ray = centerRay()
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = SL.getIgnore()
        local result = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 1000, params)
        return ray, result
    end

    local okIndex = pcall(function()
        local oldIndex
        oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", wrap(function(self, key)
            if self == mouse and not checkcaller() and SL.isOn() then
                if key == "Hit" then
                    local ray, result = centerRaycast()
                    return CFrame.new(result and result.Position or (ray.Origin + ray.Direction * 1000))
                elseif key == "Target" then
                    local _, result = centerRaycast()
                    return result and result.Instance or nil
                elseif key == "X" then
                    return centerPoint().X - GuiService:GetGuiInset().X
                elseif key == "Y" then
                    return centerPoint().Y - GuiService:GetGuiInset().Y
                elseif key == "UnitRay" then
                    return centerRay()
                end
            end
            return oldIndex(self, key)
        end))
    end)

    local okNamecall = pcall(function()
        local oldNamecall
        oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", wrap(function(self, ...)
            if self == UIS and not checkcaller() and SL.isOn() and getnamecallmethod() == "GetMouseLocation" then
                return centerPoint()
            end
            return oldNamecall(self, ...)
        end))
    end)

    SL.hooked = okIndex and okNamecall
    if not SL.hooked then
        warn("[Шифтлок] Не удалось поставить хуки мыши")
    end
end

createToggle(visualsPage, "🔒 Мобильный Шифтлок с прицелом (Точный центр)", function(Value)
    shiftLockMiniEnabled = Value
    shiftLockActive = false
    shiftLockGen = shiftLockGen + 1
    local myGen = shiftLockGen

    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
    UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default

    if shiftLockBtn then pcall(function() shiftLockBtn:Destroy() end) shiftLockBtn = nil end
    if crosshairFrame then pcall(function() crosshairFrame:Destroy() end) crosshairFrame = nil end

    if not shiftLockMiniEnabled then return end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    installShiftLockHooks()

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "ShiftLockMiniBtn"
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 48)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.84, 0, 0.51, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 55, 45)
    btn.Text = "🔒"
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.TextSize = 22
    btn.Active = true
    btn.Draggable = true
    btn.ZIndex = 65
    btn.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
    stroke.Thickness = 2.5
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 220, 255)

    shiftLockBtn = btn

    local ch = Instance.new("Frame")
    ch.Name = "PromptCrosshair"
    ch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    ch.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    ch.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    ch.Visible = false
    ch.ZIndex = 75
    ch.Parent = ScreenGui

    local hLine = Instance.new("Frame", ch)
    hLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
    hLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -1)
    hLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)
    hLine.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local vLine = Instance.new("Frame", ch)
    vLine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 1, 0)
    vLine.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -1, 0, 0)
    vLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)
    vLine.BorderSizePixel = 0

    local centerDot = Instance.new("Frame", ch)
    centerDot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
    centerDot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    centerDot.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    centerDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
    centerDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", centerDot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    crosshairFrame = ch

    local lastTap = 0
    local function toggleShift()
        if tick() - lastTap < 0.25 then return end
        lastTap = tick()
        shiftLockActive = not shiftLockActive
        ch.Visible = shiftLockActive
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = shiftLockActive and Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 70) or Color3.fromRGB(210, 55, 45)
        if not shiftLockActive then
            UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
        end
    end

    bindTapButton(btn, toggleShift)

    task.spawn(function()
        local appliedHum = nil   -- гуманоид, которому мы выставили CameraOffset
        local wasActive = false

        -- откат делаем ОДИН раз при выключении, а не каждый кадр,
        -- чтобы не ломать AutoRotate/мышь у других функций и самой игры
        local function restore()
            UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
            local c = Player.Character
            local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then h.AutoRotate = true end
            if appliedHum and appliedHum.Parent then
                appliedHum.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            appliedHum = nil
        end

        while myGen == shiftLockGen and shiftLockMiniEnabled and btn and btn.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            local char = Player.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            local active = shiftLockActive and not _G.ScriptPaused and cam and hum and hrp and hum.Health > 0

            if active then
                wasActive = true
                ch.Visible = true

                -- центр прицела с учётом отступа верхней панели
                local vs = cam.ViewportSize
                local cx, cy = vs.X * 0.5, vs.Y * 0.5
                if not ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset then
                    local inset = GuiService:GetGuiInset()
                    cx = cx - inset.X
                    cy = cy - inset.Y
                end
                ch.Position = UDim2.new(0, cx, 0, cy)

                if UIS.MouseBehavior ~= Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
                    UIS.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
                end

                -- смещение камеры ставим тому гуманоиду, за которым реально следит камера
                -- (в режиме фантома это не настоящий персонаж)
                local subject = cam.CameraSubject
                local camHum = (subject and subject:IsA("Humanoid")) and subject or hum
                if appliedHum and appliedHum ~= camHum and appliedHum.Parent then
                    appliedHum.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                end
                camHum.CameraOffset = Vector3.new(1.75, 0.35, 0)
                appliedHum = camHum

                if hum.Sit then
                    -- в кресле/транспорте персонажа не крутим
                    hum.AutoRotate = true
                else
                    hum.AutoRotate = false
                    local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
                    local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
                    if flatLook.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                        local dir = flatLook.Unit
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + dir)
                        if phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube then
                            ghostSimulationCube.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(ghostSimulationCube.Position, ghostSimulationCube.Position + dir)
                        end
                    end
                end
            else
                ch.Visible = false
                if wasActive then
                    wasActive = false
                    restore()
                end
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end

        if wasActive or appliedHum then
            restore()
        end
    end)
end)

-- =========================================================
-- ----- КОНЕЦ ФАЙЛА (ЗАГЛУШКА) -----
-- =========================================================
print("PROMPT_HACKER: Скрипт успешно загружен!")
-- =========================================================
-- ----- КОНЕЦ ЧАСТИ 7.2 -----
-- =========================================================
end -- закрывает do, открытый в начале части 7.2
-- =========================================================
-- ----- НАЧАЛО ЧАСТИ 7.3 -----
-- =========================================================
do

local hiddenHitboxesEnabled = false
local cdTrackerEnabled = false

-- =========================================================
-- ОБЩИЙ КЛАССИФИКАТОР ОПАСНЫХ ДЕТАЛЕЙ (его используют и показ хитбоксов, и подушка)
-- Категории: lethal (смерть/лава/ловушки), attack (урон/хитбоксы атак),
--            control (торнадо/притяжение), trigger (невидимые контактные зоны)
-- =========================================================
local hazardLearnedParts = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"}) -- детали, на которых вы реально получили урон
local hazardLearnedNames = {}                               -- имена таких деталей (кроме общих вроде "Part")
local hazardCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local hitboxCandidates = {}                                 -- [part] = true, кандидаты для показа

local HAZARD_OWN_PREFIXES = {
    "Prompt", "Phantom_", "Bunker_", "ZoneMarker_", "ZoneBoxVisual_",
    "Cushion_", "BendySurfacePuppet"
}
local LETHAL_WORDS = {
    "kill", "instantkill", "lethal", "deadly", "death", "killbrick", "void",
    "lava", "magma", "acid", "poison", "spike", "fire", "burn", "hazard",
    "danger", "trap", "onehit", "oneshot"
}
local WALL_ATTACK_WORDS = {"hitbox", "damage", "dmg", "hurt"}
local ATTACK_WORDS = {"hitbox", "damage", "dmg", "hurt", "slash", "blast", "shock", "wave", "aura", "explosion",
    "attack", "projectile", "bullet", "laser", "bomb", "missile", "beam", "strike", "slam", "swing", "knockback", "stun"}
local CONTROL_WORDS = {"tornado", "hurricane", "vortex", "pull", "wind"}
local HAZARD_GENERIC_NAMES = {
    part = true, meshpart = true, union = true, unionoperation = true, wedge = true,
    floor = true, ground = true, wall = true, base = true, baseplate = true,
    platform = true, block = true, brick = true, plate = true, model = true,
    handle = true, terrain = true, spawn = true, spawnlocation = true
}

local function hazardSanitize(text)
    -- "skill" содержит "kill", "avoid" содержит "void", "window" содержит "wind" — это не опасные зоны
    text = text:lower()
    text = (text:gsub("skill", ""))
    text = (text:gsub("avoid", ""))
    text = (text:gsub("window", ""))
    return text
end

local function hazardHasWord(text, list)
    if text == "" then return false end
    for _, word in ipairs(list) do
        if text:find(word, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function getHazardHumanoidModel(obj)
    local model = obj:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    while model do
        if model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return model end
        model = model:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    end
    return nil
end

-- Детали самого скрипта (PromptHitbox расширителя, платформы, маркеры, стенки подушки) не трогаем
local function isHazardOwnObject(obj)
    local roots = {godAirPlatform, godGroundCube, godVisualClone, ghostSimulationCube, ghostVisualClone, zoneBoxVisual, markerA, markerB}
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local root = roots[i]
        if root and (obj == root or obj:IsDescendantOf(root)) then return true end
    end
    local cur = obj
    while cur and cur ~= workspace do
        local n = cur.Name
        for _, prefix in ipairs(HAZARD_OWN_PREFIXES) do
            if n:sub(1, #prefix) == prefix then return true end
        end
        cur = cur.Parent
    end
    return false
end

-- Лава "на вид": CrackedLava или красно-оранжевый цвет
local function looksLikeLava(obj)
    if obj.Material == Enum.Material.CrackedLava then return true end
    local h, s, v = obj.Color:ToHSV()
    return (h <= 0.1 or h >= 0.97) and s >= 0.65 and v >= 0.55
end

local function computeHazard(obj)
    if not obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Terrain") then return nil, false end
    if obj.Name == "Baseplate" or not obj:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then return nil, false end
    if Player.Character and obj:IsDescendantOf(Player.Character) then return nil, false end
    if isHazardOwnObject(obj) then return nil, false end

    local size = obj.Size
    local maxAxis = math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z)
    local rawName = obj.Name:lower()
    local name = hazardSanitize(obj.Name)
    local humModel = getHazardHumanoidModel(obj)

    -- Внутри персонажей/NPC имя модели — это ник/имя босса, поэтому смотрим только на имя самой детали
    local parentName, grandName, parentRaw = "", "", ""
    if not humModel and obj.Parent then
        parentRaw = obj.Parent.Name:lower()
        parentName = hazardSanitize(obj.Parent.Name)
        if obj.Parent.Parent then grandName = hazardSanitize(obj.Parent.Parent.Name) end
    end

    local touch = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") ~= nil
    local cat, wall = nil, false

    if hazardLearnedNames[rawName] then
        cat, wall = "lethal", true
    elseif hazardHasWord(name, LETHAL_WORDS) or hazardHasWord(parentName, LETHAL_WORDS) or hazardHasWord(grandName, LETHAL_WORDS) then
        cat, wall = "lethal", true
    elseif hazardHasWord(name, WALL_ATTACK_WORDS) or hazardHasWord(parentName, WALL_ATTACK_WORDS) or hazardHasWord(grandName, WALL_ATTACK_WORDS) then
        cat, wall = "attack", true
    end

    -- Атрибуты, которыми разработчики помечают опасные детали
    if not cat then
        for attributeName, attributeValue in pairs(obj:GetAttributes()) do
            local key = attributeName:lower()
            local isKill = key:find("kill", 1, true) or key:find("lethal", 1, true)
                or key:find("oneshot", 1, true) or key:find("instant", 1, true)
            if isKill or key:find("damage", 1, true) or key:find("dmg", 1, true) then
                if attributeValue == true or (type(attributeValue) == "number" and attributeValue > 0) then
                    cat, wall = (isKill and "lethal" or "attack"), true
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end

    -- Лава без понятного названия: большая красно-оранжевая деталь с контактным триггером или материал CrackedLava
    if not cat and ((touch and maxAxis >= 4 and obj.Transparency < 0.9 and looksLikeLava(obj))
        or obj.Material == Enum.Material.CrackedLava) then
        cat, wall = "lethal", true
    end

    -- Только для показа (стенки вокруг них не строятся)
    if not cat then
        if hazardHasWord(name, ATTACK_WORDS) then
            cat = "attack"
        elseif hazardHasWord(name, CONTROL_WORDS) then
            cat = "control"
        elseif not humModel and not obj.CanCollide and (parentRaw:find("effect", 1, true) or parentRaw:find("skill", 1, true)
            or parentRaw:find("ability", 1, true) or parentRaw:find("projectile", 1, true)
            or parentRaw:find("attack", 1, true) or parentRaw:find("vfx", 1, true)) then
            cat = "attack"
        elseif not humModel and touch and not obj.CanCollide then
            cat = "trigger"
        elseif not humModel and not obj.CanCollide and obj.Transparency >= 0.9 and maxAxis >= 1 then
            cat = "trigger" -- любая невидимая неколлизионная деталь (скрытые зоны)
        end
    end

    if wall and maxAxis > 2040 then wall = false end -- стенка = размер + 2, лимит Part 2048
    return cat, wall
end

local function classifyHazard(obj, now)
    if hazardLearnedParts[obj] then return "lethal", true end
    now = now or tick()
    local cached = hazardCache[obj]
    if cached and now - cached.t < 2 then return cached.cat, cached.wall end
    local cat, wall = computeHazard(obj)
    hazardCache[obj] = {t = now, cat = cat, wall = wall}
    return cat, wall
end

-- =========================================================
-- ПОКАЗ ХИТБОКСОВ (цветные категории, подписи с расстоянием, без лимита Highlight=31)
-- =========================================================
local HAZARD_STYLE = {
    lethal  = {color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 45, 45),  icon = "☠", fill = 0.72},
    attack  = {color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 30), icon = "⚔", fill = 0.78},
    control = {color = Color3.fromRGB(175, 95, 255), icon = "🌀", fill = 0.80},
    trigger = {color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 70), icon = "◇", fill = 0.88}
}
local HITBOX_RANGE = 400        -- дальность показа (студы), меняется полем на вкладке «Визуалы»; 0 = без лимита
local HITBOX_MAX_VISUALS = 800   -- сколько ближайших хитбоксов рисовать одновременно (меньше = меньше лагов)
local HITBOX_LABEL_RANGE = 150   -- подписи (имя + дистанция) только у ближайших, чтобы не засорять экран

local hitboxFolder = nil
local hitboxVisuals = {}
local hitboxConns = {}
local hitboxSession = 0

local function destroyHitboxVisual(part)
    local v = hitboxVisuals[part]
    if not v then return end
    if v.box then v.box:Destroy() end
    if v.outline then v.outline:Destroy() end
    if v.gui then v.gui:Destroy() end
    hitboxVisuals[part] = nil
end

local function clearHitboxVisuals()
    for part in pairs(hitboxVisuals) do destroyHitboxVisual(part) end
    table.clear(hitboxVisuals)
    if hitboxFolder then hitboxFolder:Destroy() hitboxFolder = nil end
end

local function createHitboxVisual(part, cat)
    local style = HAZARD_STYLE[cat]
    local s = part.Size
    local big = math.max(s.X, s.Y, s.Z) > 60

    local box = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
    box.Name = "PromptHitboxFill"
    box.Adornee = part
    box.Size = s
    box.Color3 = style.color
    box.Transparency = big and 0.93 or style.fill
    box.AlwaysOnTop = true
    box.ZIndex = 2
    box.Parent = hitboxFolder

    local outline = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
    outline.Name = "PromptHitboxOutline"
    outline.Adornee = part
    outline.Color3 = style.color
    outline.LineThickness = 0.04
    outline.SurfaceTransparency = 1
    outline.Parent = hitboxFolder

    local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    gui.Name = "PromptHitboxInfo"
    gui.Adornee = part
    gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
    gui.LightInfluence = 0
    gui.MaxDistance = math.huge
    gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 30)
    gui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, math.min(s.Y * 0.5, 15) + 2, 0)
    gui.Enabled = (cat ~= "trigger")
    gui.Parent = hitboxFolder

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.TextColor3 = style.color
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 13
    label.Text = ""
    label.Parent = gui

    return {cat = cat, box = box, outline = outline, gui = gui, label = label, text = ""}
end

local function considerHitboxPart(part, now)
    if hitboxCandidates[part] then return end
    local cat = classifyHazard(part, now)
    if cat then hitboxCandidates[part] = true end
end

local function updateHitboxes()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam or not hitboxFolder then return end
    if not hitboxFolder.Parent then
        -- игра очистила workspace — пересоздаём папку и визуалы
        table.clear(hitboxVisuals)
        hitboxFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
        hitboxFolder.Name = "PromptHitboxFolder"
        hitboxFolder.Parent = workspace
    end
    local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
    local now = tick()
    local list = {}

    for part in pairs(hitboxCandidates) do
        if not part.Parent or not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
            hitboxCandidates[part] = nil
        else
            local cat = classifyHazard(part, now)
            if cat then
                local s = part.Size
                local d = (part.Position - camPos).Magnitude
                local dEdge = math.max(0, d - math.max(s.X, s.Y, s.Z) * 0.5)
                if HITBOX_RANGE <= 0 or dEdge <= HITBOX_RANGE then
                    list[#list + 1] = {part = part, cat = cat, d = d, de = dEdge}
                end
            else
                hitboxCandidates[part] = nil -- перестала подходить; вернётся при следующем пересканировании
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.de < b.de end)

    local wanted = {}
    for i = 1, math.min(#list, HITBOX_MAX_VISUALS) do
        local e = list[i]
        local part = e.part
        wanted[part] = true

        local v = hitboxVisuals[part]
        if v and (v.cat ~= e.cat or not v.box.Parent) then
            destroyHitboxVisual(part)
            v = nil
        end
        if not v then
            v = createHitboxVisual(part, e.cat)
            hitboxVisuals[part] = v
        end

        local s = part.Size
        if v.box.Size ~= s then v.box.Size = s end -- растущие хитбоксы подстраиваются
        local nm = part.Name
        if #nm > 18 then nm = nm:sub(1, 17) .. "…" end
        local text = HAZARD_STYLE[e.cat].icon .. " " .. nm .. "  " .. math.floor(e.d)
        if v.text ~= text then
            v.text = text
            v.label.Text = text
        end
        -- подпись показываем только вблизи; триггеры (◇) без подписей
        local wantLabel = (e.cat ~= "trigger") and (e.d <= HITBOX_LABEL_RANGE)
        if v.gui.Enabled ~= wantLabel then v.gui.Enabled = wantLabel end
    end

    for part in pairs(hitboxVisuals) do
        if not wanted[part] then destroyHitboxVisual(part) end
    end
end

createInput(visualsPage, "Дальность показа хитбоксов (0 = без лимита)", "400", function(Text)
    local n = tonumber(Text)
    if n and n >= 0 then HITBOX_RANGE = math.min(n, 100000) end
end)

createToggle(visualsPage, "🔮 Скрытые хитбоксы (☠ смерть  ⚔ урон  🌀 притяжение  ◇ триггер)", function(Value)
    hiddenHitboxesEnabled = Value
    hitboxSession = hitboxSession + 1
    local session = hitboxSession

    for _, c in ipairs(hitboxConns) do
        if c and c.Connected then c:Disconnect() end
    end
    table.clear(hitboxConns)

    if not Value then
        clearHitboxVisuals()
        table.clear(hitboxCandidates)
        return
    end

    if hitboxFolder then hitboxFolder:Destroy() end
    hitboxFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    hitboxFolder.Name = "PromptHitboxFolder"
    hitboxFolder.Parent = workspace

    table.insert(hitboxConns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return end
        task.defer(function()
            if hiddenHitboxesEnabled and hitboxSession == session and obj.Parent then
                considerHitboxPart(obj, tick())
            end
        end)
    end))

    task.spawn(function()
        local lastRescan = 0
        while hiddenHitboxesEnabled and hitboxSession == session and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local t = tick()
            if t - lastRescan > 4 then
                lastRescan = t
                local n = 0
                for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if not hiddenHitboxesEnabled or hitboxSession ~= session or _G.ScriptDestroyed then break end
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then considerHitboxPart(obj, t) end
                    n = n + 1
                    if n % 300 == 0 then task.wait() end -- без фриза на больших картах
                end
            end
            updateHitboxes()
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        if hitboxSession == session then clearHitboxVisuals() end -- закрытие скрипта
    end)
end)

-- =========================================================
-- 🛡️ АНТИ-ХИТБОКС ПОДУШКА (HITBOX DEFLECTOR)
-- Прозрачные локальные коллизионные стенки вокруг найденных хитбоксов и лавы. Серверный урон они гарантированно отключить не могут.
-- Самообучение: если вы получили урон, детали, на которых вы стояли/которых касались (контактные или "лавовые"), запоминаются и обносятся стенкой.
-- =========================================================
local cushionEnabled = false
local cushionSession = 0
local cushionConnections = {}
local cushionVisuals = {}
local cushionCount = 0

local CUSHION_PAD = 6 -- по 3 стада с каждой стороны: тело/руки/ноги не дотягиваются до хитбокса
local CUSHION_MAX_PART_SIZE = 2040
local CUSHION_MAX_WALLS = 500

local function disconnectCushionConnections()
    for _, connection in ipairs(cushionConnections) do
        if connection and connection.Connected then connection:Disconnect() end
    end
    table.clear(cushionConnections)
end

local function clearCushionVisuals()
    for _, visual in pairs(cushionVisuals) do
        if visual and visual.Parent then visual:Destroy() end
    end
    table.clear(cushionVisuals)
    cushionCount = 0
end

local function isCushionHitbox(obj)
    if not obj or not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
    local _, wall = classifyHazard(obj)
    return wall == true
end

local function getCushionWallSize(part)
    local s = part.Size
    return Vector3.new(s.X + CUSHION_PAD, s.Y + CUSHION_PAD, s.Z + CUSHION_PAD)
end

local function addCushionVisual(part)
    if not cushionEnabled or not isCushionHitbox(part) then return end
    if cushionVisuals[part] and cushionVisuals[part].Parent then return end
    local s = part.Size
    if math.max(s.X, s.Y, s.Z) > CUSHION_MAX_PART_SIZE then return end
    if cushionCount >= CUSHION_MAX_WALLS and not cushionVisuals[part] then return end

    local visual = Instance.new("Part")
    visual.Name = "Cushion_Shield"
    -- Чуть больший прозрачный блок работает как стенка между игроком и хитбоксом.
    visual.Size = getCushionWallSize(part)
    visual.CFrame = part.CFrame
    visual.Anchored = true
    visual.CanCollide = true
    visual.CanTouch = false
    visual.CanQuery = true -- иначе Humanoid не видит стенку как пол: нельзя прыгать/ходить по ней над лавой
    visual.CastShadow = false
    visual.Massless = true
    visual.Transparency = 1
    visual.Parent = workspace

    if not cushionVisuals[part] then cushionCount = cushionCount + 1 end
    cushionVisuals[part] = visual
end

local function disableCushion()
    cushionEnabled = false
    cushionSession = cushionSession + 1
    disconnectCushionConnections()
    clearCushionVisuals()
end

-- Стенка ВСЕГДА твёрдая (как деталь режима стройки). Если хитбокс появился прямо на вас и центр персонажа
-- оказался внутри стенки, вас без импульсов и "вылетов" переносит на ближайшую внешнюю грань (верх — приоритет, можно стоять).
local function isLocalRootInsideWall(root, wall)
    if not root or not root.Parent then return false end
    local rel = wall.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position)
    local half = wall.Size * 0.5
    return math.abs(rel.X) < half.X and math.abs(rel.Y) < half.Y and math.abs(rel.Z) < half.Z
end

local function ejectRootFromWall(root, wall)
    local rel = wall.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position)
    local half = wall.Size * 0.5
    local sideMargin, topMargin = 2.6, 3.4
    local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    local function try(name, dist)
        if dist < bestDist then best, bestDist = name, dist end
    end
    try("px", half.X - rel.X)
    try("nx", half.X + rel.X)
    try("pz", half.Z - rel.Z)
    try("nz", half.Z + rel.Z)
    try("py", (half.Y - rel.Y) * 0.6) -- верх выгоднее: можно встать на стенку
    try("ny", half.Y + rel.Y)
    local r = Vector3.new(rel.X, rel.Y, rel.Z)
    if best == "px" then r = Vector3.new(half.X + sideMargin, rel.Y, rel.Z)
    elseif best == "nx" then r = Vector3.new(-half.X - sideMargin, rel.Y, rel.Z)
    elseif best == "pz" then r = Vector3.new(rel.X, rel.Y, half.Z + sideMargin)
    elseif best == "nz" then r = Vector3.new(rel.X, rel.Y, -half.Z - sideMargin)
    elseif best == "py" then r = Vector3.new(rel.X, half.Y + topMargin, rel.Z)
    else r = Vector3.new(rel.X, -half.Y - topMargin, rel.Z) end
    local worldPos = wall.CFrame:PointToWorldSpace(r)
    local rot = root.CFrame - root.CFrame.Position
    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(worldPos) * rot
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end

-- Самообучение: запоминаем то, что рядом с персонажем в момент урона
local lastCushionNotify = 0
local function learnTouchingHazards(char)
    if not char or not char.Parent then return end
    local ok, cf, size = pcall(function() return char:GetBoundingBox() end)
    if not ok or not cf then return end

    local params = OverlapParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
    local parts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(cf, size + Vector3.new(3, 3, 3), params)

    local learnedNames = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(parts) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") and not p:IsA("Terrain") and p.Name ~= "Baseplate"
            and not hazardLearnedParts[p] and not isHazardOwnObject(p) and not getHazardHumanoidModel(p) then
            local touch = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") ~= nil
            local lavaish = looksLikeLava(p)
            if touch or lavaish then
                hazardLearnedParts[p] = true
                hazardCache[p] = nil
                hitboxCandidates[p] = true
                local lname = p.Name:lower()
                local s = p.Size
                local m = math.max(s.X, s.Y, s.Z)
                if #lname >= 4 and not HAZARD_GENERIC_NAMES[lname] and not lname:match("^%d+$")
                    and (lavaish or m >= 6) then
                    hazardLearnedNames[lname] = true
                end
                addCushionVisual(p)
                table.insert(learnedNames, p.Name)
            end
        end
    end

    if #learnedNames > 0 and tick() - lastCushionNotify > 2 then
        lastCushionNotify = tick()
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "🛡 Подушка",
                Text = "Запомнил опасное: " .. table.concat(learnedNames, ", ", 1, math.min(#learnedNames, 3)),
                Duration = 4
            })
        end)
    end
end

local function hookCushionHumanoid(char, session)
    task.spawn(function()
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not hum or not cushionEnabled or cushionSession ~= session then return end
        local last = hum.Health
        table.insert(cushionConnections, hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(h)
            local drop = last - h
            last = h
            if drop > 0 and cushionEnabled and cushionSession == session then
                learnTouchingHazards(char)
            end
        end))
    end)
end

createToggle(visualsPage, "🛡 Анти-Хитбокс Подушка (Стены вокруг хитбоксов и лавы)", function(value)
    if not value then
        disableCushion()
        return
    end
    if cushionEnabled then return end
    cushionEnabled = true
    cushionSession = cushionSession + 1
    local session = cushionSession

    -- Сначала подписка на новые детали, потом первичный скан — ничего не теряется (дубли отсекает addCushionVisual)
    table.insert(cushionConnections, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if cushionEnabled and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            task.defer(function()
                if cushionEnabled and cushionSession == session and obj and obj.Parent then addCushionVisual(obj) end
            end)
        end
    end))

    task.spawn(function()
        local n = 0
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if not cushionEnabled or cushionSession ~= session or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then addCushionVisual(obj) end
            n = n + 1
            if n % 400 == 0 then task.wait() end -- без фриза на больших картах
        end
    end)

    if Player.Character then hookCushionHumanoid(Player.Character, session) end
    table.insert(cushionConnections, Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        hookCushionHumanoid(c, session)
    end))

    table.insert(cushionConnections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not cushionEnabled or _G.ScriptDestroyed then
            disableCushion()
            return
        end

        local char = Player.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        -- в режиме бога/инвиза реальное тело — куб-симуляция, его и защищаем
        local ctrlRoot = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) or (godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube) or root
        local toRebuild = nil

        for part, wall in pairs(cushionVisuals) do
            if not part or not part.Parent or not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                if wall and wall.Parent then wall:Destroy() end
                cushionVisuals[part] = nil
                cushionCount = cushionCount - 1
            elseif not wall or not wall.Parent then
                -- стенку удалила игра/очистка карты — пересоздаём
                cushionVisuals[part] = nil
                cushionCount = cushionCount - 1
                toRebuild = toRebuild or {}
                table.insert(toRebuild, part)
            else
                -- Стенка следует за движущимся хитбоксом/растущей лавой; никаких импульсов игроку не применяется.
                local newSize = getCushionWallSize(part)
                if wall.Size ~= newSize then wall.Size = newSize end
                if wall.CFrame ~= part.CFrame then wall.CFrame = part.CFrame end

                if not wall.CanCollide then wall.CanCollide = true end
                if ctrlRoot and isLocalRootInsideWall(ctrlRoot, wall) then
                    ejectRootFromWall(ctrlRoot, wall)
                end
            end
        end

        if toRebuild then
            for _, part in ipairs(toRebuild) do addCushionVisual(part) end
        end
    end))
end)

-- вот тут находится конец 1 блока (части 7.3)
local playerCooldownData = {}

local function registerPlayerTool(pData, toolObj)
    if not toolObj or not toolObj:IsA("Tool") then return end
    local toolName = toolObj.Name
    local slotIdx = pData.NameToSlot[toolName]

    if not slotIdx then
        if #pData.SlotNames < 9 then
            table.insert(pData.SlotNames, toolName)
            slotIdx = #pData.SlotNames
            pData.NameToSlot[toolName] = slotIdx
        else
            slotIdx = 9
        end
    end

    if not pData.HookedTools[toolObj] then
        pData.HookedTools[toolObj] = true
        table.insert(pData.Connections, toolObj.Activated:Connect(function()
            local cd = toolObj:GetAttribute("Cooldown") or toolObj:GetAttribute("CD") or 4
            if type(cd) ~= "number" or cd <= 0 then cd = 4 end
            pData.Cooldowns[slotIdx] = tick() + cd
        end))
    end
end

local function setupPlayerCdTracker(plr, char, hum)
    local pData = {
        Char = char,
        SlotNames = {},
        NameToSlot = {},
        Cooldowns = {},
        HookedTools = {},
        LastAnimTime = 0,
        Connections = {}
    }
    playerCooldownData[plr] = pData

    table.insert(pData.Connections, char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child:IsA("Tool") then registerPlayerTool(pData, child) end
    end))

    local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if animator then
        table.insert(pData.Connections, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
            if tick() - pData.LastAnimTime < 0.45 then return end
            local animName = track.Animation and track.Animation.Name:lower() or ""
            if animName:find("walk") or animName:find("run") or animName:find("idle") or animName:find("jump") or animName:find("fall") then
                return
            end
            pData.LastAnimTime = tick()

            local heldTool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            if heldTool then
                registerPlayerTool(pData, heldTool)
                local sIdx = pData.NameToSlot[heldTool.Name]
                if sIdx and (not pData.Cooldowns[sIdx] or pData.Cooldowns[sIdx] < tick()) then
                    local cd = heldTool:GetAttribute("Cooldown") or heldTool:GetAttribute("CD") or 4
                    if type(cd) ~= "number" or cd <= 0 then cd = 4 end
                    pData.Cooldowns[sIdx] = tick() + cd
                end
            end
        end))
    end

    return pData
end

local function renderPlayerCdSlots(head, pData, equippedTool)
    local totalSlots = #pData.SlotNames
    local bbg = head:FindFirstChild("PromptCDTracker")
    
    if totalSlots == 0 then
        if bbg then bbg.Enabled = false end
        return
    end

    if not bbg then
        bbg = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bbg.Name = "PromptCDTracker"
        bbg.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bbg.MaxDistance = 400
        bbg.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.6, 0)
        bbg.Parent = head

        local container = Instance.new("Frame", bbg)
        container.Name = "SlotsRow"
        container.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", container)
        layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
        layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
    end
    
    bbg.Enabled = true
    bbg.Size = UDim2.new(0, totalSlots * 34 + 12, 0, 34)
    local container = bbg:FindFirstChild("SlotsRow")
    
    -- Пересоздаем слоты только если изменилось их количество
    local currentFrames = 0
    for _, ch in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("Frame") then currentFrames = currentFrames + 1 end
    end

    if currentFrames ~= totalSlots then
        for _, ch in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end
        end

        for i = 1, totalSlots do
            local slotFrame = Instance.new("Frame", container)
            slotFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
            Instance.new("UICorner", slotFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

            local topNum = Instance.new("TextLabel", slotFrame)
            topNum.Name = "Num"
            topNum.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 11)
            topNum.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            topNum.Text = tostring(i)
            topNum.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
            topNum.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            topNum.TextSize = 9

            local statusTxt = Instance.new("TextLabel", slotFrame)
            statusTxt.Name = "Status"
            statusTxt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
            statusTxt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 11)
            statusTxt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            statusTxt.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
            statusTxt.TextSize = 10
        end
    end

    -- Обновляем инфу в существующих слотах
    local idx = 0
    for _, slotFrame in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
        if slotFrame:IsA("Frame") then
            idx = idx + 1
            local sName = pData.SlotNames[idx]
            local statusTxt = slotFrame:FindFirstChild("Status")
            
            local cdEnd = pData.Cooldowns[idx]
            local rem = cdEnd and (cdEnd - tick()) or 0
            local isHeld = equippedTool and (equippedTool.Name == sName)

            if rem > 0 then
                slotFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(195, 45, 45)
                statusTxt.Text = string.format("%.1f", rem)
                statusTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            elseif isHeld then
                slotFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 185, 40)
                statusTxt.Text = sName:sub(1, 3)
                statusTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
            else
                slotFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 155, 65)
                statusTxt.Text = sName:sub(1, 3)
                statusTxt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            end
        end
    end
end

createToggle(visualsPage, "⏱ Трекер КД над головой (Умные слоты 1–9)", function(Value)
    cdTrackerEnabled = Value
    if not cdTrackerEnabled then return end

    task.spawn(function()
        while cdTrackerEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
               if plr ~= Player and plr.Character then
                 local char = plr.Character
          local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if head and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local pData = playerCooldownData[plr]
                        if not pData or pData.Char ~= char then
                            pData = setupPlayerCdTracker(plr, char, hum)
                        end
                        local eq = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                        if eq then registerPlayerTool(pData, eq) end
                        local bp = plr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
                        if bp then
                            for _, it in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                                if it:IsA("Tool") then registerPlayerTool(pData, it) end
                            end
                        end
                        renderPlayerCdSlots(head, pData, eq)
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end

        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                local gui = plr.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("PromptCDTracker")
                if gui then gui:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end)
end)

end

createToggle(visualsPage, "🛰 Радар-миникарта сверху (Ты зелёный, враги красные)", function(Value)

        local radarRadiusStuds = 200

        while minimapEnabled and container and container.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local char = Player.Character
            local myRoot = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera

            if myRoot and cam then
                local myPos = myRoot.Position
                local camLook = cam.CFrame.LookVector
                local camYaw = math.atan2(-camLook.X, -camLook.Z)

                local seen = {}
                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if plr ~= Player and plr.Character then
                        local eHrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local eHum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if eHrp and eHum and eHum.Health > 0 then
                            seen[plr] = true
                            local dot = enemyMarkers[plr]
                            if not dot then
                                dot = Instance.new("Frame", container)
                                dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 9)
                                dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

                                dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                dot.ZIndex = 58
                                Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

                                local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", dot)
                                lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 12)
                                lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -35, 1, 1)
                                lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                                lbl.Text = plr.DisplayName:sub(1, 8)
                                lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 190)
                                lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                                lbl.TextSize = 10
                                lbl.ZIndex = 59

                                enemyMarkers[plr] = dot
                            end

                            local relX = eHrp.Position.X - myPos.X
                            local relZ = eHrp.Position.Z - myPos.Z

                            local cosA = math.cos(-camYaw)
                            local sinA = math.sin(-camYaw)
                            local rotX = relX * cosA - relZ * sinA
                            local rotZ = relX * sinA + relZ * cosA

                            local normX = math.clamp(rotX / radarRadiusStuds, -0.92, 0.92)
                            local normY = math.clamp(rotZ / radarRadiusStuds, -0.92, 0.92)

                            dot.Visible = true
                            dot.Position = UDim2.new(0.5 + (normX * 0.46), 0, 0.5 + (normY * 0.46), 0)
                        end
                    end
                end

                for plr, dot in pairs(enemyMarkers) do
                    if not seen[plr] then
                        if dot and dot.Parent then dot:Destroy() end
                        enemyMarkers[plr] = nil
                    end
                end
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end

        if container and container.Parent then container:Destroy() end
    end)
end)

local stealthEyeFrame = nil

createToggle(visualsPage, "👁 Индикатор скрытности (Видят ли меня враги)", function(Value)
    stealthEyeEnabled = Value
    if stealthEyeFrame then
        pcall(function() stealthEyeFrame:Destroy() end)
        stealthEyeFrame = nil
    end

    if not stealthEyeEnabled then return end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = "PromptStealthEye"
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 52)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(1, -112, 0.25, 0)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 28, 34)
    box.Active = true
    box.Draggable = true
    box.ZIndex = 60
    box.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", box)
    stroke.Thickness = 2.5
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 130)

    local eyeLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", box)
    eyeLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    eyeLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    eyeLbl.Text = "👁"
    eyeLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
    eyeLbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    eyeLbl.TextSize = 26
    eyeLbl.ZIndex = 61

    local slashLine = Instance.new("Frame", box)
    slashLine.Name = "SlashBar"
    slashLine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 3.5)
    slashLine.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -21, 0.5, -2)
    slashLine.Rotation = -45
    slashLine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 60, 60)
    slashLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
    slashLine.Visible = true
    slashLine.ZIndex = 62
    Instance.new("UICorner", slashLine).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    stealthEyeFrame = box

    task.spawn(function()
        while stealthEyeEnabled and box and box.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local char = Player.Character
            local myRoot = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
            local status = 0

            if myRoot then
                local myPos = myRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
                local ignoreList = {}
                if char then table.insert(ignoreList, char) end
                if ghostVisualClone then table.insert(ignoreList, ghostVisualClone) end
                if ghostSimulationCube then table.insert(ignoreList, ghostSimulationCube) end

                for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if plr ~= Player and plr.Character then
                        local eHead = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        local eHrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local eHum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                        if eHead and eHrp and eHum and eHum.Health > 0 then
                            local dist = (eHead.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                            if dist <= 220 then
                                local fullIgnore = {plr.Character}
                                for _, item in ipairs(ignoreList) do table.insert(fullIgnore, item) end

                                local toMe = myPos - eHead.Position
                                local dot = eHead.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(toMe.Unit)
                                if dot > -0.1 then
                                    local ray = Ray.new(eHead.Position, toMe)
                                    local hit = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray, fullIgnore)
                                    if not hit or not hit.CanCollide or hit.Transparency >= 0.6 then
                                        status = 2
                                        break
                                    end
                                end

                                if status < 1 and dist <= 150 then
                                    local cam3pPos = eHead.Position - (eHrp.CFrame.LookVector * 12) + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                                    local ray3p = Ray.new(cam3pPos, myPos - cam3pPos)
                                    local hit3p = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray3p, fullIgnore)
                                    if not hit3p or not hit3p.CanCollide or hit3p.Transparency >= 0.6 then
                                        status = 1
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if status == 2 then
                slashLine.Visible = false
                eyeLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 65)
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 65)
                box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55, 20, 20)
            elseif status == 1 then
                slashLine.Visible = false
                eyeLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60)
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 60)
                box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 42, 18)
            else
                slashLine.Visible = true
                eyeLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
                stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 110)
                box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 28, 34)
            end

            task.wait(0.2)
        end

        if box and box.Parent then box:Destroy() end
    end)
end)

createToggle(visualsPage, "💎 Подсветка предметов и лута на карте", function(Value)
    lootEspEnabled = Value
    if not lootEspEnabled then 
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            local hl = obj:FindFirstChild("PromptLootESP")
            if hl then hl:Destroy() end
        end
        return 
    end

    -- ШИРОКИЙ ПОИСК ЛУТА: по названиям (подстроки и точные имена), по структуре
    -- (ProximityPrompt / ClickDetector / выброшенные Tool / мелкие Touch-пикапы) и по папкам-контейнерам.
    local nameWords = {
        "coin", "cash", "gem", "drop", "item", "loot", "money", "gold", "silver", "crate", "chest",
        "pickup", "pick_up", "pick up", "collect", "treasure", "reward", "gift", "present", "token",
        "ticket", "keycard", "ammo", "medkit", "firstaid", "first aid", "health", "potion", "supply",
        "supplies", "resource", "crystal", "diamond", "scrap", "material", "artifact", "relic", "bonus",
        "powerup", "power up", "dollar", "stash", "cache", "valuable", "jewel", "ruby", "emerald",
        "sapphire", "bandage", "bullet", "armor", "battery", "fuel", "blueprint", "weapon", "pistol",
        "rifle", "shotgun", "grenade", "loot", "booster", "upgrade", "orbs", "shard", "fragment", "soul", "medkit",
    }
    local exactWords = {
        key = true, keys = true, orb = true, star = true, egg = true, bag = true, ore = true,
        box = true, tool = true, gun = true, bill = true, pill = true, pills = true, pearl = true,
        heart = true, apple = true, bone = true, wood = true, stone = true, iron = true, food = true
    }
    local containerWords = {"loot", "drop", "item", "pickup", "collect", "treasure", "reward", "coin", "cash", "money"}
    local excludeWords = {
        "door", "gate", "wall", "floor", "ground", "baseplate", "terrain", "window", "fence", "roof",
        "stair", "ladder", "seat", "chair", "button", "lever", "elevator", "teleport", "portal", "shop",
        "store", "vendor", "npc", "cashier", "register", "kill", "lava", "checkpoint", "tree", "leaf",
        "grass", "road", "bridge", "spawnlocation"
    }
    local promptSkipWords = {
        "door", "enter", "exit", "talk", "sit", "buy", "sell", "trade", "ride", "drive", "leave",
        "shop", "teleport", "elevator", "speak", "dialog", "quest", "button", "lever", "switch"
    }

    local function hasAny(str, list)
        for _, w in ipairs(list) do
            if str:find(w, 1, true) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function baseName(s)
        return (s:lower():gsub("[%d%s_%-%.]+$", ""))
    end

    local function matchesName(rawName)
        local n = rawName:lower()
        if n:sub(1, 6) == "prompt" or n:sub(1, 8) == "phantom_" then return false end
        if hasAny(n, excludeWords) then return false end
        if hasAny(n, nameWords) then return true end
        return exactWords[baseName(rawName)] == true
    end

    local function isSmallModel(m)
        local ok, size = pcall(function() return m:GetExtentsSize() end)
        return ok and math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) < 30
    end

    local function insideCharacter(inst)
        if inst:IsA("Model") and inst:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
        local p = inst.Parent
        while p and p ~= workspace do
            if p:IsA("Model") and p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
            p = p.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    -- возвращает объект, который нужно подсветить (или nil)
    local function getLootTarget(obj)
        if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") or obj:IsA("ClickDetector") then
            local t = obj.Parent
            if t and t:IsA("Attachment") then t = t.Parent end
            if not t or not (t:IsA("BasePart") or t:IsA("Model")) then return nil end
            local tn = t.Name:lower()
            if tn:sub(1, 6) == "prompt" or hasAny(tn, excludeWords) then return nil end
            if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                local txt = ((obj.ActionText or "") .. " " .. (obj.ObjectText or "")):lower()
                if hasAny(txt, promptSkipWords) then return nil end
            end
            return t
        end

        if obj:IsA("Tool") then return obj end

        if obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model") then
            local parent = obj.Parent
            if matchesName(obj.Name) then
                if obj:IsA("Model") and not isSmallModel(obj) then return nil end
                if obj:IsA("BasePart") and parent and parent:IsA("Model") and parent ~= workspace
                    and matchesName(parent.Name) and isSmallModel(parent) then
                    return nil -- подсветится вся модель целиком
                end
                return obj
            end

            if parent and parent ~= workspace and (parent:IsA("Folder") or parent:IsA("Model")) then
                local pn = parent.Name:lower()
                if hasAny(pn, containerWords) and not hasAny(obj.Name:lower(), excludeWords) then
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") and parent:IsA("Model") and isSmallModel(parent) then return nil end
                    return obj
                end
            end

            if obj:IsA("BasePart") and not obj.CanCollide and obj.Size.Magnitude < 8
                and obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("TouchTransmitter") and not hasAny(obj.Name:lower(), excludeWords) then
                return obj
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function posOf(inst)
        if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst.Position end
        if inst:IsA("Model") then return inst:GetPivot().Position end
        local part = inst:FindFirstChild("Handle") or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        return part and part.Position or nil
    end

    local function adorneeOf(inst)
        if inst:IsA("Tool") then
            return inst:FindFirstChild("Handle") or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        end
        return inst
    end

    local MAX_HIGHLIGHTS = 30 -- Roblox рисует не больше ~31 Highlight одновременно, поэтому берём ближайшие
    local lootHL = {}
    local notLoot = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

    task.spawn(function()
        while lootEspEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                local char = Player.Character
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local origin = root and root.Position or workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position

                local found, list, counter = {}, {}, 0
                for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    counter = counter + 1
                    if counter % 2500 == 0 then
                        task.wait()
                        if not lootEspEnabled then break end
                    end
                    if not notLoot[obj] then
                        local ok, t = pcall(getLootTarget, obj)
                        if ok and t then
                            if not found[t] and t.Parent and not insideCharacter(t) then
                                local pos = posOf(t)
                                if pos then
                                    found[t] = true
                                    table.insert(list, {inst = t, d = (pos - origin).Magnitude})
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            notLoot[obj] = true
                        end
                    end
                end

                if lootEspEnabled then
                    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.d < b.d end)
                    local keep = {}
                    for i = 1, math.min(#list, MAX_HIGHLIGHTS) do
                        local t = list[i].inst
                        keep[t] = true
                        local old = lootHL[t]
                        if not old or not old.Parent then
                            local adornee = adorneeOf(t)
                            if adornee then
                                local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
                                hl.Name = "PromptLootESP"
                                hl.Adornee = adornee
                                hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(60, 220, 255)
                                hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                                hl.Parent = t
                                lootHL[t] = hl
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    for t, hl in pairs(lootHL) do
                        if not keep[t] then
                            if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
                            lootHL[t] = nil
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(1.5)
        end

        for t, hl in pairs(lootHL) do
            if hl then pcall(function() hl:Destroy() end) end
            lootHL[t] = nil
        end
    end)
end)

end
