do
local autoClickerEnabled = false
local autoCollectEnabled = false
local instantPromptEnabled = false

local autoClickToggleObj = nil
local autoClickBanner = nil
local autoClickTargetMarker = nil

local function cleanupAutoClickerUI()
    if autoClickBanner then pcall(function() autoClickBanner:Destroy() end) autoClickBanner = nil end
    if autoClickTargetMarker then pcall(function() autoClickTargetMarker:Destroy() end) autoClickTargetMarker = nil end
end

local function isMainMenuVisible()
    if not ScreenGui then return false end
    for _, child in ipairs(ScreenGui:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") and child.Size.X.Offset >= 300 and child.Size.Y.Offset >= 200 then
            return child.Visible
        end
    end
    return false
end

autoClickToggleObj = createToggle(farmPage, "🖱 Автокликер (Тапни точку после сворачивания -)", function(Value)
    autoClickerEnabled = Value
    cleanupAutoClickerUI()

    if not autoClickerEnabled then return end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    local banner = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    banner.Name = "AutoClickHintBanner"
    banner.Size = UDim2.new(0, 310, 0, 36)
    banner.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -155, 0.04, 0)
    banner.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 30, 38)
    banner.Text = "🖱 Сверни меню (-) и нажми в точку клика"
    banner.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    banner.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    banner.TextSize = 14
    banner.ZIndex = 80
    banner.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", banner).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", banner)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
    autoClickBanner = banner

    task.spawn(function()
        local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")

        while autoClickerEnabled and isMainMenuVisible() and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
        if not autoClickerEnabled or _G.ScriptDestroyed then
            cleanupAutoClickerUI()
            return
        end

        if banner and banner.Parent then
            banner.Text = "🎯 Нажми на экране место, куда кликать!"
            banner.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 130)
            stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 120)
        end

        local targetPos = nil
        local tapConn
        tapConn = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if not autoClickerEnabled or targetPos then return end
            if isMainMenuVisible() then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                targetPos = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
            end
        end)

        while autoClickerEnabled and not targetPos and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if isMainMenuVisible() then
                autoClickerEnabled = false
                if autoClickToggleObj and autoClickToggleObj.Set then autoClickToggleObj.Set(false) end
                break
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        end

        if tapConn then tapConn:Disconnect() end

        if not autoClickerEnabled or not targetPos or _G.ScriptDestroyed then
            cleanupAutoClickerUI()
            return
        end

        if banner and banner.Parent then
            pcall(function() banner:Destroy() end)
            autoClickBanner = nil
        end

        local marker = Instance.new("Frame")
        marker.Name = "AutoClickTargetDot"
        marker.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
        marker.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        marker.Position = UDim2.new(0, targetPos.X, 0, targetPos.Y)
        marker.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 100)
        marker.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
        marker.ZIndex = 80
        marker.Parent = ScreenGui
        Instance.new("UICorner", marker).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local mStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", marker)
        mStroke.Thickness = 2
        mStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        autoClickTargetMarker = marker

        local inset = GuiService:GetGuiInset()
        local clickX = targetPos.X + inset.X
        local clickY = targetPos.Y + inset.Y

        while autoClickerEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if isMainMenuVisible() then
                autoClickerEnabled = false
                if autoClickToggleObj and autoClickToggleObj.Set then autoClickToggleObj.Set(false) end
                break
            end

            if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                local char = Player.Character
                local tool = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                if tool then pcall(function() tool:Activate() end) end

                pcall(function()
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(clickX, clickY, 0, true, game, 1)
                    task.wait(0.02)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(clickX, clickY, 0, false, game, 1)
                end)
            end
            task.wait(0.1)
        end

        cleanupAutoClickerUI()
    end)
end)

-- вот тут находится конец 1 блока (части 8.1)
createToggle(farmPage, "🧲 Автосбор предметов и монет вокруг", function(Value)
    autoCollectEnabled = Value
    if not autoCollectEnabled then return end

    task.spawn(function()
        while autoCollectEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                local char = Player.Character
                local hrp = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                if hrp then
                    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                        if obj:IsA("TouchTransmitter") and obj.Parent and obj.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local part = obj.Parent
                            local lowerName = part.Name:lower()
                            local parentName = part.Parent and part.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
                            if lowerName:find("coin") or lowerName:find("cash") or lowerName:find("drop")
                                or lowerName:find("gem") or lowerName:find("orb") or lowerName:find("star")
                                or lowerName:find("reward") or lowerName:find("collect") or parentName:find("drop") then
                                if (part.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 120 then
                                    pcall(function()
                                        firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 0)
                                        firetouchinterest(hrp, part, 1)
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
end)

local savedPromptStats = {}

local function applyInstantPrompt(prompt)
    if not prompt or not prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then return end
    if not savedPromptStats[prompt] then
        savedPromptStats[prompt] = {
            Hold = prompt.HoldDuration,
            Dist = prompt.MaxActivationDistance
        }
    end
    prompt.HoldDuration = 0
    if prompt.MaxActivationDistance < 25 then
        prompt.MaxActivationDistance = 25
    end
end

createToggle(movePage, "⚡ Мгновенное взаимодействие (Без удержания)", function(Value)
    instantPromptEnabled = Value
    if instantPromptEnabled then
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then applyInstantPrompt(obj) end
        end

        local promptConn
        promptConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if not instantPromptEnabled or _G.ScriptDestroyed then
                if promptConn then promptConn:Disconnect() end
                return
            end
            if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                task.defer(function()
                    if instantPromptEnabled and obj and obj.Parent then applyInstantPrompt(obj) end
                end)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            while instantPromptEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do task.wait(1) end
            if promptConn then promptConn:Disconnect() end
            for prompt, orig in pairs(savedPromptStats) do
                if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        prompt.HoldDuration = orig.Hold
                        prompt.MaxActivationDistance = orig.Dist
                    end)
                end
            end
            savedPromptStats = {}
        end)
    else
        for prompt, orig in pairs(savedPromptStats) do
            if prompt and prompt.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    prompt.HoldDuration = orig.Hold
                    prompt.MaxActivationDistance = orig.Dist
                end)
            end
        end
        savedPromptStats = {}
    end
end)

end

local autoMathSolverEnabled = false

local function evaluateMathExpression(rawText)
    if type(rawText) ~= "string" or #rawText > 160 then return nil, nil end
    local s = rawText:gsub("×", "*"):gsub("[хХxX]", "*"):gsub("÷", "/"):gsub(":", "/"):gsub(",", ".")
    local pos, len = 1, #s
    local function spaces() while pos <= len and s:sub(pos,pos):match("%s") do pos = pos + 1 end end
    local expression
    local function primary()
        spaces()
        if s:sub(pos,pos) == "(" then
            pos = pos + 1; local v = expression(); spaces()
            if s:sub(pos,pos) ~= ")" then error("Ожидалась )", 0) end
            pos = pos + 1; return v
        end
        local name = s:sub(pos):match("^([%a_]+)")
        if name then
            pos = pos + #name; name = name:lower()
            local constants = {pi = math.pi, e = math.exp(1)}
            if constants[name] then return constants[name] end
            local funcs = {sqrt=math.sqrt, abs=math.abs, sin=math.sin, cos=math.cos,
                tan=math.tan, floor=math.floor, ceil=math.ceil, log=math.log}
            local f = funcs[name]
            if not f then error("Неизвестная функция/константа: " .. name, 0) end
            spaces(); if s:sub(pos,pos) ~= "(" then error("После функции нужна скобка", 0) end
            pos = pos + 1; local v = expression(); spaces()
            if s:sub(pos,pos) ~= ")" then error("Ожидалась )", 0) end
            pos = pos + 1; return f(v)
        end
        local rest = s:sub(pos)
        local token = rest:match("^(%d+%.?%d*)") or rest:match("^(%.%d+")
        if not token then error("Ожидалось число", 0) end
        pos = pos + #token
        return tonumber(token)
    end
    local function power()
        local v = primary(); spaces()
        if s:sub(pos,pos) == "^" then pos = pos + 1; v = v ^ power() end
        return v
    end
    local function unary()
        spaces(); local c = s:sub(pos,pos)
        if c == "+" then pos=pos+1; return unary() end
        if c == "-" then pos=pos+1; return -unary() end
        return power()
    end
    local function term()
        local v = unary()
        while true do
            spaces(); local op=s:sub(pos,pos)
            if op ~= "*" and op ~= "/" then break end
            pos=pos+1; local r=unary()
            if op=="/" and r==0 then error("Деление на ноль",0) end
            v = op=="*" and v*r or v/r
        end
        return v
    end
    expression = function()
        local v=term()
        while true do
            spaces(); local op=s:sub(pos,pos)
            if op~="+" and op~="-" then break end
            pos=pos+1; local r=term(); v=op=="+" and v+r or v-r
        end
        return v
    end
    local ok, value = pcall(function()
        local v=expression(); spaces()
        if pos<=len then error("Лишние символы",0) end
        if type(v)~="number" or v~=v or math.abs(v)==math.huge or math.abs(v)>1e15 then error("Результат вне диапазона",0) end
        return v
    end)
    if not ok then return nil,nil end
    local nearest = value >= 0 and math.floor(value + 0.5) or math.ceil(value - 0.5)
    local formatted = math.abs(value-nearest)<1e-10 and tostring(nearest) or string.format("%.6g",value)
    return s, formatted
end

local mathOverlayBanner = nil
local worldMathBillboards = {}

local function clearWorldMathBillboards()
    for _, bbg in ipairs(worldMathBillboards) do
        if bbg and bbg.Parent then pcall(function() bbg:Destroy() end) end
    end
    worldMathBillboards = {}
end

createToggle(visualsPage, "🧮 Авто-решатель примеров (Экран + Мир)", function(Value)
    autoMathSolverEnabled = Value
    if mathOverlayBanner then
        pcall(function() mathOverlayBanner:Destroy() end)
        mathOverlayBanner = nil
    end
    clearWorldMathBillboards()

    if not autoMathSolverEnabled then return end
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end

    local banner = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    banner.Name = "PromptMathBanner"
    banner.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 38)
    banner.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -110, 0.04, 0)
    banner.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 30, 35)
    banner.Text = "🧮 Жду пример..."
    banner.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 130)
    banner.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    banner.TextSize = 16
    banner.Active = true
    banner.Draggable = true
    banner.ZIndex = 70
    banner.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", banner).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", banner)
    stroke.Thickness = 2
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 120)

    mathOverlayBanner = banner

    task.spawn(function()
        while autoMathSolverEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                local foundExpr, foundAns = nil, nil

                local pGui = Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                if pGui then
                    for _, uiObj in ipairs(pGui:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (uiObj:IsA("TextLabel") or uiObj:IsA("TextButton")) and uiObj.Visible then
                            local txt = uiObj.Text
                            if txt and #txt >= 3 and #txt <= 40 and not txt:find("🧮") then
                                local expr, ans = evaluateMathExpression(txt)
                                if expr and ans then
                                    foundExpr, foundAns = expr, ans
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                local char = Player.Character
                local myRoot = (phantomRunning and ghostSimulationCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                if myRoot then
                    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                        if obj:IsA("TextLabel") and obj.Visible then
                            local guiHolder = obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui") or obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BillboardGui")
                            local adorneePart = guiHolder and (guiHolder.Adornee or guiHolder.Parent)
                            if adorneePart and adorneePart:IsA("BasePart") then
                                if (adorneePart.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude <= 130 then
                                    local expr, ans = evaluateMathExpression(obj.Text)
                                    if expr and ans then
                                        foundExpr, foundAns = expr, ans
                                        if not adorneePart:FindFirstChild("PromptWorldMathHint") then
                                            local hint = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                                            hint.Name = "PromptWorldMathHint"
                                            hint.Adornee = adorneePart
                                            hint.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 34)
                                            hint.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, (adorneePart.Size.Y * 0.5) + 2.2, 0)
                                            hint.AlwaysOnTop = true
                                            hint.Parent = adorneePart

                                            local hLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", hint)
                                            hLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                                            hLbl.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 140, 55)
                                            hLbl.Text = "= " .. ans
                                            hLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                            hLbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                                            hLbl.TextSize = 18
                                            Instance.new("UICorner", hLbl).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

                                            table.insert(worldMathBillboards, hint)
                                        else
                                            local existingLbl = adorneePart.PromptWorldMathHint:FindFirstChildOfClass("TextLabel")
                                            if existingLbl then existingLbl.Text = "= " .. ans end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if foundExpr and foundAns and banner and banner.Parent then
                    banner.Text = "🧮 " .. foundExpr .. " = " .. foundAns
                    banner.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 130)
                elseif banner and banner.Parent then
                    banner.Text = "🧮 Жду пример..."
                    banner.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.35)
        end

        if mathOverlayBanner then
            pcall(function() mathOverlayBanner:Destroy() end)
            mathOverlayBanner = nil
        end
        clearWorldMathBillboards()
    end)
end)

local calcBaseTitle = "🔢 Калькулятор (ответ рядом с заголовком)"
local calcParaFrame = createParagraph(farmPage, calcBaseTitle, "Введите пример ниже (например: 16*3)")

local calcTitleLabel = nil
local calcContentLabel = nil
local searchTarget = calcParaFrame or farmPage
for _, ch in ipairs(searchTarget:GetDescendants()) do
    if ch:IsA("TextLabel") then
        if ch.Text == calcBaseTitle then
            calcTitleLabel = ch
        elseif ch.Text == "Введите пример ниже (например: 16*3)" then
            calcContentLabel = ch
        end
    end
end

local function updateCalcDisplay(answerText, exprText)
    if not calcTitleLabel then return end
    if answerText then
        calcTitleLabel.Text = calcBaseTitle .. "  ->  ОТВЕТ: " .. answerText
        if calcContentLabel then
            calcContentLabel.Text = "Пример: " .. (exprText or "")
        end
    else
        calcTitleLabel.Text = calcBaseTitle
        if calcContentLabel then
            calcContentLabel.Text = "Введите пример ниже (например: 16*3)"
        end
    end
end

createInput(farmPage, "Ввести пример вручную", "Пример: 16*3 или (12+8)/4...", function(Text)
    if not Text or Text == "" then
        updateCalcDisplay(nil, nil)
        return
    end
    local expr, ans = evaluateMathExpression(Text)
    if expr and ans then
        updateCalcDisplay(ans, expr)
    else
        updateCalcDisplay("Ошибка", Text)
    end
end)

-- ❄️ ЗАВИСАНИЕ В ВОЗДУХЕ (AIR FREEZE)
local airFreezeEnabled = false
local airFreezeConnection = nil

createToggle(movePage, "❄️ Зависание в воздухе (Air Freeze)", function(Value)
    airFreezeEnabled = Value
    
    if airFreezeConnection then
        airFreezeConnection:Disconnect()
        airFreezeConnection = nil
    end

    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if airFreezeEnabled then
        if hrp then
            hrp.Anchored = true
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        if hum then
            hum.PlatformStand = true
            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    track.Speed = 0
                end
            end
        end

        airFreezeConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not airFreezeEnabled or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
            local c = Player.Character
            local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local u = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            
            if h then
                h.Anchored = true
                h.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            if u then
                u.PlatformStand = true
            end
        end)
    else
        if hrp then
            hrp.Anchored = false
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        if hum then
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    track.Speed = 1
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
    if airFreezeConnection then
        airFreezeConnection:Disconnect()
        airFreezeConnection = nil
    end
    airFreezeEnabled = false
end)

-- =========================================================
-- 🖤 ЧЕРНИЛЬНЫЙ ДАЙВИНГ (INK DIVE + ПОЛУПРОЗРАЧНАЯ КУКЛА)
-- =========================================================
local bendyHudEnabled = false
local bendyDiveActive = false
local bendyProxyRig = nil
local bendyConnStepped = nil
local bendyConnHeartbeat = nil
local bendyHudGui = nil
local bendyHudBtnRef = nil
local bendyInkOverlay = nil

local function getBendyRoot(model)
    if not model then return nil end
    return model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or model.PrimaryPart
        or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
end

local function destroyBendyInkOverlay()
    if bendyInkOverlay then
        pcall(function() bendyInkOverlay:Destroy() end)
        bendyInkOverlay = nil
    end
end

local function createBendyInkOverlay()
    destroyBendyInkOverlay()
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "PromptBendyInkOverlay"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.DisplayOrder = 20
    local parent = nil
    pcall(function() parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    local parentOk = false
    if parent then parentOk = pcall(function() gui.Parent = parent end) end
    if not parentOk then gui.Parent = Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    bendyInkOverlay = gui

    local edges = {
        {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)},
        {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -24)},
        {Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)},
        {Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 0)},
    }
    for _, edge in ipairs(edges) do
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.Size = edge.Size
        frame.Position = edge.Position
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 8, 18)
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.48
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Active = false
        frame.Selectable = false
        frame.Parent = gui
    end
end

local function stopBendyDive(teleportBack)
    if bendyConnStepped then bendyConnStepped:Disconnect() bendyConnStepped = nil end
    if bendyConnHeartbeat then bendyConnHeartbeat:Disconnect() bendyConnHeartbeat = nil end
    destroyBendyInkOverlay()

    local char = Player.Character
    local realHrp = getBendyRoot(char)
    local realHum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if bendyProxyRig then
        local pHrp = getBendyRoot(bendyProxyRig)
        if teleportBack and realHrp and pHrp then
            realHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            realHrp.CFrame = pHrp.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
        end
        pcall(function() bendyProxyRig:Destroy() end)
        bendyProxyRig = nil
    end

    if char then
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = (part.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or part.Name == "Torso" or part.Name == "UpperTorso" or part.Name == "LowerTorso")
            end
        end
    end

    if realHum then
        realHum.PlatformStand = false
        realHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = realHum
    end
end

local function startBendyDive(hudBtn)
    local char = Player.Character
    local realHrp = getBendyRoot(char)
    local realHum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not realHrp or not realHum then
        warn("[Prompt_Hacker Ink Diving] В этой игре не найден совместимый корневой Part/Humanoid.")
        return false
    end

    local walkAnim, jumpAnim
    local origAnim = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if origAnim then
        local r = origAnim:FindFirstChild("run") or origAnim:FindFirstChild("walk")
        if r and r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation") then
            walkAnim = r:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation"):Clone()
        end
        local j = origAnim:FindFirstChild("jump")
        if j and j:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation") then
            jumpAnim = j:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation"):Clone()
        end
    end

    local previousArchivable = char.Archivable
    char.Archivable = true
    local cloneOk, proxy = pcall(function()
        return char:Clone()
    end)
    char.Archivable = previousArchivable
    if not cloneOk or not proxy then
        warn("[Prompt_Hacker Ink Diving] Не удалось клонировать персонажа в этой игре.")
        return false
    end
    proxy.Name = "BendySurfacePuppet"

    for _, obj in ipairs(proxy:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("LocalScript") or obj:IsA("Script") or obj:IsA("ForceField") then
            obj:Destroy()
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.Anchored = false
            obj.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
            obj.Color = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 45)
            obj.Transparency = (obj.Name == "HumanoidRootPart") and 1 or 0.55
            obj.CanCollide = (obj.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or obj.Name == "Torso" or obj.Name == "UpperTorso" or obj.Name == "LowerTorso")
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            obj.Transparency = 0.55
        end
    end

    local pHrp = getBendyRoot(proxy)
    local pHum = proxy:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not pHrp or not pHum then
        proxy:Destroy()
        return false
    end

    pHum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
    pHum.WalkSpeed = realHum.WalkSpeed
    pHum.JumpPower = realHum.JumpPower
    pHum.UseJumpPower = realHum.UseJumpPower
    pHum.JumpHeight = realHum.JumpHeight
    pHum.AutoRotate = true
    proxy.Parent = workspace
    bendyProxyRig = proxy
    createBendyInkOverlay()

    proxy:PivotTo(realHrp.CFrame)
    workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = pHum

    local animator = pHum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", pHum)
    local walkTrack, jumpTrack
    if walkAnim then
        walkAnim.Parent = proxy
        walkTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(walkAnim)
        walkTrack.Looped = true
    end
    if jumpAnim then
        jumpAnim.Parent = proxy
        jumpTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(jumpAnim)
        jumpTrack.Looped = false
    end

    pHum.Running:Connect(function(spd)
        if not walkTrack then return end
        if spd > 1 then
            if not walkTrack.IsPlaying then walkTrack:Play(0.15) end
            walkTrack:AdjustSpeed(spd / 16)
        else
            if walkTrack.IsPlaying then walkTrack:Stop(0.15) end
        end
    end)

    pHum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, state)
        if not jumpTrack then return end
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
            if not jumpTrack.IsPlaying then jumpTrack:Play(0.1) end
        else
            if jumpTrack.IsPlaying then jumpTrack:Stop(0.15) end
        end
    end)

    bendyConnStepped = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not bendyDiveActive or not char.Parent then return end
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end)

    local bob = 0
    bendyConnHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not bendyDiveActive then return end
        if _G.ScriptDestroyed or not char.Parent or not realHrp.Parent or not proxy.Parent or realHum.Health <= 0 then
            bendyDiveActive = false
            if hudBtn then
                hudBtn.Text = "OFF"
                hudBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 55, 45)
            end
            stopBendyDive(false)
            return
        end

        pHum.WalkSpeed = realHum.WalkSpeed
        pHum:Move(realHum.MoveDirection, false)
        if realHum.Jump then
            pHum.Jump = true
        end

        bob = (bob == 0) and 0.1 or 0
        local voidLimit = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 3
        local deepY = math.max(pHrp.Position.Y - 35 + bob, voidLimit)

        realHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
        realHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0.4, 0)
        realHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        realHrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pHrp.Position.X, deepY, pHrp.Position.Z) * (pHrp.CFrame - pHrp.CFrame.Position)
    end)
    return true
end

local function destroyBendyHud()
    bendyDiveActive = false
    stopBendyDive(true)
    if bendyHudGui then
        pcall(function() bendyHudGui:Destroy() end)
        bendyHudGui = nil
    end
end

-- =========================================================
-- 🧊 ANTI-SLIDE: REAL CHARACTER ONLY (v4)
-- Антискольжение для настоящего Player.Character.
-- Обычная земля не меняется; лёд получает ускорение и торможение.
-- =========================================================
do
    local AntiSlide = {
        Enabled = false,
        Connection = nil,
        CharacterConnection = nil,
        RemovingConnection = nil,
        Saved = {},

        StopSpeed = 0.5,
        DecayRate = 30,
        IdleDelay = 0.06,
        LandingRamp = 0.08,
        ForeignCameraDelay = 0.3,

        IceFriction = 0.1,
        IceMaterials = {
            [Enum.Material.Ice] = true,
        },
        IceAccel = 40,

        ShouldPause = nil,
        IsIce = nil,
    }

    -- Ink Diving явно ставит Anti-Slide на паузу.
    -- Проверка камеры ниже остаётся дополнительной защитой для других режимов.
    AntiSlide.ShouldPause = function()
        return bendyDiveActive == true or _G.PH_TrollActive == true
    end

    local MAX_FLOOR_SPEED = 150
    local idleTime = 0
    local groundedTime = 0
    local foreignCameraTime = 0
    local lastTick = nil

    local floorBase = nil
    local floorPos = nil
    local floorTime = 0
    local floorVel = Vector3.zero

    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    rayParams.RespectCanCollide = true
    local rayCharacter = nil

    local function resetState()
        idleTime = 0
        groundedTime = 0
        foreignCameraTime = 0
        lastTick = nil
        floorBase = nil
        floorPos = nil
        floorTime = 0
        floorVel = Vector3.zero
        rayCharacter = nil
    end

    local SKIP_STATES = {
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Flying] = true,
    }

    local function isCameraForeign(character)
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local subject = cam and cam.CameraSubject
        return subject ~= nil and not subject:IsDescendantOf(character)
    end

    local function isMover(inst)
        if inst:IsA("BodyMover") then
            return true
        end
        if (inst:IsA("LinearVelocity") or inst:IsA("VectorForce")
            or inst:IsA("AlignPosition")) and inst.Enabled then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function hasExternalMover(character, root)
        for _, child in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
            if isMover(child) then
                return true
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if isMover(child) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function getFloor(character, root, humanoid)
        if rayCharacter ~= character then
            rayCharacter = character
            rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = { character }
        end
        local length = humanoid.HipHeight + root.Size.Y / 2 + 2.5
        return workspace:Raycast(root.Position, Vector3.new(0, -length, 0), rayParams)
    end

    local function isIce(floor)
        local part = floor.Instance

        if AntiSlide.IceMaterials[floor.Material] then
            return true
        end

        if AntiSlide.IsIce then
            local ok, result = pcall(AntiSlide.IsIce, part)
            if ok and result then
                return true
            end
        end

        local ok, props = pcall(function()
            return part.CurrentPhysicalProperties
        end)
        if ok and props and props.Friction < AntiSlide.IceFriction then
            return true
        end

        return false
    end

    local function getFloorVelocity(part, point, now)
        if part:IsA("Terrain") then
            floorBase = nil
            return Vector3.zero
        end

        local base = part.AssemblyRootPart or part
        local velocity

        if base.Anchored then
            if floorBase == base then
                local elapsed = now - floorTime
                if elapsed >= 0.008 then
                    floorVel = (base.Position - floorPos) / elapsed
                    floorPos = base.Position
                    floorTime = now
                end
                velocity = floorVel
            else
                floorBase = base
                floorPos = base.Position
                floorTime = now
                floorVel = Vector3.zero
                return nil
            end
        else
            floorBase = nil
            velocity = part:GetVelocityAtPosition(point)
        end

        if velocity ~= velocity or velocity.Magnitude > MAX_FLOOR_SPEED then
            return Vector3.zero
        end
        return velocity
    end

    local function applyAntiSlide()
        if not AntiSlide.Enabled or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end

        local now = os.clock()
        local dt = lastTick and math.min(now - lastTick, 0.1) or 0
        lastTick = now

        local character = Player.Character
        if not character then return end

        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoid or not root then return end
        if humanoid.Health <= 0 or root.Anchored or humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 0 then return end

        if isCameraForeign(character) then
            foreignCameraTime = foreignCameraTime + dt
            if foreignCameraTime >= AntiSlide.ForeignCameraDelay then
                idleTime, groundedTime = 0, 0
                return
            end
        else
            foreignCameraTime = 0
        end

        if AntiSlide.ShouldPause then
            local ok, paused = pcall(AntiSlide.ShouldPause)
            if ok and paused then
                idleTime, groundedTime = 0, 0
                return
            end
        end

        if humanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air or SKIP_STATES[humanoid:GetState()] then
            groundedTime = 0
            idleTime = 0
            return
        end

        groundedTime = groundedTime + dt

        local moveDirection = humanoid.MoveDirection
        local moveFlat = Vector3.new(moveDirection.X, 0, moveDirection.Z)
        local moving = moveFlat.Magnitude > 0.01

        if moving then
            idleTime = 0
        else
            idleTime = idleTime + dt
            if idleTime < AntiSlide.IdleDelay then return end
        end

        if hasExternalMover(character, root) then return end

        local floor = getFloor(character, root, humanoid)
        if not floor then return end

        local angle = math.deg(math.acos(math.clamp(floor.Normal.Y, -1, 1)))
        if angle > humanoid.MaxSlopeAngle + 1 then return end

        local slippery = isIce(floor)
        if moving and not slippery then return end

        local floorVelocity = getFloorVelocity(floor.Instance, floor.Position, now)
        if not floorVelocity then return end

        local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local relative = Vector3.new(
            velocity.X - floorVelocity.X,
            0,
            velocity.Z - floorVelocity.Z
        )

        local ramp = math.clamp(
            groundedTime / math.max(AntiSlide.LandingRamp, 0.001),
            0,
            1
        )

        if moving then
            local direction = moveFlat.Unit
            local targetSpeed = moveFlat.Magnitude * humanoid.WalkSpeed
            local along = relative:Dot(direction)
            local lateral = relative - direction * along
            local blend = 1 - math.exp(-AntiSlide.IceAccel * ramp * dt)

            if along < targetSpeed then
                along = along + (targetSpeed - along) * blend
            end
            lateral = lateral * (1 - blend)
            relative = direction * along + lateral
        else
            local speed = relative.Magnitude
            if speed < 0.001 then return end

            if speed <= AntiSlide.StopSpeed and ramp >= 1 then
                relative = Vector3.zero
            else
                relative = relative * math.exp(-AntiSlide.DecayRate * ramp * dt)
            end
        end

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
            floorVelocity.X + relative.X,
            velocity.Y,
            floorVelocity.Z + relative.Z
        )
    end

    local function enableAntiSlide()
        if AntiSlide.Enabled then return end
        AntiSlide.Enabled = true
        resetState()

        local signal = RunService.PreSimulation or RunService.Stepped
        AntiSlide.Connection = signal:Connect(applyAntiSlide)
    end

    local function disableAntiSlide()
        AntiSlide.Enabled = false

        if AntiSlide.Connection then
            AntiSlide.Connection:Disconnect()
            AntiSlide.Connection = nil
        end

        resetState()
    end

    AntiSlide.CharacterConnection = Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(resetState)
    AntiSlide.RemovingConnection = Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(resetState)

    -- Существующий GUI-тумблер сохранён, новые кнопки не добавляются.
    createToggle(mainPage, "🧊 Анти скольжение (реальный персонаж)", function(value)
        if value then
            enableAntiSlide()
        else
            disableAntiSlide()
        end
    end)

    -- Отключаем цикл и события при полном закрытии скрипта.
    task.spawn(function()
        while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        disableAntiSlide()

        if AntiSlide.CharacterConnection then
            AntiSlide.CharacterConnection:Disconnect()
            AntiSlide.CharacterConnection = nil
        end
        if AntiSlide.RemovingConnection then
            AntiSlide.RemovingConnection:Disconnect()
            AntiSlide.RemovingConnection = nil
        end
    end)
end

createToggle(mainPage, "🖤 Чернильный дайвинг (Кукла + кнопка OFF/ON)", function(Value)
    bendyHudEnabled = Value
    destroyBendyHud()

    if not bendyHudEnabled then return end

    local hGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    hGui.Name = "PromptBendyDiveHUD"
    hGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    local hParent = nil
    pcall(function() hParent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    local hParentOk = false
    if hParent then hParentOk = pcall(function() hGui.Parent = hParent end) end
    if not hParentOk then hGui.Parent = Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
    bendyHudGui = hGui

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 115, 0, 48)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -135, 0.35, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 55, 45)
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    btn.TextSize = 18
    btn.Text = "OFF"
    btn.Active = true
    btn.Draggable = true
    btn.Parent = hGui
    bendyHudBtnRef = btn
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        bendyDiveActive = not bendyDiveActive
        if bendyDiveActive then
            btn.Text = "ON"
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 175, 55)
            if not startBendyDive(btn) then
                bendyDiveActive = false
                btn.Text = "OFF"
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 55, 45)
            end
        else
            btn.Text = "OFF"
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 55, 45)
            stopBendyDive(true)
        end
    end)
end)

-- API для временной паузы/восстановления дайвинга (например, режимом троллинга).
_G.PH_SetDiveActive = function(active)
    if not bendyHudEnabled then return false end
    if active then
        if not bendyDiveActive then
            bendyDiveActive = true
            if bendyHudBtnRef then
                bendyHudBtnRef.Text = "ON"
                bendyHudBtnRef.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 175, 55)
            end
            if not startBendyDive(bendyHudBtnRef) then
                bendyDiveActive = false
                if bendyHudBtnRef then
                    bendyHudBtnRef.Text = "OFF"
                    bendyHudBtnRef.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 55, 45)
                end
                return false
            end
        end
    else
        if bendyDiveActive then
            bendyDiveActive = false
            if bendyHudBtnRef then
                bendyHudBtnRef.Text = "OFF"
                bendyHudBtnRef.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 55, 45)
            end
            stopBendyDive(true)
        end
    end
    return true
end

_G.PH_IsDiving = function() return bendyDiveActive end
_G.PH_GetDiveRoot = function()
    if bendyDiveActive and bendyProxyRig and bendyProxyRig.Parent then
        return bendyProxyRig:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    return nil
end

end
end

local draftPointA = nil
local draftPointB = nil
local draftMarkerA = nil
local draftMarkerB = nil
local editingBoxData = nil

local function getBoxColorByRule(rule)
    if rule == "+" then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    elseif rule == "-" then return Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
    else return Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255) end
end

local function createZoneMarker(position, labelText, color)
    local marker = Instance.new("Part")
    marker.Name = "ZoneMarker_" .. tostring(labelText)
    marker.Size = Vector3.new(4, 4, 4)
    marker.Position = position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
    marker.Anchored = true
    marker.CanCollide = false
    marker.CanQuery = false
    marker.CanTouch = false
    marker.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    marker.Transparency = 0.35
    marker.Color = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    marker.Parent = workspace

    local bbg = Instance.new("BillboardGui", marker)
    bbg.Name = "MarkerTitleGui"
    bbg.AlwaysOnTop = true
    bbg.MaxDistance = 15000
    bbg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 36)
    bbg.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bbg)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = "📍 " .. tostring(labelText)
    lbl.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    lbl.TextSize = 18

    return marker
end

local function buildBoxVisual(ptA, ptB, boxTitle, ruleStr)
    local sizeX = math.abs(ptA.X - ptB.X) + 10
    local sizeZ = math.abs(ptA.Z - ptB.Z) + 10
    local center = Vector3.new((ptA.X + ptB.X)/2, (ptA.Y + ptB.Y)/2, (ptA.Z + ptB.Z)/2)
    local col = getBoxColorByRule(ruleStr)

    local part = Instance.new("Part")
    part.Name = "ZoneBoxVisual_" .. boxTitle
    part.Size = Vector3.new(sizeX, 20, sizeZ)
    part.Position = center
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.Transparency = 0.8
    part.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
    part.Color = col
    part.Parent = workspace

    local sel = Instance.new("SelectionBox", part)
    sel.Name = "BoxSel"
    sel.Adornee = part
    sel.Color3 = col
    sel.LineThickness = 0.08

    local bbg = Instance.new("BillboardGui", part)
    bbg.Name = "BoxTitleGui"
    bbg.AlwaysOnTop = true
    bbg.MaxDistance = 15000
    bbg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 50)
    bbg.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 14, 0)

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bbg)
    lbl.Name = "TitleLbl"
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = "📦 " .. boxTitle
    lbl.TextColor3 = col
    lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    lbl.TextSize = 20

    task.spawn(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        while part and part.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local dist = (cam.CFrame.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
            lbl.TextSize = math.clamp(18 + (dist / 40), 18, 95)
            bbg.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.clamp(240 + (dist / 8), 240, 1200), 0, math.clamp(45 + (dist / 30), 45, 250))
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)

    return part
end

local function updateVisualColor(bData)
    if bData.Visual then
        local col = getBoxColorByRule(bData.Rule)
        bData.Visual.Color = col
        local sel = bData.Visual:FindFirstChild("BoxSel")
        if sel then sel.Color3 = col end
        local gui = bData.Visual:FindFirstChild("BoxTitleGui")
        if gui and gui:FindFirstChild("TitleLbl") then gui.TitleLbl.TextColor3 = col end
    end
end

local function updateCreatorUI()
    if editingBoxData then
        nameBox.TextEditable = false
        nameBox.Text = editingBoxData.Name
        saveBoxBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        saveBoxBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        if not editingBoxData.PointA then
            statusLbl.Text = "Восстановите Точку А для: " .. editingBoxData.Name
            btnA.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 50)
            btnA.Text = "📍 Поставить А"
            btnA.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            btnB.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
            btnB.Text = "📍 Точка Б (Есть)"
            btnB.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        elseif not editingBoxData.PointB then
            statusLbl.Text = "Восстановите Точку Б для: " .. editingBoxData.Name
            btnA.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
            btnA.Text = "📍 Точка А (Есть)"
            btnA.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
            btnB.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 50)
            btnB.Text = "📍 Поставить Б"
            btnB.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end
        return
    end

    if not draftPointA then
        statusLbl.Text = "Шаг 1: Поставьте Точку А"
        btnA.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 50)
        btnA.Text = "📍 Точка А"
        btnA.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        btnB.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        btnB.Text = "📍 Точка Б (Блок)"
        btnB.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        nameBox.TextEditable = false
        nameBox.Text = ""
        nameBox.PlaceholderText = "Сначала поставьте А и Б..."
        saveBoxBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        saveBoxBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
    elseif draftPointA and not draftPointB then
        statusLbl.Text = "Шаг 2: Поставьте Точку Б (А заблокирована)"
        btnA.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        btnA.Text = "📍 Точка А (Блок)"
        btnA.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        btnB.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 50)
        btnB.Text = "📍 Точка Б"
        btnB.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        nameBox.TextEditable = false
        nameBox.PlaceholderText = "Теперь поставьте Точку Б..."
    else
        statusLbl.Text = "Шаг 3: Введите название (Без имени не работает)"
        btnA.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        btnA.Text = "📍 Точка А (Блок)"
        btnA.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        btnB.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        btnB.Text = "📍 Точка Б (Блок)"
        btnB.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        nameBox.TextEditable = true
        nameBox.PlaceholderText = "Введите название коробки..."
        saveBoxBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200)
        saveBoxBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    end
end

local boxesScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", zonesPage)
boxesScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 135)
boxesScroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22)
boxesScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
boxesScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4
Instance.new("UICorner", boxesScroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

local boxesLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", boxesScroll)
boxesLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
boxesLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
    boxesScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, boxesLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)
end)

local function hasAnotherPlusBox(excludeBox)
    for _, item in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes) do
        if item ~= excludeBox and item.Rule == "+" then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local refreshBoxesList
refreshBoxesList = function()
    for _, ch in pairs(boxesScroll:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("Frame") then ch:Destroy() end
    end

    for idx, bData in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes) do
        local row = Instance.new("Frame", boxesScroll)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 32)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
        Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

        local ruleBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        ruleBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
        ruleBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -12)
        ruleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
        ruleBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        ruleBtn.TextSize = 15
        Instance.new("UICorner", ruleBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)

        local boxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", ruleBtn)
        boxStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        boxStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

        local function syncRuleBtnStyle()
            ruleBtn.Text = bData.Rule or ""
            if bData.Rule == "+" then
                ruleBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
                boxStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
            elseif bData.Rule == "-" then
                ruleBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
                boxStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
            else
                ruleBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
                boxStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90)
            end
        end
        syncRuleBtnStyle()

        ruleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local cur = bData.Rule or ""
            if cur == "" then
                bData.Rule = hasAnotherPlusBox(bData) and "-" or "+"
            elseif cur == "+" then
                bData.Rule = "-"
            else
                bData.Rule = ""
            end
            syncRuleBtnStyle()
            updateVisualColor(bData)
        end)

        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -165, 1, 0)
        title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 0)
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        title.Text = (bData.Active and "" or "⚠️ ") .. bData.Name
        title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        title.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
        title.TextSize = 13
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

        local delABtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        delABtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 22)
        delABtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0.5, -11)
        delABtn.BackgroundColor3 = bData.PointA and Color3.fromRGB(160, 110, 30) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        delABtn.Text = "Уд. А"
        delABtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        delABtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        delABtn.TextSize = 10
        Instance.new("UICorner", delABtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        delABtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not bData.PointA or editingBoxData then return end
            bData.PointA = nil
            bData.Active = false
            if bData.MarkerA then bData.MarkerA:Destroy() bData.MarkerA = nil end
            if bData.Visual then bData.Visual:Destroy() bData.Visual = nil end
            editingBoxData = bData
            updateCreatorUI()
            refreshBoxesList()
        end)

        local delBBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        delBBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 22)
        delBBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0.5, -11)
        delBBtn.BackgroundColor3 = bData.PointB and Color3.fromRGB(40, 110, 160) or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        delBBtn.Text = "Уд. Б"
        delBBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        delBBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        delBBtn.TextSize = 10
        Instance.new("UICorner", delBBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        delBBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not bData.PointB or editingBoxData then return end
            bData.PointB = nil
            bData.Active = false
            if bData.MarkerB then bData.MarkerB:Destroy() bData.MarkerB = nil end
            if bData.Visual then bData.Visual:Destroy() bData.Visual = nil end
            editingBoxData = bData
            updateCreatorUI()
            refreshBoxesList()
        end)

        local delAllBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        delAllBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 22)
        delAllBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, -11)
        delAllBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 40, 40)
        delAllBtn.Text = "🗑️"
        delAllBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        delAllBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        delAllBtn.TextSize = 11
        Instance.new("UICorner", delAllBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        delAllBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if bData.MarkerA then bData.MarkerA:Destroy() end
            if bData.MarkerB then bData.MarkerB:Destroy() end
            if bData.Visual then bData.Visual:Destroy() end
            if editingBoxData == bData then editingBoxData = nil end
            table.remove(_G.SavedZoneBoxes, idx)
            updateCreatorUI()
            refreshBoxesList()
        end)
    end
end

-- =========================================================
-- ----- КОНЕЦ ЧАСТИ 9.2 -----
-- =========================================================
-- =========================================================
-- ----- НАЧАЛО ЧАСТИ 9.3 -----
-- =========================================================

btnA.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local char = Player.Character
    local root = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) or (godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
    if not root then return end
    if editingBoxData and not editingBoxData.PointA then
        editingBoxData.PointA = root.Position
        editingBoxData.MarkerA = createZoneMarker(editingBoxData.PointA, editingBoxData.Name .. " [А]", Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60))
        editingBoxData.Visual = buildBoxVisual(editingBoxData.PointA, editingBoxData.PointB, editingBoxData.Name, editingBoxData.Rule)
        editingBoxData.Active = true
        editingBoxData = nil
        updateCreatorUI()
        refreshBoxesList()
        return
    end
    if not editingBoxData and not draftPointA then
        draftPointA = root.Position
        if draftMarkerA then draftMarkerA:Destroy() end
        draftMarkerA = createZoneMarker(draftPointA, "ЧЕРНОВИК: ТОЧКА А", Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60))
        updateCreatorUI()
    end
end)

btnB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local char = Player.Character
    local root = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) or (godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
    if not root then return end
    if editingBoxData and not editingBoxData.PointB then
        editingBoxData.PointB = root.Position
        editingBoxData.MarkerB = createZoneMarker(editingBoxData.PointB, editingBoxData.Name .. " [Б]", Color3.fromRGB(60, 220, 255))
        editingBoxData.Visual = buildBoxVisual(editingBoxData.PointA, editingBoxData.PointB, editingBoxData.Name, editingBoxData.Rule)
        editingBoxData.Active = true
        editingBoxData = nil
        updateCreatorUI()
        refreshBoxesList()
        return
    end
    if not editingBoxData and draftPointA and not draftPointB then
        draftPointB = root.Position
        if draftMarkerB then draftMarkerB:Destroy() end
        draftMarkerB = createZoneMarker(draftPointB, "ЧЕРНОВИК: ТОЧКА Б", Color3.fromRGB(60, 220, 255))
        updateCreatorUI()
    end
end)

local function finalizeNewBox()
    if editingBoxData or not draftPointA or not draftPointB then return end
    local boxName = nameBox.Text:gsub("^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
    if boxName == "" then return end
    if draftMarkerA then draftMarkerA:Destroy() draftMarkerA = nil end
    if draftMarkerB then draftMarkerB:Destroy() draftMarkerB = nil end
    local mA = createZoneMarker(draftPointA, boxName .. " [А]", Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60))
    local mB = createZoneMarker(draftPointB, boxName .. " [Б]", Color3.fromRGB(60, 220, 255))
    local vis = buildBoxVisual(draftPointA, draftPointB, boxName, "")
    table.insert(_G.SavedZoneBoxes, {Name = boxName, PointA = draftPointA, PointB = draftPointB, MarkerA = mA, MarkerB = mB, Visual = vis, Rule = "", Active = true})
    draftPointA, draftPointB, nameBox.Text = nil, nil, ""
    updateCreatorUI()
    refreshBoxesList()
end

saveBoxBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(finalizeNewBox)
nameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed) if enterPressed then finalizeNewBox() end end)

toggleZonesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    zonesEnabled = not zonesEnabled
    toggleZonesBtn.Text = zonesEnabled and "Зоны: ВКЛ (+ внутри / - снаружи)" or "Зоны: ВЫКЛ"
    toggleZonesBtn.BackgroundColor3 = zonesEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(50, 180, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50)
    if zonesEnabled then
        task.spawn(function()
            while zonesEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
                if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                    local char = Player.Character
                    local hrp = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) or (godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                    if hrp then
                        for _, bData in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes) do
                            if bData.Active and bData.PointA and bData.PointB and (bData.Rule == "+" or bData.Rule == "-") then
                                local minX = math.min(bData.PointA.X, bData.PointB.X) - 5
                                local maxX = math.max(bData.PointA.X, bData.PointB.X) + 5
                                local midY = (bData.PointA.Y + bData.PointB.Y) / 2
                                local minY, maxY = midY - 10, midY + 10
                                local minZ = math.min(bData.PointA.Z, bData.PointB.Z) - 5
                                local maxZ = math.max(bData.PointA.Z, bData.PointB.Z) + 5
                                local pos = hrp.Position
                                local isInside = (pos.X >= minX and pos.X <= maxX) and (pos.Y >= minY and pos.Y <= maxY) and (pos.Z >= minZ and pos.Z <= maxZ)

                                if bData.Rule == "+" and not isInside then
                                    local clamped = Vector3.new(math.clamp(pos.X, minX + 1.5, maxX - 1.5), math.clamp(pos.Y, minY + 1.5, maxY - 1.5), math.clamp(pos.Z, minZ + 1.5, maxZ - 1.5))
                                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(clamped) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
                                elseif bData.Rule == "-" and isInside then
                                    local dL, dR, dT, dB = math.abs(pos.X - minX), math.abs(maxX - pos.X), math.abs(pos.Z - minZ), math.abs(maxZ - pos.Z)
                                    local mD = math.min(dL, dR, dT, dB)
                                    local pushPos = (mD == dL and Vector3.new(minX - 6, pos.Y, pos.Z)) or (mD == dR and Vector3.new(maxX + 6, pos.Y, pos.Z)) or (mD == dT and Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y, minZ - 6)) or Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y, maxZ + 6)
                                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(pushPos) * (hrp.CFrame - hrp.CFrame.Position)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
        end)
    end
end)

refreshBoxesList()

local function recalculateOrders()
    orderCounter = 0
    local sorted = {}
    for _, pt in ipairs(savedPoints) do
        if pt.Order and pt.Order > 0 then table.insert(sorted, pt) end
    end
    table.sort(sorted, function(a,b) return a.Order < b.Order end)
    for i, pt in ipairs(sorted) do
        pt.Order = i
        if pt.NumBox then pt.NumBox.Text = tostring(i) end
        orderCounter = i
    end
end

local refreshPointList
refreshPointList = function()
    for _, child in pairs(pointScrollFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then child:Destroy() end
    end
    for index, pt in ipairs(savedPoints) do
        local pFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
        pFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
        pFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
        pFrame.Parent = pointScrollFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", pFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

        local numBox = Instance.new("TextButton")
        numBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
        numBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -12)
        numBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
        numBox.Text = pt.Order and tostring(pt.Order) or ""
        numBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
        numBox.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        numBox.TextSize = 13
        numBox.Parent = pFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", numBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        pt.NumBox = numBox
        numBox.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if pt.Order and pt.Order > 0 then
                pt.Order = nil
                numBox.Text = ""
                recalculateOrders()
            else
                orderCounter = orderCounter + 1
                pt.Order = orderCounter
                numBox.Text = tostring(orderCounter)
            end
        end)

        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -140, 1, 0)
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 0)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.Text = pt.Name
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        nameLabel.TextSize = 13
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
        nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLabel.Parent = pFrame

        local tpBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        tpBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 22)
        tpBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -85, 0.5, -11)
        tpBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 100, 160)
        tpBtn.Text = "🌀"
        tpBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        tpBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        tpBtn.TextSize = 11
        tpBtn.Parent = pFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", tpBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        tpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if invisActive and ghostSimulationCube then ghostSimulationCube.Position = pt.Pos
            elseif godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube then godGroundCube.Position = pt.Pos
            else
                local char = Player.Character
                if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then char.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(pt.Pos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) end
            end
        end)

        local delBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        delBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 22)
        delBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -42, 0.5, -11)
        delBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 40, 40)
        delBtn.Text = "🗑️"
        delBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        delBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        delBtn.TextSize = 11
        delBtn.Parent = pFrame
        Instance.new("UICorner", delBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        delBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if pt.Visual then pt.Visual:Destroy() end
            table.remove(savedPoints, index)
            recalculateOrders()
            refreshPointList()
        end)
    end
end

addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then
        local currentPos = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) and ghostSimulationCube.Position or ((godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube) and godGroundCube.Position or hrp.Position)
        if box.Text == "" then
            local maxNum = 0
            for _, pt in ipairs(savedPoints) do
                local num = tonumber(pt.Name:match("Точка%s+(%d+)"))
                if num and num > maxNum then maxNum = num end
            end
            savedNameTemp = "Точка " .. (maxNum + 1)
        else
            savedNameTemp = box.Text
        end

        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Size = Vector3.new(1.5, 1.5, 1.5)
        part.Position = currentPos
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
        part.Parent = workspace

        local adorn = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment", part)
        adorn.Size = part.Size
        adorn.Color3 = part.Color
        adorn.AlwaysOnTop = true
        adorn.ZIndex = 4
        adorn.Adornee = part

        local bbg = Instance.new("BillboardGui", part)
        bbg.AlwaysOnTop = true
        bbg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 40)
        bbg.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bbg)
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = savedNameTemp
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        lbl.TextSize = 16

        task.spawn(function()
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            while part and part.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
                local dist = (cam.CFrame.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                lbl.TextSize = math.clamp(14 + (dist / 120), 14, 45)
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
        end)

        table.insert(savedPoints, {Name = savedNameTemp, Pos = currentPos, Order = nil, Visual = part})
        local maxNum = 0
        for _, pt in ipairs(savedPoints) do
            local num = tonumber(pt.Name:match("Точка%s+(%d+)"))
            if num and num > maxNum then maxNum = num end
        end
        box.Text = ""
        savedNameTemp = "Точка " .. (maxNum + 1)
        refreshPointList()
    end
end)

    local i = #savedPoints
    while i >= 1 do
        if savedPoints[i].Order and savedPoints[i].Order > 0 then
            if savedPoints[i].Visual then savedPoints[i].Visual:Destroy() end
            table.remove(savedPoints, i)
        end
        i = i - 1
    end
    recalculateOrders()
    refreshPointList()
end)

local function moveToDest(hrp, dest, abortFn)
    if isTeleportMode then
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
        return
    end
    local wasAnc = hrp.Anchored
    if not dropOnPoint then hrp.Anchored = true end
    while hrp and hrp.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
        if abortFn and abortFn() then break end
        local diff = dest - hrp.Position
        local dist = diff.Magnitude
        if dist <= 1.8 then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest)
            break
        end
        local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position + diff.Unit * math.min(dist, ziplineSpeedValue * dt))
    end
    if hrp and hrp.Parent and not invisActive then hrp.Anchored = wasAnc end
end

local ziplineActive = false
runZiplineBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if ziplineActive then ziplineActive = false return end
    local char = Player.Character
    local hrp = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) or (godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube) or (char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
    if not hrp then return end

    ziplineActive = true
    runZiplineBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 100, 20)
    runZiplineBtn.Text = "🛑 Стоп"

    task.spawn(function()
        repeat
            local ordered = {}
            for _, pt in ipairs(savedPoints) do
                if pt.Order and pt.Order > 0 then table.insert(ordered, pt) end
            end
            table.sort(ordered, function(a, b) return a.Order < b.Order end)
            if #ordered == 0 then break end

            for _, pt in ipairs(ordered) do
                if not ziplineActive or _G.ScriptDestroyed then break end
                local r = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) or (godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube) or (Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                if not r then break end
                moveToDest(r, pt.Pos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), function() return not ziplineActive end)
                if ziplineDelayValue > 0 and ziplineActive then task.wait(ziplineDelayValue) end
            end
            task.wait(0.05)
        until not isZiplineLoop or not ziplineActive or _G.ScriptDestroyed

        ziplineActive = false
        runZiplineBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 180, 40)
        runZiplineBtn.Text = "🚀 Старт"
    end)
end)

local function bindDeathTracker(char)
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    if hum and hrp then
        hum.Died:Connect(function() if hrp and hrp.Parent then lastDeathPos = hrp.Position end end)
    end
end
if Player.Character then task.spawn(bindDeathTracker, Player.Character) end

Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
    task.spawn(bindDeathTracker, newChar)
    if not deathZiplineEnabled or _G.ScriptPaused or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
    local newHrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    if not newHrp then return end
    task.wait(0.45)

    local ordered = {}
    for _, pt in ipairs(savedPoints) do
        if pt.Order and pt.Order > 0 then table.insert(ordered, pt) end
    end
    table.sort(ordered, function(a, b) return a.Order < b.Order end)
    if #ordered == 0 then return end

    local backPos = lastDeathPos or newHrp.Position
    for _, pt in ipairs(ordered) do
        if not deathZiplineEnabled or _G.ScriptDestroyed or not newHrp.Parent then break end
        moveToDest(newHrp, pt.Pos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), function() return not deathZiplineEnabled end)
        if ziplineDelayValue > 0 then task.wait(ziplineDelayValue) end
    end
    if deathZiplineEnabled and newHrp.Parent and backPos then
        moveToDest(newHrp, backPos + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0), function() return not deathZiplineEnabled end)
    end
end)

refreshPointList()

-- =========================================================
-- ----- НАЧАЛО ЧАСТИ 11 (v3.0) -----
-- Конфиги / Копия мира / Шейдеры / Макро / Килл-аура
-- Весь код лежит внутри одной функции и не занимает локальные переменные основного скрипта.
-- =========================================================
xpcall(function()

local PH = {}
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local UIS3 = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local GuiService3 = game:GetService("GuiService")
local StarterGui3 = game:GetService("StarterGui")

PH.ROOT = "PromptHacker"
PH.CFG = "PromptHacker/configs"
PH.MAC = "PromptHacker/macros"
PH.settings = {
    shaderIdx = 1, shaderStrength = 1,
    killRange = 18, killDelay = 0.12,
}
PH.current = "default"
PH.skip = {}
PH.loading = false
PH.autosave = false

-- ---------- общие помощники ----------
function PH.notify(text)
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui3:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "⚡ Prompt v3.0", Text = tostring(text), Duration = 4})
    end)
end

function PH.corner(obj, r)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 5)
    c.Parent = obj
end

function PH.label(page, text, height, color)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height or 22)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    l.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    l.TextSize = 13
    l.TextWrapped = true
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.Parent = page
    return l
end

function PH.btn(page, text, color, cb)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    b.Text = text
    b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    b.TextSize = 13
    b.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    b.Parent = page
    PH.corner(b, 5)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
        cb(b)
    end)
    return b
end

function PH.makeList(page, height)
    local sf = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    sf.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
    sf.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 22)
    sf.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sf.ScrollBarThickness = 4
    sf.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    sf.Parent = page
    PH.corner(sf, 6)
    local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    lay.Parent = sf
    lay:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        sf.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 6)
    end)
    return sf
end

function PH.clearList(sf)
    for _, c in ipairs(sf:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy() end
    end
end

function PH.addRow(sf, text, onMain, onDel)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 28)
    row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
    row.Parent = sf
    PH.corner(row, 5)

    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.new(1, onDel and -34 or -4, 1, -4)
    b.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
    b.Text = text
    b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    b.TextSize = 12
    b.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    b.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    b.Parent = row
    PH.corner(b, 4)
    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
        onMain()
    end)

    if onDel then
        local d = Instance.new("TextButton")
        d.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 1, -4)
        d.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 2)
        d.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50)
        d.Text = "✕"
        d.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        d.TextSize = 13
        d.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        d.Parent = row
        PH.corner(d, 4)
        d.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
            onDel()
        end)
    end
    return row
end

-- ---------- файлы (папка PromptHacker) ----------
function PH.hasFs()
    return type(writefile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function"
        and type(isfile) == "function" and type(makefolder) == "function" and type(isfolder) == "function"
end

function PH.ensureDirs()
    if not PH.hasFs() then return false end
    pcall(function()
        for _, d in ipairs({PH.ROOT, PH.CFG, PH.MAC}) do
            if not isfolder(d) then makefolder(d) end
        end
    end)
    return true
end

function PH.write(path, tbl)
    if not PH.ensureDirs() then
        PH.notify("Нет доступа к файлам (writefile) в этом экзекьюторе")
        return false
    end
    local ok, err = pcall(function() writefile(path, HttpService:JSONEncode(tbl)) end)
    if not ok then PH.notify("Ошибка записи: " .. tostring(err)) end
    return ok
end

function PH.read(path)
    if not PH.hasFs() then return nil end
    local ok, res = pcall(function()
        if not isfile(path) then return nil end
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path))
    end)
    if ok then return res end
    return nil
end

function PH.listNames(dir)
    local names = {}
    if not PH.hasFs() or type(listfiles) ~= "function" then return names end
    pcall(function()
        if not isfolder(dir) then return end
        for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(dir)) do
            local n = tostring(f):match('([^/\\]+)%.json$')
            if n then table.insert(names, n) end
        end
    end)
    table.sort(names)
    return names
end

function PH.delFile(path)
    pcall(function()
        if type(delfile) == "function" and isfile(path) then delfile(path) end
    end)
end

function PH.safeName(n)
    n = tostring(n or "")
    n = n:gsub('[/\\:*?"<>|%c]', "")
    n = n:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    if n == "" then n = "default" end
    return n
end

function PH.autoPath() return PH.CFG .. "/autoload.txt" end

function PH.getAutoload()
    if not PH.hasFs() then return nil end
    local res
