    pcall(function()
        local p = PH.autoPath()
        if isfile(p) then
            local t = readfile(p):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
            if t ~= "" then res = t end
        end
    end)
    return res
end

function PH.setAutoload(name)
    if not PH.hasFs() then return end
    pcall(function()
        PH.ensureDirs()
        writefile(PH.autoPath(), name or "")
    end)
end

function PH.v3(v) return {v.X, v.Y, v.Z} end
function PH.toV3(t) return Vector3.new(t[1] or 0, t[2] or 0, t[3] or 0) end

function PH.click(btn)
    if not btn then return end
    local ok = false
    if type(firesignal) == "function" then ok = pcall(firesignal, btn.MouseButton1Click) end
    if not ok and type(getconnections) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(btn.MouseButton1Click)) do c:Fire() end
        end)
    end
end

-- корень персонажа, которым ты реально управляешь (учитывает бога/инвиз/чернильный дайвинг)
function PH.myRoot()
    local dive = _G.PH_GetDiveRoot and _G.PH_GetDiveRoot()
    if dive then return dive, true end
    local r = (invisActive and ghostSimulationCube) or (godmodeEnabled and godGroundCube)
    if r then return r, false end
    local c = Player.Character
    return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), false
end

local OWN_PREFIXES = {"PromptHitbox", "PromptCopy_", "PromptWorldCopies", "PromptShader", "Phantom_", "Bunker_",
    "ZoneMarker_", "ZoneBoxVisual_", "Cushion_", "BendySurfacePuppet"}

function PH.isOwn(obj)
    local roots = {godAirPlatform, godGroundCube, godVisualClone, ghostSimulationCube, ghostVisualClone, zoneBoxVisual, markerA, markerB}
    for i = 1, 8 do
        local root = roots[i]
        if root and (obj == root or obj:IsDescendantOf(root)) then return true end
    end
    local cur = obj
    while cur and cur ~= workspace do
        local n = cur.Name
        for _, pre in ipairs(OWN_PREFIXES) do
            if n:sub(1, #pre) == pre then return true end
        end
        cur = cur.Parent
    end
    return false
end

function PH.inCharacter(obj)
    local m = obj:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    while m do
        if m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
        m = m:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    end
    return false
end

PH.ensureDirs()

-- =========================================================
-- 💾 КОНФИГИ (коробки + точки + включённые тумблеры)
-- =========================================================
function PH.collect()
    local data = {version = 3, toggles = {}, boxes = {}, points = {}, settings = PH.settings,
        zonesEnabled = zonesEnabled and true or false}
    for name, api in pairs(_G.PH_Toggles or {}) do
        if not PH.skip[name] then
            local ok, v = pcall(api.Get)
            if ok and v then data.toggles[name] = true end
        end
    end
    for _, b in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes or {}) do
        if b.PointA and b.PointB then
            table.insert(data.boxes, {name = b.Name, a = PH.v3(b.PointA), b = PH.v3(b.PointB),
                rule = b.Rule or "", active = (b.Active ~= false)})
        end
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(savedPoints or {}) do
        if p.Pos then
            table.insert(data.points, {name = p.Name, pos = PH.v3(p.Pos), order = p.Order})
        end
    end
    return data
end

function PH.applyBoxes(list)
    for _, b in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes) do
        pcall(function()
            if b.Visual then b.Visual:Destroy() end
            if b.MarkerA then b.MarkerA:Destroy() end
            if b.MarkerB then b.MarkerB:Destroy() end
        end)
    end
    table.clear(_G.SavedZoneBoxes)
    for _, d in ipairs(list or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            local a, b = PH.toV3(d.a), PH.toV3(d.b)
            local name = tostring(d.name or "Коробка")
            local mA = createZoneMarker(a, name .. " [А]", Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60))
            local mB = createZoneMarker(b, name .. " [Б]", Color3.fromRGB(60, 220, 255))
            local vis = buildBoxVisual(a, b, name, d.rule or "")
            table.insert(_G.SavedZoneBoxes, {Name = name, PointA = a, PointB = b, MarkerA = mA, MarkerB = mB,
                Visual = vis, Rule = d.rule or "", Active = (d.active ~= false)})
        end)
    end
    pcall(refreshBoxesList)
end

function PH.makePointVisual(name, pos)
    local part = Instance.new("Part")
    part.Size = Vector3.new(1.5, 1.5, 1.5)
    part.Position = pos
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    part.Parent = workspace

    local adorn = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment", part)
    adorn.Size = part.Size
    adorn.Color3 = part.Color
    adorn.AlwaysOnTop = true
    adorn.ZIndex = 4
    adorn.Adornee = part

    local bbg = Instance.new("BillboardGui", part)
    bbg.AlwaysOnTop = true
    bbg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 40)
    bbg.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", bbg)
    lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    lbl.Text = name
    lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
    lbl.TextSize = 16

    task.spawn(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        while part and part.Parent and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            local dist = (cam.CFrame.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
            lbl.TextSize = math.clamp(14 + (dist / 120), 14, 45)
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)
    return part
end

function PH.applyPoints(list)
    for _, pt in ipairs(savedPoints) do
        pcall(function() if pt.Visual then pt.Visual:Destroy() end end)
    end
    table.clear(savedPoints)
    for _, d in ipairs(list or {}) do
        pcall(function()
            local pos = PH.toV3(d.pos)
            local name = tostring(d.name or "Точка")
            local vis = PH.makePointVisual(name, pos)
            table.insert(savedPoints, {Name = name, Pos = pos, Order = tonumber(d.order), Visual = vis})
        end)
    end
    pcall(recalculateOrders)
    pcall(refreshPointList)
end

function PH.applyToggles(map)
    for name, api in pairs(_G.PH_Toggles or {}) do
        if not PH.skip[name] then
            local want = (map[name] == true)
            local ok, cur = pcall(api.Get)
            if ok and cur ~= want then
                pcall(api.Apply, want)
                task.wait(0.12)
            end
        end
    end
end

function PH.loadConfig(name)
    name = PH.safeName(name)
    local data = PH.read(PH.CFG .. "/" .. name .. ".json")
    if type(data) ~= "table" then
        PH.notify("Конфиг не найден: " .. name)
        return false
    end
    PH.loading = true
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        if type(data.settings) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(data.settings) do PH.settings[k] = v end
        end
        PH.applyBoxes(data.boxes)
        PH.applyPoints(data.points)
        PH.applyToggles(type(data.toggles) == "table" and data.toggles or {})
        local want = (data.zonesEnabled == true)
        if want ~= (zonesEnabled and true or false) then PH.click(toggleZonesBtn) end
        if PH.updateShaderBtn then PH.updateShaderBtn() end
    end)
    PH.loading = false
    if ok then
        PH.notify("Конфиг загружен: " .. name)
    else
        PH.notify("Ошибка загрузки: " .. tostring(err))
    end
    return ok
end

function PH.saveConfig(name)
    name = PH.safeName(name)
    local ok = PH.write(PH.CFG .. "/" .. name .. ".json", PH.collect())
    if ok then
        PH.notify("Конфиг сохранён: " .. name)
        if PH.getAutoload() then PH.setAutoload(name) end
        if PH.refreshCfgList then PH.refreshCfgList() end
    end
    return ok
end

local cfgPage = createTab("💾 Конфиг")
PH.label(cfgPage, "💾 Конфиги: коробки, точки и включённые тумблеры. Папка: PromptHacker/configs", 34)
createInput(cfgPage, "Имя конфига", "default", function(t) PH.current = PH.safeName(t) end)
PH.btn(cfgPage, "💾 Сохранить конфиг", Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200), function() PH.saveConfig(PH.current) end)
PH.btn(cfgPage, "📂 Загрузить конфиг", Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 80), function() task.spawn(PH.loadConfig, PH.current) end)
PH.btn(cfgPage, "🗑 Удалить конфиг", Color3.fromRGB(170, 50, 50), function()
    PH.delFile(PH.CFG .. "/" .. PH.current .. ".json")
    PH.notify("Удалён: " .. PH.current)
    if PH.refreshCfgList then PH.refreshCfgList() end
end)

PH.skip["🔁 Автозагрузка при запуске"] = true
PH.skip["⏱ Автосохранение (каждые 30 с)"] = true
local autoTg = createToggle(cfgPage, "🔁 Автозагрузка при запуске", function(v)
    PH.setAutoload(v and PH.current or nil)
    if v then PH.saveConfig(PH.current) end
end)
createToggle(cfgPage, "⏱ Автосохранение (каждые 30 с)", function(v) PH.autosave = v end)
if PH.getAutoload() then autoTg.Set(true) end

PH.label(cfgPage, "Сохранённые конфиги (нажми — загрузить, ✕ — удалить):", 20, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
PH.cfgList = PH.makeList(cfgPage, 120)
function PH.refreshCfgList()
    PH.clearList(PH.cfgList)
    for _, n in ipairs(PH.listNames(PH.CFG)) do
        PH.addRow(PH.cfgList, n, function()
            PH.current = n
            task.spawn(PH.loadConfig, n)
        end, function()
            PH.delFile(PH.CFG .. "/" .. n .. ".json")
            PH.refreshCfgList()
        end)
    end
end
PH.btn(cfgPage, "🔄 Обновить список", Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45), function() PH.refreshCfgList() end)
PH.refreshCfgList()

task.spawn(function()
    while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
        for _ = 1, 60 do
            if _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
        if PH.autosave and not PH.loading then PH.saveConfig(PH.current) end
    end
end)

-- =========================================================
-- 🌍 WORLD COPY (во вкладке «Зоны»): копия всего внутри коробки + вставка куда хочешь + удаление копий
-- Копии клиентские (видишь только ты). Terrain (ландшафт) скопировать нельзя.
-- =========================================================
PH.clip = nil
PH.COPY_HEIGHT = 60 -- высота копируемого объёма над нижней гранью коробки (студы); поменяй здесь, если нужно выше/ниже
PH.copies = {}
PH.copyCounter = 0
PH.pasteArmed = false

function PH.copyFolder()
    local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("PromptWorldCopies")
    if not f then
        f = Instance.new("Folder")
        f.Name = "PromptWorldCopies"
        f.Parent = workspace
    end
    return f
end

function PH.copyBox(b)
    if not b or not b.PointA or not b.PointB then return end
    local A, B = b.PointA, b.PointB
    local minX, maxX = math.min(A.X, B.X) - 5, math.max(A.X, B.X) + 5
    local minZ, maxZ = math.min(A.Z, B.Z) - 5, math.max(A.Z, B.Z) + 5
    local minY = (A.Y + B.Y) / 2 - 10
    local h = PH.COPY_HEIGHT
    local size = Vector3.new(maxX - minX, h, maxZ - minZ)
    local center = Vector3.new((minX + maxX) / 2, minY + h / 2, (minZ + maxZ) / 2)
    local origin = CFrame.new((minX + maxX) / 2, minY, (minZ + maxZ) / 2)

    PH.notify("Копирую «" .. tostring(b.Name) .. "»...")
    local params = OverlapParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {}
    local parts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox(CFrame.new(center), size, params)
    local limitAxis = math.max(size.X, size.Z, h) * 3
    local items = {}

    for i, p in ipairs(parts) do
        if #items >= 4000 then break end
        if p:IsA("BasePart") and not p:IsA("Terrain") and not PH.isOwn(p) and not PH.inCharacter(p) then
            local s = p.Size
            if math.max(s.X, s.Y, s.Z) <= limitAxis then
                local ok, clone = pcall(function() return p:Clone() end)
                if ok and clone then
                    for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") or d:IsA("JointInstance") or d:IsA("Constraint")
                            or d:IsA("WeldConstraint") or d:IsA("BodyMover") or d:IsA("ProximityPrompt")
                            or d:IsA("ClickDetector") or d:IsA("Sound") then
                            d:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                    clone.Anchored = true
                    clone.Parent = nil
                    items[#items + 1] = {template = clone, rel = origin:ToObjectSpace(p.CFrame)}
                end
            end
        end
        if i % 150 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end

    if #items == 0 then
        PH.notify("В коробке нечего копировать (деталей: 0)")
        return
    end
    PH.clip = {name = tostring(b.Name), items = items, radius = math.max(size.X, size.Z) / 2, height = h}
    if PH.clipLabel then PH.clipLabel.Text = "Буфер: «" .. PH.clip.name .. "» — деталей: " .. #items end
    PH.notify("Скопировано деталей: " .. #items)
end

function PH.paste(pos)
    if not PH.clip then
        PH.notify("Сначала скопируй коробку")
        return
    end
    PH.copyCounter = PH.copyCounter + 1
    local rootCf = CFrame.new(pos)
    local model = Instance.new("Model")
    model.Name = "PromptCopy_" .. PH.copyCounter
    model.Parent = PH.copyFolder()
    for i, item in ipairs(PH.clip.items) do
        local p = item.template:Clone()
        p.CFrame = rootCf * item.rel
        p.Anchored = true
        p.Parent = model
        if i % 200 == 0 then task.wait() end
    end
    table.insert(PH.copies, {name = "Копия " .. PH.copyCounter .. " (" .. PH.clip.name .. ")", model = model})
    if PH.refreshCopies then PH.refreshCopies() end
    PH.notify("Вставлено: Копия " .. PH.copyCounter)
end

PH.label(zonesPage, "🌍 КОПИРОВАТЬ / ВСТАВИТЬ — копия всего, что внутри коробки, с цветами и материалами (видишь только ты)", 46, Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255))
PH.label(zonesPage, "Коробка (нажми в списке, чтобы выбрать):", 20, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
PH.selBox = nil
PH.boxList = PH.makeList(zonesPage, 90)
function PH.refreshBoxCopyList()
    PH.clearList(PH.boxList)
    for _, b in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes or {}) do
        if b.PointA and b.PointB then
            local mark = (PH.selBox == b) and "✅ " or "▫️ "
            PH.addRow(PH.boxList, mark .. tostring(b.Name), function()
                PH.selBox = b
                PH.refreshBoxCopyList()
            end)
        end
    end
end

-- находит коробку, внутри которой стоишь (если ничего не выбрано вручную)
function PH.boxUnderMe()
    local r = PH.myRoot()
    if not r then return nil end
    local pos = r.Position
    for _, b in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes or {}) do
        if b.PointA and b.PointB then
            local minX, maxX = math.min(b.PointA.X, b.PointB.X) - 5, math.max(b.PointA.X, b.PointB.X) + 5
            local minZ, maxZ = math.min(b.PointA.Z, b.PointB.Z) - 5, math.max(b.PointA.Z, b.PointB.Z) + 5
            if pos.X >= minX and pos.X <= maxX and pos.Z >= minZ and pos.Z <= maxZ then return b end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

PH.btn(zonesPage, "📋 Копировать", Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200), function()
    local b = PH.selBox
    if b then
        local still = false
        for _, x in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes or {}) do if x == b then still = true break end end
        if not still then b = nil PH.selBox = nil end
    end
    if not b then
        local list = {}
        for _, x in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes or {}) do
            if x.PointA and x.PointB then list[#list + 1] = x end
        end
        if #list == 1 then b = list[1] else b = PH.boxUnderMe() end
    end
    if not b then
        PH.notify("Выбери коробку в списке выше (или встань внутрь неё)")
        return
    end
    task.spawn(PH.copyBox, b)
end)
PH.clipLabel = PH.label(zonesPage, "Буфер: пусто", 22, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
PH.btn(zonesPage, "📌 Вставить", Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 80), function()
    local r = PH.myRoot()
    if not r or not PH.clip then
        PH.notify("Сначала скопируй коробку")
        return
    end
    local look = r.CFrame.LookVector
    look = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
    if look.Magnitude < 0.01 then look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
    local pos = r.Position + look.Unit * (PH.clip.radius + 6) - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    task.spawn(PH.paste, pos)
end)
PH.armBtn = PH.btn(zonesPage, "🎯 Вставить по тапу (нажми и тапни в мире)", Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200), function(b)
    if not PH.clip then
        PH.notify("Сначала скопируй коробку")
        return
    end
    PH.pasteArmed = not PH.pasteArmed
    b.Text = PH.pasteArmed and "🎯 Тапни по месту вставки... (нажми ещё раз — отмена)" or "🎯 Вставить по тапу (нажми и тапни в мире)"
end)
PH.label(zonesPage, "Созданные копии (нажми — телепорт к копии, ✕ — удалить):", 20, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
PH.copyList = PH.makeList(zonesPage, 100)
function PH.refreshCopies()
    PH.clearList(PH.copyList)
    for idx, c in ipairs(PH.copies) do
        PH.addRow(PH.copyList, "📦 " .. c.name, function()
            local r = PH.myRoot()
            if r and c.model and c.model.Parent then
                local ok, cf, sz = pcall(function() return c.model:GetBoundingBox() end)
                if ok and cf then r.CFrame = CFrame.new(cf.Position + Vector3.new(0, sz.Y / 2 + 4, 0)) end
            end
        end, function()
            pcall(function() c.model:Destroy() end)
            table.remove(PH.copies, idx)
            PH.refreshCopies()
        end)
    end
end
PH.btn(zonesPage, "🗑 Удалить копии в выбранной коробке", Color3.fromRGB(170, 90, 40), function()
    local b = PH.selBox or PH.boxUnderMe()
    if not b or not b.PointA or not b.PointB then
        PH.notify("Выбери коробку в списке (или встань внутрь неё)")
        return
    end
    local minX, maxX = math.min(b.PointA.X, b.PointB.X) - 5, math.max(b.PointA.X, b.PointB.X) + 5
    local minZ, maxZ = math.min(b.PointA.Z, b.PointB.Z) - 5, math.max(b.PointA.Z, b.PointB.Z) + 5
    local removed = 0
    for i = #PH.copies, 1, -1 do
        local c = PH.copies[i]
        local ok, cf = pcall(function() return c.model:GetBoundingBox() end)
        if ok and cf then
            local p = cf.Position
            if p.X >= minX and p.X <= maxX and p.Z >= minZ and p.Z <= maxZ then
                pcall(function() c.model:Destroy() end)
                table.remove(PH.copies, i)
                removed = removed + 1
            end
        end
    end
    PH.refreshCopies()
    PH.notify(removed > 0 and ("Удалено копий в «" .. tostring(b.Name) .. "»: " .. removed)
        or ("В «" .. tostring(b.Name) .. "» копий нет"))
end)
PH.btn(zonesPage, "🗑 Удалить ВСЕ копии", Color3.fromRGB(170, 50, 50), function()
    for _, c in ipairs(PH.copies) do pcall(function() c.model:Destroy() end) end
    table.clear(PH.copies)
    PH.refreshCopies()
    PH.notify("Все копии удалены")
end)

UIS3.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
    if not PH.pasteArmed or gp or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
    local t = input.UserInputType
    if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
    PH.pasteArmed = false
    if PH.armBtn then PH.armBtn.Text = "🎯 Вставить по тапу (нажми и тапни в мире)" end
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local ray = cam:ViewportPointToRay(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
    local rp = RaycastParams.new()
    rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = {Player.Character, PH.copyFolder()}
    local res = workspace:Raycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 2000, rp)
    if res then
        task.spawn(PH.paste, res.Position)
    else
        PH.notify("Не попал по поверхности, попробуй ещё раз")
    end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    local lastSig = nil
    while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
        local parts = {}
        for _, b in ipairs(_G.SavedZoneBoxes or {}) do parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(b.Name) end
        local sig = table.concat(parts, "|")
        if sig ~= lastSig then
            lastSig = sig
            PH.refreshBoxCopyList()
        end
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

-- =========================================================
-- ✨ ШЕЙДЕРЫ (реалистичность)
-- =========================================================
PH.presetNames = {"Реализм", "Кино", "Бенди (сепия)", "Яркий", "Мрак"}
PH.presets = {
    {cc = {b = 0.02, c = 0.14, s = 0.14, t = Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 243)}, bloom = {i = 0.35, sz = 26, th = 0.9},
        rays = {i = 0.10, sp = 0.75}, dof = {f = 0.10, d = 90, r = 140}, lit = {e = 0.12, df = 1, sp = 1, soft = 0.25}},
    {cc = {b = -0.02, c = 0.22, s = -0.12, t = Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 215)}, bloom = {i = 0.45, sz = 30, th = 0.85},
        rays = {i = 0.08, sp = 0.7}, dof = {f = 0.18, d = 70, r = 90}, lit = {e = 0.05, df = 1, sp = 1, soft = 0.3}},
    {cc = {b = 0, c = 0.25, s = -0.7, t = Color3.fromRGB(255, 222, 175)}, bloom = {i = 0.5, sz = 32, th = 0.8},
        rays = {i = 0.05, sp = 0.6}, dof = {f = 0.22, d = 60, r = 80}, lit = {e = -0.1, df = 1, sp = 1, soft = 0.35}},
    {cc = {b = 0.04, c = 0.08, s = 0.38, t = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}, bloom = {i = 0.55, sz = 24, th = 0.85},
        rays = {i = 0.2, sp = 0.9}, dof = {f = 0.05, d = 120, r = 200}, lit = {e = 0.2, df = 1, sp = 1, soft = 0.2}},
    {cc = {b = -0.05, c = 0.25, s = -0.2, t = Color3.fromRGB(190, 200, 255)}, bloom = {i = 0.3, sz = 24, th = 0.9},
        rays = {i = 0.02, sp = 0.5}, dof = {f = 0.15, d = 70, r = 100}, lit = {e = -0.3, df = 1, sp = 1, soft = 0.3}},
}
PH.shaderOn = false
PH.orig = nil

function PH.getFx(class, name)
    local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(name)
    if e and not e:IsA(class) then
        e:Destroy()
        e = nil
    end
    if not e then
        e = Instance.new(class)
        e.Name = name
        e.Parent = Lighting
    end
    return e
end

function PH.applyShader()
    if not PH.orig then
        PH.orig = {e = Lighting.ExposureCompensation, df = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
            sp = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale, soft = Lighting.ShadowSoftness, sh = Lighting.GlobalShadows}
        pcall(function()
            if type(gethiddenproperty) == "function" then PH.orig.tech = gethiddenproperty(Lighting, "Technology") end
        end)
    end
    local idx = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(PH.settings.shaderIdx) or 1), 1, #PH.presets)
    local p = PH.presets[idx]
    local k = math.clamp(tonumber(PH.settings.shaderStrength) or 1, 0.1, 2)

    local cc = PH.getFx("ColorCorrectionEffect", "PromptShader_CC")
    cc.Brightness = p.cc.b * k
    cc.Contrast = p.cc.c * k
    cc.Saturation = p.cc.s * k
    cc.TintColor = p.cc.t

    local bl = PH.getFx("BloomEffect", "PromptShader_Bloom")
    bl.Intensity = p.bloom.i * k
    bl.Size = p.bloom.sz
    bl.Threshold = p.bloom.th

    local sr = PH.getFx("SunRaysEffect", "PromptShader_Rays")
    sr.Intensity = p.rays.i * k
    sr.Spread = p.rays.sp

    local dof = PH.getFx("DepthOfFieldEffect", "PromptShader_DoF")
    dof.FarIntensity = p.dof.f * k
    dof.NearIntensity = 0
    dof.FocusDistance = p.dof.d
    dof.InFocusRadius = p.dof.r

    Lighting.ExposureCompensation = p.lit.e * k
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = p.lit.df
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = p.lit.sp
    if not fullBrightEnabled then
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        Lighting.ShadowSoftness = p.lit.soft
    end
    pcall(function()
        if type(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then sethiddenproperty(Lighting, "Technology", Enum.Technology.Future) end
    end)
end

function PH.removeShader()
    for _, n in ipairs({"PromptShader_CC", "PromptShader_Bloom", "PromptShader_Rays", "PromptShader_DoF"}) do
        local e = Lighting:FindFirstChild(n)
        if e then e:Destroy() end
    end
    if PH.orig then
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = PH.orig.e
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = PH.orig.df
        Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = PH.orig.sp
        if not fullBrightEnabled then
            Lighting.ShadowSoftness = PH.orig.soft
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = PH.orig.sh
        end
        pcall(function()
            if PH.orig.tech and type(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then
                sethiddenproperty(Lighting, "Technology", PH.orig.tech)
            end
        end)
        PH.orig = nil
    end
end

local shPage = createTab("✨ Шейдеры")
PH.label(shPage, "✨ Шейдеры: цвет, блум, лучи солнца, глубина резкости, тени. Можно листать пресеты на лету.", 34)
createToggle(shPage, "✨ Шейдеры (реализм)", function(v)
    PH.shaderOn = v
    if not v then
        PH.removeShader()
        return
    end
    PH.applyShader()
    task.spawn(function()
        while PH.shaderOn and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if not Lighting:FindFirstChild("PromptShader_CC") then PH.applyShader() end
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end)
end)
PH.shaderBtn = PH.btn(shPage, "🎨 Пресет: Реализм (нажми — следующий)", Color3.fromRGB(110, 70, 160), function()
    local n = (math.floor(tonumber(PH.settings.shaderIdx) or 1) % #PH.presets) + 1
    PH.settings.shaderIdx = n
    PH.updateShaderBtn()
    if PH.shaderOn then PH.applyShader() end
end)
function PH.updateShaderBtn()
    local idx = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(PH.settings.shaderIdx) or 1), 1, #PH.presets)
    if PH.shaderBtn then PH.shaderBtn.Text = "🎨 Пресет: " .. PH.presetNames[idx] .. " (нажми — следующий)" end
    if PH.shaderOn then PH.applyShader() end
end
createInput(shPage, "Сила эффектов (0.1–2, дефолт 1)", "1", function(t)
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n then
        PH.settings.shaderStrength = math.clamp(n, 0.1, 2)
        if PH.shaderOn then PH.applyShader() end
    end
end)

-- =========================================================
-- ⚔ КИЛЛ АУРА
-- Ищет ближайшую цель в радиусе и бьёт оружием из рук (Tool): firetouchinterest + Activate.
-- В чернильном дайвинге вылезает на удар через модуль «Вылезать из дайвинга на удар» (его нужно включить).
-- =========================================================
PH.ka = {on = false, npcs = false, hitTeam = false, equip = true, face = false}
PH.npcCache = {}

function PH.refreshNpcs()
    local list = {}
    for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("Humanoid") and d.Health > 0 then
            local m = d.Parent
            if m and m:IsA("Model") and m ~= Player.Character and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) and not PH.isOwn(m) then
                local r = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m.PrimaryPart
                if r then list[#list + 1] = {model = m, hum = d, root = r} end
            end
        end
    end
    PH.npcCache = list
end

function PH.findTarget(pos, range)
    local best, bestD = nil, range
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Player and plr.Character then
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local r = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local sameTeam = (Player.Team ~= nil and plr.Team == Player.Team)
            if hum and r and hum.Health > 0 and (PH.ka.hitTeam or not sameTeam) then
                local d = (r.Position - pos).Magnitude
                if d <= bestD then best, bestD = r, d end
            end
        end
    end
    if PH.ka.npcs then
        for _, n in ipairs(PH.npcCache) do
            if n.hum and n.hum.Parent and n.hum.Health > 0 and n.root and n.root.Parent then
                local d = (n.root.Position - pos).Magnitude
                if d <= bestD then best, bestD = n.root, d end
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

function PH.attack(targetRoot)
    local char = Player.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if not tool and PH.ka.equip and hum then
        local bp = Player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        local t = bp and bp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if t then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(t) end)
            task.wait(0.1)
            tool = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        end
    end
    if not tool then return end

    local function swing()
        local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if handle and type(firetouchinterest) == "function" then
            pcall(firetouchinterest, handle, targetRoot, 0)
            pcall(firetouchinterest, handle, targetRoot, 1)
        end
        pcall(function() tool:Activate() end)
    end

    swing()
end

function PH.startKillAura()
    PH.ka.on = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local lastNpc = 0
        while PH.ka.on and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if not _G.ScriptPaused then
                local root, diving = PH.myRoot()
                local hum = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    if PH.ka.npcs and tick() - lastNpc > 5 then
                        lastNpc = tick()
                        PH.refreshNpcs()
                    end
                    local t = PH.findTarget(root.Position, tonumber(PH.settings.killRange) or 18)
                    if t then
                        if PH.ka.face and not diving then
                            pcall(function()
                                root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, Vector3.new(t.Position.X, root.Position.Y, t.Position.Z))
                            end)
                        end
                        PH.attack(t)
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(math.clamp(tonumber(PH.settings.killDelay) or 0.12, 0.05, 2))
        end
    end)
end

-- Вкладка Kill Aura удалена по запросу; боевые автодействия не запускаются.



-- =========================================================
-- 🎬 МАКРО / SMART-МАКРО (папка PromptHacker/macros)
-- Запись: клавиши, нажатия по экрану (тапы) и, по желанию, твой путь (позиция). Повтор с ограничениями.
-- =========================================================
PH.mac = {rec = false, play = false, events = {}, t0 = 0, recPos = false, held = {}, ptrDown = nil,
    name = "macro1", opts = {loops = 1, speed = 1, pause = 0.2, maxTime = 0, hpStop = 0, enemyR = 0}}

function PH.overMyUI(pos)
    for _, ch in ipairs(ScreenGui:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Visible then
            local p, s = ch.AbsolutePosition, ch.AbsoluteSize
            if pos.X >= p.X and pos.X <= p.X + s.X and pos.Y >= p.Y and pos.Y <= p.Y + s.Y then return true end
        end
    end
    return false
end

function PH.updateMacUi()
    local m = PH.mac
    if PH.macStatus then
        local st = m.rec and "● ЗАПИСЬ" or (m.play and "▶ ВОСПРОИЗВЕДЕНИЕ" or "ожидание")
        PH.macStatus.Text = "Статус: " .. st .. " | событий: " .. #m.events
    end
    if PH.recBtn then
        PH.recBtn.Text = m.rec and "⏹ Остановить запись" or "⏺ Начать запись"
        PH.recBtn.BackgroundColor3 = m.rec and Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200)
    end
    if PH.playBtn then
        PH.playBtn.Text = m.play and "⏹ Остановить макро" or "▶ Запустить макро"
        PH.playBtn.BackgroundColor3 = m.play and Color3.fromRGB(180, 110, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 80)
    end
    if PH.hudRec and PH.hudRec.Parent then
        PH.hudRec.Text = m.rec and "⏹" or "⏺"
        PH.hudRec.BackgroundColor3 = m.rec and Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 50) or Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200)
    end
    if PH.hudPlay and PH.hudPlay.Parent then
        PH.hudPlay.Text = m.play and "⏹" or "▶"
        PH.hudPlay.BackgroundColor3 = m.play and Color3.fromRGB(200, 120, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 150, 80)
    end
end

function PH.onInput(input, down)
    local m = PH.mac
    if not m.rec or _G.ScriptDestroyed then return end
    local ut = input.UserInputType
    local t = tick() - m.t0
    local e
    if ut == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
        if UIS3:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        local kn = input.KeyCode.Name
        if kn == "F6" or kn == "F7" or kn == "F8" then return end -- горячие клавиши самого макро не записываем
        e = {t = t, k = "key", c = kn, d = down}
    elseif ut == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or ut == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 or ut == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        local pos = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
        if PH.overMyUI(pos) then return end
        e = {t = t, k = "ptr", b = (ut == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) and 1 or 0, x = pos.X, y = pos.Y, d = down}
    end
    if e then
        table.insert(m.events, e)
        if #m.events % 5 == 0 then PH.updateMacUi() end
    end
end
UIS3.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) PH.onInput(input, true) end)
UIS3.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) PH.onInput(input, false) end)

function PH.startRecording()
    local m = PH.mac
    if m.play then return end
    m.events = {}
    m.t0 = tick()
    m.rec = true
    PH.updateMacUi()
    task.spawn(function()
        local last = nil
        while m.rec and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            if m.recPos then
                local r = PH.myRoot()
                if r then
                    local p = r.Position
                    if not last or (p - last).Magnitude > 0.6 then
                        last = p
                        local _, ry = r.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
                        table.insert(m.events, {t = tick() - m.t0, k = "pos", x = p.X, y = p.Y, z = p.Z, r = ry})
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
    end)
end

function PH.stopRecording()
    local m = PH.mac
    m.rec = false
    -- убираем «мёртвое» время до первого действия и упорядочиваем события (путь пишется отдельным потоком)
    local ev = m.events
    if #ev > 0 then
        for i, e in ipairs(ev) do e._i = i end
        table.sort(ev, function(a, b)
            if a.t ~= b.t then return a.t < b.t end
            return a._i < b._i
        end)
        local first = ev[1].t
        for _, e in ipairs(ev) do
            e.t = math.max(0, e.t - first)
            e._i = nil
        end
    end
    PH.updateMacUi()
    PH.notify("Записано событий: " .. #ev)
end

function PH.stopPlay()
    local m = PH.mac
    m.play = false
    for code in pairs(m.held) do
        pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode[code], false, game) end)
    end
    m.held = {}
    if m.ptrDown then
        local p = m.ptrDown
        pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(p.x, p.y, p.b, false, game, 0) end)
        m.ptrDown = nil
    end
    PH.updateMacUi()
end

function PH.exec(e, nextPos)
    local m = PH.mac
    if e.k == "key" then
        local ok, code = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[e.c] end)
        if ok and code then
            pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(e.d and true or false, code, false, game) end)
            m.held[e.c] = e.d and true or nil
        end
    elseif e.k == "ptr" then
        local inset = GuiService3:GetGuiInset()
        local y = e.y + inset.Y
        pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(e.x, y, e.b or 0, e.d and true or false, game, 0) end)
        m.ptrDown = e.d and {x = e.x, y = y, b = e.b or 0} or nil
    elseif e.k == "pos" then
        local r = PH.myRoot()
        if r then
            local target = CFrame.new(e.x, e.y, e.z) * CFrame.Angles(0, e.r or 0, 0)
            local dur = nextPos and math.clamp((nextPos.t - e.t) / m.opts.speed, 0.03, 2) or 0.1
            pcall(function()
                r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                TweenService:Create(r, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {CFrame = target}):Play()
            end)
        end
    end
end

function PH.enemyNear(range)
    local r = PH.myRoot()
    if not r then return false end
    return PH.findTarget(r.Position, range) ~= nil
end

function PH.playMacro(events)
    local m = PH.mac
    if m.play or m.rec then return end
    if #events == 0 then
        PH.notify("Макро пустое: сначала запиши или загрузи")
        return
    end
    m.play = true
    PH.updateMacUi()
    task.spawn(function()
        local o = m.opts
        local started = tick()
        local loop = 0

        local nextPos = {}
        local upcoming = nil
        for i = #events, 1, -1 do
            nextPos[i] = upcoming
            if events[i].k == "pos" then upcoming = events[i] end
        end

        local function limitHit()
            if o.maxTime > 0 and tick() - started > o.maxTime then return true end
            if o.hpStop > 0 then
                local h = Player.Character and Player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if h and h.MaxHealth > 0 and (h.Health / h.MaxHealth * 100) < o.hpStop then return true end
            end
            return false
        end

        while m.play and not _G.ScriptDestroyed do
            loop = loop + 1
            if o.loops > 0 and loop > o.loops then break end

            if o.enemyR > 0 then
                if PH.ka.npcs and #PH.npcCache == 0 then PH.refreshNpcs() end
                while m.play and not limitHit() and not PH.enemyNear(o.enemyR) do task.wait(0.2) end
                if not m.play or limitHit() then break end
            end

            local base = tick()
            local stopAll = false
            for i, e in ipairs(events) do
                while m.play and (tick() - base) < (e.t / o.speed) do task.wait(0.01) end
                if not m.play then break end
                while _G.ScriptPaused and m.play do task.wait(0.2) end
                if limitHit() then
                    stopAll = true
                    break
                end
                PH.exec(e, nextPos[i])
            end
            if stopAll then break end

            if o.pause > 0 then
                local w = tick()
                while m.play and tick() - w < o.pause do task.wait(0.05) end
            end
        end
        PH.stopPlay()
    end)
end

function PH.saveMacro(name)
    name = PH.safeName(name)
    local m = PH.mac
    if #m.events == 0 then
        PH.notify("Нечего сохранять: макро пустое")
        return
    end
    local ok = PH.write(PH.MAC .. "/" .. name .. ".json", {name = name, events = m.events, opts = m.opts, recPos = m.recPos})
    if ok then
        PH.notify("Макро сохранено: " .. name)
        if PH.refreshMacList then PH.refreshMacList() end
    end
end

function PH.loadMacro(name)
    name = PH.safeName(name)
    local data = PH.read(PH.MAC .. "/" .. name .. ".json")
    if type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.events) ~= "table" then
        PH.notify("Макро не найдено: " .. name)
        return
    end
    local m = PH.mac
    if m.play then PH.stopPlay() end
    m.events = data.events
    m.name = name
    if type(data.opts) == "table" then
        for k, v in pairs(data.opts) do
            if type(v) == "number" then m.opts[k] = v end
        end
    end
    PH.updateMacUi()
    PH.notify("Макро загружено: " .. name .. " (" .. #m.events .. ")")
end

local macPage = createTab("🎬 Макро")
PH.label(macPage, "🎬 Макро: записывает клавиши, тапы по экрану и (по желанию) твой путь. Папка: PromptHacker/macros", 34)
PH.macStatus = PH.label(macPage, "Статус: ожидание | событий: 0", 20, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
createInput(macPage, "Имя макро", "macro1", function(t) PH.mac.name = PH.safeName(t) end)
local recPosTg = createToggle(macPage, "🚶 Записывать позицию (путь)", function(v) PH.mac.recPos = v end)
PH.skip["🚶 Записывать позицию (путь)"] = false
PH.recBtn = PH.btn(macPage, "⏺ Начать запись", Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200), function()
    if PH.mac.rec then PH.stopRecording() else PH.startRecording() end
end)
PH.playBtn = PH.btn(macPage, "▶ Запустить макро", Color3.fromRGB(50, 140, 80), function()
    if PH.mac.play then PH.stopPlay() else PH.playMacro(PH.mac.events) end
end)
PH.btn(macPage, "💾 Сохранить макро в файл", Color3.fromRGB(40, 90, 150), function() PH.saveMacro(PH.mac.name) end)
PH.btn(macPage, "📂 Загрузить макро по имени", Color3.fromRGB(60, 110, 60), function() PH.loadMacro(PH.mac.name) end)

PH.label(macPage, "🧠 SMART-ограничения (0 = выключено):", 20, Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255))
createInput(macPage, "Повторов (0 = бесконечно)", "1", function(t)
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n then PH.mac.opts.loops = math.max(0, math.floor(n)) end
end)
createInput(macPage, "Скорость (x, дефолт 1)", "1", function(t)
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n then PH.mac.opts.speed = math.clamp(n, 0.1, 10) end
end)
createInput(macPage, "Пауза между повторами (сек)", "0.2", function(t)
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n then PH.mac.opts.pause = math.clamp(n, 0, 600) end
end)
createInput(macPage, "Макс. время работы (сек)", "0", function(t)
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n then PH.mac.opts.maxTime = math.max(0, n) end
end)
createInput(macPage, "Стоп, если HP ниже (%)", "0", function(t)
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n then PH.mac.opts.hpStop = math.clamp(n, 0, 100) end
end)
createInput(macPage, "Работать, только пока враг в радиусе", "0", function(t)
    local n = tonumber(t)
    if n then PH.mac.opts.enemyR = math.clamp(n, 0, 500) end
end)

PH.label(macPage, "Сохранённые макро (нажми — загрузить, ✕ — удалить):", 20, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200))
PH.macList = PH.makeList(macPage, 110)
function PH.refreshMacList()
    PH.clearList(PH.macList)
    for _, n in ipairs(PH.listNames(PH.MAC)) do
        PH.addRow(PH.macList, n, function() PH.loadMacro(n) end, function()
            PH.delFile(PH.MAC .. "/" .. n .. ".json")
            PH.refreshMacList()
        end)
    end
end
PH.btn(macPage, "🔄 Обновить список макро", Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45), function() PH.refreshMacList() end)
PH.refreshMacList()

-- Мини-панель макро на экране (перетаскиваемая): ⏺ запись / ▶ запуск. Тапы по ней не записываются.
-- Горячие клавиши (ПК): F6 — запись, F7 — запуск, F8 — стоп.
PH.macHud = nil
function PH.destroyMacHud()
    if PH.macHud then pcall(function() PH.macHud:Destroy() end) end
    PH.macHud, PH.hudRec, PH.hudPlay = nil, nil, nil
end

function PH.makeMacHud()
    PH.destroyMacHud()
    if ScreenGui then ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 999 end
    local f = Instance.new("Frame")
    f.Name = "PromptMacroHud"
    f.Size = UDim2.new(0, 112, 0, 40)
    f.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -56, 0.86, 0)
    f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 25, 30)
    f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    f.Active = true
    f.Draggable = true
    f.ZIndex = 90
    f.Parent = ScreenGui
    PH.corner(f, 10)
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
    st.Thickness = 1.5
    st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(120, 220, 255)

    local function mk(x, text, color, cb)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 1, -10)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 5)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = color
        b.Text = text
        b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        b.TextSize = 18
        b.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        b.ZIndex = 91
        b.Parent = f
        PH.corner(b, 6)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not _G.ScriptDestroyed then cb() end
        end)
        return b
    end
    PH.hudRec = mk(6, "⏺", Color3.fromRGB(40, 120, 200), function()
        if PH.mac.rec then PH.stopRecording() else PH.startRecording() end
    end)
    PH.hudPlay = mk(58, "▶", Color3.fromRGB(50, 150, 80), function()
        if PH.mac.play then PH.stopPlay() else PH.playMacro(PH.mac.events) end
    end)
    PH.macHud = f
    PH.updateMacUi()
end

createToggle(macPage, "🎛 Мини-кнопки макро на экране (⏺ / ▶)", function(v)
    if v then PH.makeMacHud() else PH.destroyMacHud() end
end)

UIS3.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
    if _G.ScriptDestroyed or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
    if UIS3:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    local k = input.KeyCode
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.F6 then
        if PH.mac.rec then PH.stopRecording() else PH.startRecording() end
    elseif k == Enum.KeyCode.F7 then
        if PH.mac.play then PH.stopPlay() else PH.playMacro(PH.mac.events) end
    elseif k == Enum.KeyCode.F8 then
        if PH.mac.rec then PH.stopRecording() end
        if PH.mac.play then PH.stopPlay() end
    end
end)

-- при смерти персонажа воспроизведение останавливаем, чтобы макро не жало кнопки «вслепую»
Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
    if PH.mac.play then PH.stopPlay() end
end)

PH.updateMacUi()

-- =========================================================
-- Закрытие скрипта: убираем копии, шейдеры, останавливаем аура/макро. Автозагрузка конфига при запуске.
-- =========================================================
task.spawn(function()
    while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do task.wait(0.5) end
    if PH.autosave and not PH.loading then pcall(PH.saveConfig, PH.current) end
    PH.ka.on = false
    PH.shaderOn = false
    PH.mac.rec = false
    pcall(PH.stopPlay)
    pcall(PH.removeShader)
    pcall(function()
        local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("PromptWorldCopies")
        if f then f:Destroy() end
    end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2.5)
    local name = PH.getAutoload()
    if name then
        PH.current = name
        PH.loadConfig(name)
    end
end)

end, function(err)
    warn("[PromptHacker v3.0] Часть 11: " .. tostring(err))
end)
-- =========================================================
-- ----- КОНЕЦ ЧАСТИ 11 -----
-- =========================================================


-- Отключение диагностики при полном закрытии GUI
task.spawn(function()
    while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do task.wait(0.5) end
    if phLogConnection then pcall(function() phLogConnection:Disconnect() end); phLogConnection = nil end
end)

-- =========================================================
-- 🪄 MAGIC ORB: лазерная точка прицела, магические эффекты и настройка скорости
-- ИСПРАВЛЕННАЯ ВЕРСИЯ. Заменяет исходные строки 7922–8600 целиком (оба блока: Orb + Клон-сапёр).
-- =========================================================
do
    -- Безопасные запасные значения, если в основном скрипте эти имена не объявлены.
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = RunService or game:GetService("RunService")
    local UserInputService = UserInputService or game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local Player = Player or Players.LocalPlayer

    -- ИСПРАВЛЕНО: ORB_CYAN / ORB_PURPLE нигде не были объявлены (были nil).
    -- Из-за stroke.Color = nil createOrbPanel падал с ошибкой, и кнопки не появлялись,
    -- а Orb не создавался (Color3 = nil).
    local ORB_CYAN = Color3.fromRGB(0, 230, 255)
    local ORB_PURPLE = Color3.fromRGB(170, 70, 255)

    local orbEnabled = false
    local orbSpeed = 120 -- studs/sec
    local orbPart = nil
    local orbHitNormal = nil
    local orbFlightConnection = nil
    local trajectoryConnection = nil
    local orbMoteConnection = nil
    local trajectoryPart = nil
    local orbitMotes = {}
    local orbDragConnections = {}
    local orbPanel = nil

    -- Список игнорируемых объектов для лучей (персонаж, клон, маркер). Без nil-дыр в таблице.
    local function buildFilter()
        local list = {}
        if Player.Character then table.insert(list, Player.Character) end
        local clone = workspace:FindFirstChild("PH_ClickClone")
        if clone then table.insert(list, clone) end
        if trajectoryPart then table.insert(list, trajectoryPart) end
        if orbPart then table.insert(list, orbPart) end
        return list
    end

    local function newRayParams()
        local params = RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.RespectCanCollide = true
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = buildFilter()
        return params
    end

    local function destroyTrajectory()
        if trajectoryConnection then trajectoryConnection:Disconnect(); trajectoryConnection = nil end
        if trajectoryPart then pcall(function() trajectoryPart:Destroy() end); trajectoryPart = nil end
    end

    local function destroyOrb()
        if orbFlightConnection then orbFlightConnection:Disconnect(); orbFlightConnection = nil end
        if orbMoteConnection then orbMoteConnection:Disconnect(); orbMoteConnection = nil end
        for _, mote in ipairs(orbitMotes) do
            if mote and mote.Parent then pcall(function() mote:Destroy() end) end
        end
        table.clear(orbitMotes)
        if orbPart then pcall(function() orbPart:Destroy() end); orbPart = nil end
        orbHitNormal = nil
    end

    local function destroyOrbPanel()
        for _, connection in ipairs(orbDragConnections) do
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
        end
        table.clear(orbDragConnections)
        if orbPanel then pcall(function() orbPanel:Destroy() end); orbPanel = nil end
    end

    local function startTrajectory()
        destroyTrajectory()
        -- Маленькая красная точка-маркер на поверхности.
        local marker = Instance.new("Part")
        marker.Name = "PH_OrbAimLaserDot"
        marker.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        marker.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        marker.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 25, 25)
        marker.Transparency = 1
        marker.Size = Vector3.new(0.24, 0.24, 0.24)
        marker.Anchored = true
        marker.CanCollide = false
        marker.CanTouch = false
        marker.CanQuery = false
        marker.CastShadow = false
        marker.Parent = workspace
        trajectoryPart = marker

        trajectoryConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local dot = trajectoryPart
            if not dot or not dot.Parent then return end
            if not orbEnabled or _G.ScriptDestroyed then
                dot.Transparency = 1
                return
            end
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then
                dot.Transparency = 1
                return
            end

            local hit = workspace:Raycast(cam.CFrame.Position, cam.CFrame.LookVector * 500, newRayParams())
            if hit then
                dot.Position = hit.Position + hit.Normal * 0.035
                dot.Transparency = 0.03
            else
                dot.Transparency = 1
            end
        end)
    end

    local function throwOrb()
        destroyOrb()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end

        local look = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local char = Player.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        -- Старт у персонажа, полёт — в точку, куда смотрит прицел (красная точка).
        local startPos
        if root then
            startPos = root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0) + look * 1.5
        else
            startPos = cam.CFrame.Position + look * 3
        end
        local aimHit = workspace:Raycast(cam.CFrame.Position, look * 500, newRayParams())
        local targetPos = aimHit and aimHit.Position or (cam.CFrame.Position + look * 500)
        local direction = targetPos - startPos
        if direction.Magnitude < 0.5 or direction:Dot(look) <= 0 then
            direction = look
        else
            direction = direction.Unit
        end

        local orb = Instance.new("Part")
        orb.Name = "PH_MagicOrb"
        orb.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
        orb.Size = Vector3.new(1.8, 1.8, 1.8)
        orb.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        orb.Color = ORB_PURPLE
        orb.Anchored = true
        orb.CanCollide = false
        orb.CanTouch = false
        orb.CanQuery = false
        orb.CFrame = CFrame.new(startPos)
        orb.Parent = workspace
        orbPart = orb

        local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
        light.Color = ORB_CYAN
        light.Brightness = 3
        light.Range = 18
        light.Parent = orb

        local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
        highlight.Name = "PH_OrbGlow"
        highlight.Adornee = orb
        highlight.FillColor = ORB_PURPLE
        highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(235, 220, 255)
        highlight.FillTransparency = 0.25
        highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
        highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        highlight.Parent = orb

        local a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a0.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, -0.75)
        a0.Parent = orb
        local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
        a1.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0.75)
        a1.Parent = orb
        local trail = Instance.new("Trail")
        trail.Attachment0 = a0
        trail.Attachment1 = a1
        trail.Lifetime = 0.42
        trail.MinLength = 0.05
        trail.LightEmission = 1
        trail.Color = ColorSequence.new(ORB_CYAN, ORB_PURPLE)
        trail.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.05, 1)
        trail.WidthScale = NumberSequence.new(1.1, 0)
        trail.Parent = orb

        local particles = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        particles.Name = "ArcaneSparks"
        particles.Texture = "rbxasset://textures/particles/sparkles_main.dds"
        particles.Color = ColorSequence.new(ORB_CYAN, ORB_PURPLE)
        particles.LightEmission = 1
        particles.Rate = 75
        particles.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.25, 0.7)
        particles.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 4)
        particles.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(180, 180)
        particles.Size = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.28),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
        })
        particles.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.05, 1)
        particles.Parent = orb

        for i = 1, 6 do
            local mote = Instance.new("Part")
            mote.Name = "PH_OrbRune"
            mote.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
            mote.Size = Vector3.new(0.22, 0.22, 0.22)
            mote.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            mote.Color = (i % 2 == 0) and ORB_CYAN or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 255)
            mote.Anchored = true
            mote.CanCollide = false
            mote.CanTouch = false
            mote.CanQuery = false
            mote.Parent = workspace
            table.insert(orbitMotes, mote)
        end

        local stopped = false
        local traveled = 0
        local born = os.clock()

        orbMoteConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not orb.Parent then return end
            local t = os.clock() - born
            local count = #orbitMotes
            for i, mote in ipairs(orbitMotes) do
                if mote and mote.Parent then
                    local angle = t * 4 + (i / count) * math.pi * 2
                    mote.Position = orb.Position + Vector3.new(
                        math.cos(angle) * 1.55, math.sin(angle * 1.7) * 0.65,
                        math.sin(angle) * 1.55
                    )
                end
            end
        end)

        orbFlightConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not orb.Parent then destroyOrb(); return end
            if stopped then return end
            local step = direction * orbSpeed * math.min(dt, 0.1)
            local hit = workspace:Raycast(orb.Position, step, newRayParams())
            if hit then
                orb.Position = hit.Position + hit.Normal * 0.25
                orbHitNormal = hit.Normal
                stopped = true
                particles:Emit(55)
                light.Brightness = 6
                task.delay(0.18, function()
                    if light and light.Parent then light.Brightness = 3 end
                end)
            else
                orb.Position = orb.Position + step
                traveled += step.Magnitude
                if traveled > 2500 then destroyOrb() end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function teleportToOrb()
        local char = Player.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not (orbPart and orbPart.Parent and char and root) then return end

        local target = orbPart.Position
        local normal = orbHitNormal
        if normal then
            -- Отступаем от поверхности, чтобы не застрять в стене/полу.
            local lift = (normal.Y < -0.5) and Vector3.zero or Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
            target = target + normal * 2.5 + lift
        end
        -- Сохраняем текущий поворот персонажа и гасим скорость.
        char:PivotTo(CFrame.new(target) * char:GetPivot().Rotation)
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        destroyOrb()
    end

    local function createOrbPanel()
        destroyOrbPanel()
        -- Две квадратные кнопки в одном прозрачном перетаскиваемом контейнере.
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.Name = "PH_MagicOrbButtons"
        frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 126, 0, 60)
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -63, 0.78, 0)
        frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Active = true
        frame.ZIndex = 90
        frame.Parent = ScreenGui
        orbPanel = frame -- сохраняем сразу, чтобы панель не «терялась» при любой ошибке ниже

        local dragInput, dragStart, startPosition = nil, nil, nil
        local dragMoved = false
        local suppressClick = false

        local function beginDrag(input)
            if dragInput then return end
            local t = input.UserInputType
            if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            dragInput = input
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPosition = frame.Position
            dragMoved = false
        end
        frame.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)

        table.insert(orbDragConnections, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if not dragInput then return end
            -- ИСПРАВЛЕНО: на телефоне двигаем панель только тем пальцем, который начал перетаскивание
            -- (раньше любой второй палец, например камера, сдвигал кнопки).
            local mouseDrag = dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            local touchDrag = dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == dragInput
            if not (mouseDrag or touchDrag) then return end

            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            if not dragMoved and delta.Magnitude > 8 then
                dragMoved = true
                suppressClick = true -- сразу, чтобы Activated при отпускании не сработал как нажатие
            end
            if dragMoved then
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPosition.X.Scale, startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPosition.Y.Scale, startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end))

        table.insert(orbDragConnections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if not dragInput then return end
            local ended = (input == dragInput)
                or (dragInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                    and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
            if ended then
                dragInput = nil
                if dragMoved then
                    task.delay(0.25, function() suppressClick = false end)
                else
                    suppressClick = false
                end
            end
        end))

        local function makeButton(text, x, color, strokeColor, callback)
            local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
            button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
            button.Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0)
            button.BackgroundColor3 = color
            button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
            button.BorderSizePixel = 0
            button.Text = text
            button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            button.TextSize = 13
            button.TextWrapped = true
            button.ZIndex = 92
            button.Parent = frame
            button.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
            Instance.new("UICorner", button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
            local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            stroke.Thickness = 1.5
            stroke.Color = strokeColor
            stroke.Parent = button
            button.Activated:Connect(function()
                if suppressClick then return end
                if orbEnabled and not _G.ScriptDestroyed then
                    local ok, err = pcall(callback)
                    if not ok then warn("[Prompt_Hacker] Orb: " .. tostring(err)) end
                end
            end)
        end

        makeButton("🌀\nКИНУТЬ", 0, Color3.fromRGB(91, 38, 145), ORB_CYAN, throwOrb)
        makeButton("⚡\nТЕЛЕПОРТ", 66, Color3.fromRGB(35, 91, 139), ORB_PURPLE, teleportToOrb)
    end

    createToggle(visualsPage, "🪄 Магический Orb (лазерная точка + заклинание)", function(value)
        orbEnabled = value
        if value then
            createOrbPanel()
            startTrajectory() -- красная точка появляется сразу при включении тумблера
        else
            destroyOrbPanel()
            destroyTrajectory()
            destroyOrb()
        end
    end)

    -- createInput может отсутствовать в основном скрипте — не даём блоку упасть.
    if createInput then
        createInput(visualsPage, "Скорость Orb (studs/sec)", "120", function(value)
            local n = tonumber(value)
            if n then orbSpeed = math.clamp(n, 10, 2000) end
        end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        while not _G.ScriptDestroyed do task.wait(0.5) end
        orbEnabled = false
        destroyOrbPanel()
        destroyTrajectory()
        destroyOrb()
    end)
end

-- =========================================================
-- 🧭 КЛОН-САПЁР: движение по клику, прыжки и предупреждение об опасности
-- Клиентская визуальная копия. Поиск опасностей эвристический:
-- используются имена частей и атрибуты Danger/Damage/IsHazard/Kill.
-- Не все игры помечают уроняемые зоны доступными клиенту признаками.
-- =========================================================
do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = RunService or game:GetService("RunService")
    local PathfindingService = game:GetService("PathfindingService")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local Player = Player or Players.LocalPlayer

    local cloneEnabled = false
    local cloneModel, cloneHumanoid, cloneRoot = nil, nil, nil
    local cloneInputConnections = {}
    local cloneTouchConnections = {}
    local cloneMoveToken = 0
    local cloneWarning = nil
    local cloneBusy = false
    local cloneAnimationTracks = {}
    local cloneAnimationConnections = {}
    local hazardCache, hazardCacheTime = nil, 0

    local function stopCloneAnimations()
        for _, connection in ipairs(cloneAnimationConnections) do
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
        end
        table.clear(cloneAnimationConnections)
        for _, track in pairs(cloneAnimationTracks) do
            pcall(function() track:Stop(0.12); track:Destroy() end)
        end
        table.clear(cloneAnimationTracks)
    end

    local function setupCloneAnimations()
        stopCloneAnimations()
        if not cloneHumanoid then return end
        local animator = cloneHumanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then
            animator = Instance.new("Animator")
            animator.Parent = cloneHumanoid
        end

        local isR15 = cloneHumanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
        local ids = isR15 and {
            Idle = "507766666", Walk = "507777826", Run = "507767714", Jump = "507765000"
        } or {
            Idle = "180435571", Walk = "180426354", Run = "180426354", Jump = "125750702"
        }

        for name, assetId in pairs(ids) do
            local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
            animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. assetId
            local ok, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(animation) end)
            animation:Destroy()
            if ok and track then
                track.Priority = (name == "Idle") and Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
                    or ((name == "Jump") and Enum.AnimationPriority.Action or Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement)
                track.Looped = name ~= "Jump"
                cloneAnimationTracks[name] = track
            end
        end

        local function playMovement(speed)
            if not cloneHumanoid or cloneHumanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
            local moving = speed > 0.5
            local desired = moving and "Walk" or "Idle"
            local other = moving and "Idle" or "Walk"
            if cloneAnimationTracks[other] and cloneAnimationTracks[other].IsPlaying then
                cloneAnimationTracks[other]:Stop(0.15)
            end
            local track = cloneAnimationTracks[desired]
            if track and not track.IsPlaying then track:Play(0.15) end
        end

        table.insert(cloneAnimationConnections, cloneHumanoid.Running:Connect(playMovement))
        table.insert(cloneAnimationConnections, cloneHumanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, state)
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                local jump = cloneAnimationTracks.Jump
                if jump then
                    if cloneAnimationTracks.Idle and cloneAnimationTracks.Idle.IsPlaying then cloneAnimationTracks.Idle:Stop(0.08) end
                    if cloneAnimationTracks.Walk and cloneAnimationTracks.Walk.IsPlaying then cloneAnimationTracks.Walk:Stop(0.08) end
                    if not jump.IsPlaying then jump:Play(0.08) end
                end
            elseif state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running then
                local jump = cloneAnimationTracks.Jump
                if jump and jump.IsPlaying then jump:Stop(0.1) end
                if cloneHumanoid then
                    playMovement(cloneHumanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude * cloneHumanoid.WalkSpeed)
                end
            end
        end))
        if cloneAnimationTracks.Idle then cloneAnimationTracks.Idle:Play(0.2) end
    end

    local hazardWords = {"kill", "damage", "hazard", "lava", "acid", "poison", "deadly", "death", "убий", "урон", "лава", "кисл", "яд"}

    local function isHazard(inst)
        if not inst or not inst:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        for _, key in ipairs({"Danger", "Damage", "IsHazard", "Kill"}) do
            if inst:GetAttribute(key) == true then return true end
        end
        local n = string.lower(inst.Name)
        for _, word in ipairs(hazardWords) do
            if string.find(n, word, 1, true) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function showCloneWarning(message, color)
        if not cloneModel then return end
        local head = cloneModel:FindFirstChild("Head") or cloneRoot
        if not head then return end
        if not cloneWarning or not cloneWarning.Parent then
            cloneWarning = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            cloneWarning.Name = "PH_CloneWarning"
            cloneWarning.Size = UDim2.new(0, 230, 0, 48)
            cloneWarning.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0)
            cloneWarning.AlwaysOnTop = true
            cloneWarning.MaxDistance = math.huge -- ИСПРАВЛЕНО: 0 мог прятать подсказку
            cloneWarning.Parent = head
            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Name = "Message"
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            label.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 12, 12)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            label.BorderSizePixel = 0
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            label.TextSize = 15
            label.TextWrapped = true
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
            label.Parent = cloneWarning
            Instance.new("UICorner", label).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        end
        local label = cloneWarning:FindFirstChild("Message")
        if label then
            label.Text = message
            label.TextColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
        end
        cloneWarning.Enabled = true
    end

    local function destroyClone()
        cloneMoveToken += 1
        cloneBusy = false
        hazardCache = nil
        for _, connection in ipairs(cloneInputConnections) do
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
        end
        table.clear(cloneInputConnections)
        for _, connection in ipairs(cloneTouchConnections) do
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
        end
        table.clear(cloneTouchConnections)
        stopCloneAnimations()
        if cloneModel then pcall(function() cloneModel:Destroy() end) end
        cloneModel, cloneHumanoid, cloneRoot, cloneWarning = nil, nil, nil, nil
    end

    local function createClone()
        destroyClone()
        local character = Player.Character
        if not character then return false end
        local oldArchivable = character.Archivable
        character.Archivable = true
        local ok, copy = pcall(function() return character:Clone() end)
        character.Archivable = oldArchivable
        if not ok or not copy then
            warn("[Prompt_Hacker] Не удалось создать клон персонажа")
            return false
        end
        copy.Name = "PH_ClickClone"
        for _, obj in ipairs(copy:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Script") or obj:IsA("LocalScript") then
                obj:Destroy()
            elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                obj.Anchored = false
            end
        end
        local humanoid = copy:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = copy:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not humanoid or not root then copy:Destroy(); return false end
        cloneHumanoid, cloneRoot = humanoid, root

        humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
        humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
        humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
        humanoid.JumpPower = 52
        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
        humanoid.Sit = false
        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
        if humanoid.Health <= 0 then humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth end
        for _, state in ipairs({
            Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running,
            Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics,
            Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping,
            Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall,
            Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed,
            Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
        }) do
            pcall(function() humanoid:SetStateEnabled(state, true) end)
        end

        copy.PrimaryPart = root
        copy:PivotTo(character:GetPivot() * CFrame.new(4, 0.5, 0))
        copy.Parent = workspace
        cloneModel = copy
        setupCloneAnimations()

        for _, part in ipairs(copy:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                table.insert(cloneTouchConnections, part.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
                    if cloneEnabled and isHazard(hit) then
                        showCloneWarning("⚠️ ОПАСНОСТЬ: " .. hit.Name, Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 65))
                    end
                end))
            end
        end
        table.insert(cloneTouchConnections, humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            showCloneWarning("💀 КЛОН ПОГИБ — ПЕРЕКЛЮЧИ ТУМБЛЕР", Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 65))
        end))

        showCloneWarning("🧭 КЛОН ГОТОВ — НАЖМИ НА ТОЧКУ", Color3.fromRGB(130, 220, 255))
        return true
    end

    local function collectHazards()
        local now = os.clock()
        if hazardCache and now - hazardCacheTime < 10 then return hazardCache end
        local found = {}
        local character = Player.Character
        for _, inst in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if isHazard(inst)
                and not (cloneModel and inst:IsDescendantOf(cloneModel))
                and not (character and inst:IsDescendantOf(character)) then
                table.insert(found, inst)
            end
        end
        hazardCache, hazardCacheTime = found, now
        return found
    end

    -- ИСПРАВЛЕНО: раньше bestDistance = radius отсекал большие опасные детали
    -- (расстояние до центра больше radius, хотя край рядом).
    local function nearestHazard(position, hazards, radius)
        local best, bestDistance = nil, math.huge
        for _, part in ipairs(hazards) do
            if part and part.Parent then
                local distance = (part.Position - position).Magnitude
                local reach = radius + math.max(part.Size.X, part.Size.Y, part.Size.Z) * 0.5
                if distance < reach and distance < bestDistance then
                    best, bestDistance = part, distance
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    -- Расстояние от HumanoidRootPart до ступней. Точки пути лежат НА ЗЕМЛЕ,
    -- а корень персонажа примерно на 3 studs выше.
    local function feetOffset()
        if not cloneHumanoid or not cloneRoot then return 3 end
        local hip = cloneHumanoid.HipHeight
        if cloneHumanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then hip = math.max(hip, 2) end
        return hip + cloneRoot.Size.Y * 0.5
    end

    local function flatDistance(a, b)
        return Vector3.new(a.X - b.X, 0, a.Z - b.Z).Magnitude
    end

    local function cloneAlive(token)
        return token == cloneMoveToken
            and cloneModel ~= nil and cloneModel.Parent ~= nil
            and cloneRoot ~= nil and cloneRoot.Parent ~= nil
            and cloneHumanoid ~= nil and cloneHumanoid.Health > 0
    end

    local function isGrounded()
        return cloneHumanoid and cloneHumanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
    end

    -- Возвращает "done", "stuck" или "cancelled".
    local function followWaypoints(waypoints, token, hazards)
        for index, waypoint in ipairs(waypoints) do
            if not cloneAlive(token) then return "cancelled" end

            -- ИСПРАВЛЕНО: сравнение было 3D (корень выше земли на ~3 studs), поэтому
            -- «точка достигнута» (< 2.8) никогда не срабатывала → клон каждый раз ждал
            -- таймаут и писал «ПРЕПЯТСТВИЕ». Теперь расстояние считается по горизонтали.
            if index == 1 and flatDistance(cloneRoot.Position, waypoint.Position) < 2 then
                continue
            end

            local hazard = nearestHazard(waypoint.Position, hazards, 7)
            if hazard then
                showCloneWarning("⚠️ ОПАСНОСТЬ: " .. hazard.Name, Color3.fromRGB(255, 65, 65))
            else
                showCloneWarning("🧭 ИДУ К ТОЧКЕ", Color3.fromRGB(130, 220, 255))
            end

            local function needsJump()
                local feetY = cloneRoot.Position.Y - feetOffset()
                return waypoint.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump or (waypoint.Position.Y - feetY) > 1.5
            end

            if needsJump() and isGrounded() then cloneHumanoid.Jump = true end
            cloneHumanoid:MoveTo(waypoint.Position)

            local startFlat = flatDistance(cloneRoot.Position, waypoint.Position)
            local deadline = os.clock() + math.max(4, startFlat / math.max(cloneHumanoid.WalkSpeed, 1) * 2 + 3)
            local lastRefresh = os.clock()
            local lastCheckTime = os.clock()
            local lastCheckPos = cloneRoot.Position
            local stuckTime = 0
            local reached = false

            while os.clock() < deadline do
                if not cloneAlive(token) then return "cancelled" end
                local rootPos = cloneRoot.Position
                local feetY = rootPos.Y - feetOffset()

                if flatDistance(rootPos, waypoint.Position) < 2.5 and math.abs(waypoint.Position.Y - feetY) < 4 then
                    reached = true
                    break
                end

                -- Прыжок на подъёме / по указанию пути.
                if needsJump() and isGrounded() then
                    cloneHumanoid.Jump = true
                end

                local now = os.clock()
                if now - lastRefresh > 1.25 then
                    cloneHumanoid:MoveTo(waypoint.Position)
                    lastRefresh = now
                end

                -- Детектор застревания: почти не двигаемся → прыгаем, затем перестраиваем путь.
                if now - lastCheckTime >= 0.6 then
                    if (rootPos - lastCheckPos).Magnitude < 0.6 then
                        stuckTime += now - lastCheckTime
                        if isGrounded() then cloneHumanoid.Jump = true end
                        cloneHumanoid:MoveTo(waypoint.Position)
                    else
                        stuckTime = 0
                    end
                    lastCheckPos, lastCheckTime = rootPos, now
                    if stuckTime >= 1.8 then return "stuck" end
                end

                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end

            if not reached then return "stuck" end
        end
        return "done"
    end

    local function moveCloneTo(destination)
        if not cloneEnabled or not cloneModel or not cloneRoot or not cloneHumanoid then return end
        cloneMoveToken += 1
        local myToken = cloneMoveToken
        cloneBusy = true

        task.spawn(function()
            local hazards = collectHazards()
            local finished = false

            for attempt = 1, 4 do
                if not cloneAlive(myToken) then return end

                local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({
                    AgentRadius = 2,
                    AgentHeight = 5,
                    AgentCanJump = true,
                    AgentCanClimb = true,
                    WaypointSpacing = 3,
                    Costs = {}
                })
                local ok = pcall(function() path:ComputeAsync(cloneRoot.Position, destination) end)
                if not cloneAlive(myToken) then return end
                if not ok or path.Status ~= Enum.PathStatus.Success then
                    showCloneWarning("⚠️ НЕ НАШЁЛ БЕЗОПАСНЫЙ ПУТЬ", Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 80))
                    cloneBusy = false
                    return
                end

                local result = followWaypoints(path:GetWaypoints(), myToken, hazards)
                if result == "cancelled" then return end
                if result == "done" then finished = true; break end

                -- Застряли: прыжок и перестроение пути (обход препятствия).
                showCloneWarning("🔄 ПРЕПЯТСТВИЕ — ПЕРЕСТРАИВАЮ ПУТЬ", Color3.fromRGB(255, 175, 70))
                if isGrounded() then cloneHumanoid.Jump = true end
                task.wait(0.35)
            end

            if cloneMoveToken ~= myToken then return end
            cloneBusy = false
            if not (cloneRoot and cloneRoot.Parent) then return end
            if finished and flatDistance(cloneRoot.Position, destination) < 7 then
                showCloneWarning("✓ ТОЧКА ДОСТИГНУТА", Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 160))
            else
                showCloneWarning("⚠️ ПРЕПЯТСТВИЕ — НЕ МОГУ ПРОЙТИ", Color3.fromRGB(255, 175, 70))
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Касание попало в наш интерфейс или в кнопку игры (например, прыжок) — клона не посылаем.
    local function isOverBlockingGui(pos)
        local ok, blocked = pcall(function()
            -- ScreenGui может находиться в CoreGui (через executor), а не в PlayerGui.
            -- В таком случае GetGuiObjectsAtPosition у PlayerGui не видит наши кнопки.
            -- Проверяем прямоугольники элементов собственного ScreenGui отдельно.
